# Audit Fungsional RDF Node (GUI + Backend)

Cakupan: `rdf-node/` di branch `main` (commit `ad4d0bb`), Edge (source, agent, MQTT, API, command/helper, panel 480×320) dan Ground (receiver, preview). **Keamanan sengaja tidak diaudit** (auth, CSRF, ACL, TLS policy, secrets). Temuan keamanan hanya disebut bila membuat fungsi gagal.

## Cara audit

- 12 auditor paralel. Masing-masing memegang satu area: source, agent, MQTT, Ground, command/journal/helper, API/config/CLI, kode panel, uji GUI langsung, dan docs/deploy. Tiga lainnya menangani celah yang ditemukan oleh completeness critic: interop Mosquitto asli, kontrak waktu Edge–Ground, serta kesesuaian dengan engine KrakenSDR upstream.
- Setiap temuan dicek ulang oleh verifier terpisah yang sengaja mencoba membantahnya. Sebagian besar temuan sudah direproduksi dengan script, broker fixture, Mosquitto 2.0.18, Playwright (Chromium 480×320), atau simulator engine yang dibangun dari kode `krakenrf/krakensdr_doa`.
- Hasil: 155 temuan mentah. 146 terkonfirmasi, 8 dibantah, dan 1 tidak pasti. Setelah duplikat lintas area digabung, tersisa sekitar **70 masalah unik**.
- Baseline: `python3 run.py selftest` menghasilkan 254/255 lulus. Satu-satunya yang gagal adalah tes yang mengharapkan user non-root, jadi itu bukan bug.
- Batasan: semua uji dilakukan di container dengan fixture dan simulator, bukan di Raspberry Pi, layar sentuh, T900, atau KrakenSDR asli.

## Yang sudah benar

- Matematika arah benar: `theta_mirror = (360 − raw) % 360`, 360 dibungkus ke 0, jarum memutar lewat jalur terpendek, dan puncak spektrum sejajar dengan jarum.
- Uji dengan Mosquitto asli (TLS, TCP, ws://, wss://) berjalan end-to-end di Python 3.12, sesuai cara `provision-ground.sh` menyiapkan broker. Perkecualiannya ada di temuan K1.
- Parsing `status.json` (DAQ) cocok dengan format yang ditulis upstream.
- Pemisahan klien Control/Bulk, pembatasan budget, dan journal bekerja sesuai desain pada jalur normal.

Ini sudah saya cek sendiri dan hasilnya `CA cert does not include key usage extension`. | Di Raspberry Pi OS Trixie / Debian 13 dan Ubuntu 25.04+, **Edge dan Ground sama sekali tidak bisa konek MQTT TLS**. | T1 | **Upgrade dari 1.0.0 membuat `rdf-edge` crash-loop.** Config lama berisi key yang sudah dihapus (`telemetry.require_ground_receipt_for_bulk`, `freshness.receipt_warn_ms`, `freshness.receipt_lost_ms`), dan loader yang strict menolaknya dengan `UNKNOWN_CONFIG_KEY`. | Mengikuti prosedur upgrade di docs membuat panel, telemetry, dan command mati sampai config diedit manual. | T2 | **Satu glitch baca file sumber langsung menghentikan Angular selama 20 detik.** Contohnya file ditulis non-atomik oleh engine, `EMPTY_CSV`, `JSONDecodeError`, atau rename atomik di tengah pembacaan. Satu glitch saja membuat deteksi invalid, lalu Bulk di-pause selama `resume_stable_seconds`. | Dalam repro, sekitar 40% frame Angular hilang. Keduanya diperlakukan sebagai nilai yang sama. | Saat commissioning, panel Edge dan Ground menampilkan angka yang berbeda untuk arah yang sama, dan Ground berganti sumber diam-diam. Ini berisiko membuat keputusan `angle_verified` jadi salah. | `source.py:89`, `ground.py:254`, `app.js:290` | NEC-2 |

## Prioritas 3: Rendah (contoh; daftar lengkap ada di lampiran)

- Toast operasi lama muncul setiap kali halaman dimuat (GUI-1, EDGE-UI-1).
- Badge "AKTIF" di dialog Profil tidak ikut berubah (GUI-2, EDGE-UI-15).
- Tab Data tetap hijau "TERKIRIM" walaupun broker putus (GUI-3, EDGE-UI-5).
- Swipe tidak berfungsi di atas daftar topic Data (GUI-9, EDGE-UI-10).
- Pada keyboard layar mode simbol, tombol Spasi/Backspace keluar dari layar (GUI-5, EDGE-UI-13).
- Kode backend mentah berbahasa Inggris tampil ke operator (GUI-14, EDGE-UI-17).
- Kiosk Ground membuka port 8790 alih-alih 8791 (KIO-1).
- `doctor` menampilkan nilai `config.yaml`, bukan nilai runtime (API-7).
- Overlay "API lokal tidak merespons" berkedip saat load (GUI-15).
- Docs: README menyebut Angular "terkompresi", masih ada referensi chunk/receipt, dan anonymous MQTT dijanjikan tetapi ditolak validasi (DOC-1, DOC-2, EDGE-UI-18, API-10).

## Saran urutan perbaikan

1. **K1 + T1 + DEP-2/3**: masalah ini memblokir instalasi baru di OS terbaru dan upgrade dari 1.0.0.
2. **T2 + AGT-2 + GRD-10**: penyebab utama grafik Angular sering pause/STALE di lapangan.
3. **T4 + T5 + NEC-5**: kesesuaian dengan engine KrakenSDR asli (AGC, konvensi doa.xml, VFO float).
4. **T3 + GRD-7/CMD-5 + MOSQ-3**: command atau ACK yang hilang harus punya timeout atau rekonsiliasi.
5. **CMD-3, CMD-6, CMD-7, CMD-11, HLP-1**: jalur pemulihan lokal (shutdown/reboot/PPP) harus selalu bisa dipakai.
6. Perbaikan UI panel/Ground (penyebab error MQTT, overlay STALE, toast, frekuensi float).
7. Dokumentasi, rollback, dan renew sertifikat.

## Lampiran: semua temuan per area (teks asli auditor, bahasa Inggris)
Kolom *Verifikasi* adalah putusan verifier adversarial. Severity yang tercantum adalah hasil koreksi verifier. Temuan yang dibantah (refuted) tetap dicantumkan di bagian akhir tiap area.

### Source & monitor

_The Edge source layer (source.py, util.py, monitor.py) reads `DOA_value.html`, `status.json`, `settings.json` and `doa.xml` with size and stability checks and strict number and range checks. It then applies freshness, DAQ, frame-progress, clock, config-attribution and authority/angle gates. The bearing math is correct: theta_mirror is (360-raw)%360, raw 360 wraps to 0, the needle takes the shortest path, and the spectrum peak lines up with the theta_mirror needle. The tests for this area pass. The main functional weaknesses: (1) every one-poll read or parse failure immediately invalidates detection and DAQ health, which pauses Bulk angular for 20 s; (2) exception types that `poll()` does not catch freeze the whole Agent snapshot, so health keeps reporting `daq=1`; (3) raw Python exception text is forwarded as reason codes that Ground rejects; (4) the `detection.state` label is misleading. On the host monitor, `vcgencmd` cannot work for the unprivileged service account, and several site-specific link settings have no setup or documentation path._

#### SRC-1 · HIGH · confirmed · bug
**A single transient read/parse failure invalidates a still-fresh detection and pauses Bulk angular for resume_stable_seconds (20 s)**  
`src/rdf_node/source.py:192`

- **Masalah:** Source.poll sets self.error (CSV, line 192-194), self.status_error (line 175-176) or self.config_error (line 150-151) whenever ONE stable_read/parse fails. Examples: FILE_CHANGED_DURING_READ, a partial in-place write giving EMPTY_CSV or JSONDecodeError, or FILE_REPLACED_DURING_READ when an atomic rename happens between os.open and path.stat in util.stable_read:204. view() then adds the error (line 223) or marks status not fresh (line 217), so detection.valid=False and DAQ goes UNKNOWN, even though the cached record/status is still well inside the freshness windows. In agent._schedule (agent.py:421 and 462-464), any invalid snapshot that is not unverified_only sets reason SOURCE_NOT_ELIGIBLE. That clears the Bulk outbox and pushes bulk_resume_after out by resume_stable_seconds (default 20 s), so one 250 ms glitch stops normal telemetry/angular for 20 s. The same snapshot also publishes health daq=2 and flashes the panel needle/alert. The planning doc (docs/plans/Rancangan...md:233,259) says upstream may write these files non-atomically and asks for a retry on the next tick, not an outage.
- **Dampak:** Normal operation can lose a large share of angular frames to Ground (about 40% in the repro), the Link tab shows 'Grafik pause' WAIT_STABLE/SOURCE_NOT_ELIGIBLE with no real cause, health daq flaps to 2 (Ground rejects LIVE DoA/angular for that window), and the parse_errors counter climbs on a healthy source.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced with scratchpad/audit/angular_pause.py (Agent + loopback broker fixture, real scheduler, default resume_stable_seconds=20, writer at 10 Hz). In-place writer (open 'wb' + write, like upstream): 4 pauses in 150 s triggered by 'JSONDecodeError:STATUS_UNAVAILABLE', 'ValueError:STATUS_UNAVAILABLE' and 'EMPTY_CSV' (transitions at 43.2 s, 80.9 s, 84.1 s, 122.3 s, each followed by 20 s WAIT_STABLE). Only 8 telemetry/angular frames reached the broker, versus 14 with an atomic writer. The atomic writer also hit one 'FILE_REPLACED_DURING_READ' at 144.1 s, which started a 20 s pause even though the data read was complete. scratchpad/audit/nonatomic.py: about 3-6% of polls see a transient …
- **Saran perbaikan:** Treat read/parse failures as transient: retry stable_read once or twice inside the same poll, and keep the last good record/status. Raise self.error/status_error/config_error only after N consecutive failures, or once the cached object exceeds its freshness window, which already guards staleness. In stable_read, accept the bytes read when before/after fstat on the open fd match, even if the path now points to a new inode. In the agent, only reset bulk_resume_after when the source has been ineligible for more than one snapshot. Add a regression test with a concurrent in-place writer.
- **Verifikasi:** Code path verified. source.py:192-194, 175-176 and 150-151 set error/status_error/config_error after any single failed read. view() at source.py:217/223/231 then makes valid=False, and the cached record/status being fresh does not help. The scheduler re-reads the snapshot every 50 ms. At agent.py:421 any invalid, non-unverified-only snapshot gives SOURCE_NOT_ELIGIBLE, and agent.py:462-464 then clears the Bulk outbox and sets bulk_resume_after=now+20 s. No hysteresis exists. I reproduced it independently with nonatomic.py: a writer using one in-place write() per file at 5 Hz over 40 s gave 160 polls, 4 invalid views and 2 JSONDecodeError:STATUS_UNAVAILABLE. Each of those would start a 20 s …

#### SRC-2 · MEDIUM · confirmed · robustness
**Exceptions not caught by Source.poll freeze the whole Agent snapshot, so health keeps publishing daq=1 with a frozen age**  
`src/rdf_node/source.py:192`

- **Masalah:** Source.poll only catches (OSError, ValueError, TypeError, KeyError, RecursionError). Other errors escape. csv.reader raises _csv.Error, which is not a ValueError, for a lone '\r' inside a line. util.integer() raises OverflowError for a JSON number such as 1e400 in status.json/settings.json (strict_json rejects only the NaN/Infinity literals), or for int values above the float range. sqlite3.OperationalError can come from journal.config_revision. In agent._collect (agent.py:230-238) the exception skips self._snapshot(), so snapshot_data stays exactly as before, with only 'collector_error' set. For the CSV block, which runs on every poll, this lasts as long as the bad file exists. The scheduler keeps publishing telemetry/health every second with a fresh t and q but the frozen daq/age fields, so Ground treats the node as DAQ-healthy. The panel keeps the last valid needle behind its 5 s stale overlay.
- **Dampak:** While the source is broken or the engine is dead, Ground sees fresh health with daq=1 and a constant small age indefinitely. Host and link data in the local snapshot also stop updating.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced with scratchpad/audit/freeze_mqtt.py (Agent + broker fixture). After injecting a CR into the station_id field of DOA_value.html and then stopping the writer entirely, the last six health messages were (q,daq,age) = (18,1,149) ... (23,1,149), and the snapshot still had detection.valid=True with collector_error='Error'. scratchpad/audit/freeze.py: snapshot_seq stuck at 17 and Source.poll raised '_csv.Error: new-line character seen in unquoted field'. A status.json with "daq_num_dropped_frames":1e400 raises OverflowError ('cannot convert float infinity to integer').
- **Saran perbaikan:** In Source.poll, catch Exception (or add csv.Error, OverflowError and sqlite3.Error) per block and convert it into the block's error code. In util.integer/finite, reject non-finite values before calling int() and catch OverflowError. In agent._collect, still call _snapshot() (or publish an explicit COLLECTOR_ERROR invalid state) when source.poll raises, so health reflects the failure.
- **Verifikasi:** Reproduced. With a lone CR in the station_id field, Source.poll raises _csv.Error ('new-line character seen in unquoted field'), which is a subclass of Exception, not ValueError. parse_status with "daq_num_dropped_frames":1e400 raises OverflowError ('cannot convert float infinity to integer'). Neither is in the tuples caught at source.py:150/175/192. agent._collect (agent.py:229-238) catches it but skips _snapshot(), so snapshot_data stays frozen. The scheduler (agent.py:367-373) keeps publishing health with a fresh t and q but the frozen daq=1 and age. Ground (ground.py:171/282) therefore keeps treating DAQ/processing as healthy. Locally the 5 s stale overlay (app.js:411) and /healthz 503 …

#### SRC-3 · MEDIUM · confirmed · contract-mismatch
**Raw Python exception text is used as a validation reason, so Ground rejects telemetry/diagnostic/doa exactly when CSV parsing fails**  
`src/rdf_node/source.py:194`

- **Masalah:** For a ValueError, Source.poll stores self.error=str(e)[:64]. That is often Python's own message, for example "could not convert string to float: ''", "invalid literal for int() with base 10: '…'", or "'utf-8' codec can't decode byte …". view() puts it in detection.reasons, and diagnostic_view (line 258) forwards it upper-cased into validation_reasons for telemetry/diagnostic/doa. Ground (ground.py:203-206) requires every reason to match [A-Z0-9_:-]{1,64}, so spaces and quotes make it reject the whole message with BAD_DIAGNOSTIC_DOA. The doa.xml diagnostic path is meant for exactly the case where DOA_value.html is unusable, but a non-numeric value in any numeric CSV field disables it. The local panel also shows the raw English Python message as the alert text and as the 'Gate:' line.
- **Dampak:** Ground loses the raw doa.xml diagnostic stream (and its reasons) in the common case of an upstream CSV format mismatch, which is when operators need it most. Edge panel alerts show untranslated Python errors.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced with scratchpad/audit/diag_reason.py, which runs Edge Source + diagnostic_view and feeds the payload into Ground.receive. An empty heading field, 'n/a' in latency, a '…0.0' float timestamp and a leading 0xff byte all gave edge.error set to Python text, and Ground answered 'REJECTED BAD_DIAGNOSTIC_DOA' every time. Codes such as EMPTY_CSV and NUMBER_OUT_OF_RANGE are accepted.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Map parse failures to stable codes, for example CSV_FIELD_<index>_INVALID, CSV_UTF8_INVALID or CSV_INTEGER_INVALID, by wrapping float/int conversions or catching ValueError per field. Never forward str(e). Optionally sanitise reasons in diagnostic_view to [A-Z0-9_:-]. Add a test that runs Edge diagnostic payloads through Ground.receive.
- **Verifikasi:** Reproduced by running diag_reason.py. source.py:194 stores str(e) for a ValueError, which yields Python text such as "could not convert string to float: ''" or "'utf-8' codec can't decode byte...". diagnostic_view (source.py:258) only upper-cases it. Ground (ground.py:203-206) requires every reason to match [A-Z0-9_:-]{1,64}, so all four cases (empty field, 'n/a', float timestamp, non-UTF-8 byte) were REJECTED BAD_DIAGNOSTIC_DOA. Reasons that are proper codes (EMPTY_CSV, CSV_FIELD_COUNT, FileNotFoundError:DOA_UNAVAILABLE) pass. The local alert (agent.py:271-272) also shows the raw English Python text. This only happens on an upstream format mismatch, which is an edge case but exactly when …

#### MON-1 · MEDIUM · confirmed · bug
**Throttle/undervoltage are always unknown for the deployed service: vcgencmd needs the 'video' group, which rdf-edge does not have**  
`src/rdf_node/monitor.py:135`

- **Masalah:** Monitor.probe runs `vcgencmd get_throttled` as the service user. install.sh:44 creates rdf-edge with `useradd --system --user-group` (own group only), and deploy/systemd/rdf-edge.service sets no SupplementaryGroups. On Raspberry Pi OS, /dev/vchiq and /dev/vcio are root:video 0660, so vcgencmd exits non-zero, run() returns None, and throttled/undervoltage stay None forever. The agent's UNDERVOLTAGE error alert (agent.py:268) therefore never fires in production, and the System tab and health/detail always show 'unknown'/null.
- **Dampak:** Brown-out and throttling on the UAV Pi, which directly cause dropped DAQ frames, go unreported locally and to Ground.
- **Bukti:** Code trace: monitor.py:135-140 only sets the fields when vcgencmd succeeds. The installer, the unit file and the docs never add the video group (grep for SupplementaryGroups/video finds nothing relevant). Not runnable on this container (no Pi hardware), so the device permission behavior is based on platform knowledge, not reproduced here.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Read the world-readable /sys/devices/platform/soc/soc:firmware/get_throttled (or the hwmon in0_lcrit_alarm) first and fall back to vcgencmd. Alternatively add SupplementaryGroups=video to rdf-edge.service and document it.
- **Verifikasi:** install.sh:44 creates rdf-edge with only its own group, and deploy/systemd/rdf-edge.service sets User/Group=rdf-edge with no SupplementaryGroups. Nothing in the repo adds the video group. On Raspberry Pi OS, /dev/vchiq and /dev/vcio are root:video 0660 (99-com.rules). vcgencmd run by a non-video user fails ('VCHI initialization failed' / can't open /dev/vcio). In that case monitor.run returns None, or returns output without '0x', so monitor.py:135-140 never sets undervoltage or throttled. The UNDERVOLTAGE alert (agent.py:268), the System tab and health/detail throt/uv therefore stay null in production. I could not reproduce this without Pi hardware, but it is well-established platform …

#### SRC-4 · LOW · confirmed · ux
**detection.state shows UNVERIFIED or NO_FRESH_DOA for unrelated failures (missing files, stale data, clock, command in progress)**  
`src/rdf_node/source.py:239`

- **Masalah:** state is computed as UNVERIFIED if any reason contains the substring 'UNVERIFIED', otherwise NO_FRESH_DOA. CONFIG_ATTRIBUTION_UNVERIFIED is added whenever there is no record, settings are unavailable, or settings.json is newer than the last record. As a result, a fully verified node with DOA_value.html missing, settings.json missing, or a stopped engine reports state=UNVERIFIED. Conversely, fresh data blocked only by CLOCK_UNTRUSTED or CONTROL_IN_PROGRESS reports NO_FRESH_DOA. The panel shows this label on the main screen next to the age (app.js:295: `${d.state} / ${age}`), which gives contradictions such as 'NO_FRESH_DOA / 0.6 dtk', or tells the operator that verification is missing when the file is actually absent.
- **Dampak:** Operators on the 480x320 main screen get the wrong diagnosis. They may re-run `setup --verify-source` when the real problem is a missing or stale source, or they may think DoA is stale while the actual block is the clock or a running command.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced with scratchpad/audit/state_label.py (authority_verified=angle_verified=True). DOA_value.html missing gives state=UNVERIFIED with reasons [FileNotFoundError:DOA_UNAVAILABLE, NO_RECORD, DAQ_NOT_HEALTHY, CONFIG_ATTRIBUTION_UNVERIFIED]. settings.json missing gives UNVERIFIED. Engine stopped 10 min with settings saved afterwards gives UNVERIFIED. Fresh data with clock untrusted gives state=NO_FRESH_DOA, age=607ms, reasons=[CLOCK_UNTRUSTED]. Fresh data with a command in progress gives NO_FRESH_DOA, age=611ms.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Derive state from the highest-priority reason: SETUP_REQUIRED, then SOURCE_ERROR/NO_RECORD, NO_FRESH_DOA, DAQ_NOT_HEALTHY, CLOCK_UNTRUSTED, CONTROL_IN_PROGRESS, CONFIG_UNAVAILABLE/ATTRIBUTION, and finally UNVERIFIED only for SOURCE_UNVERIFIED/ANGLE_UNVERIFIED. Match exact codes, not substrings, and display a localized label in the panel.
- **Verifikasi:** Logic verified at source.py:232-239. With no record, attrib is False, which always adds CONFIG_ATTRIBUTION_UNVERIFIED, so a missing or unreadable DOA file gives state=UNVERIFIED. With fresh data blocked only by CLOCK_UNTRUSTED or CONTROL_IN_PROGRESS, no reason contains 'UNVERIFIED', so the state is NO_FRESH_DOA. app.js:295 shows `${d.state} / ${age}` on the main screen when no doa.xml diagnostic is available. However, the alert banner (app.js:302-303, built from agent.py:271) shows reasons[0], e.g. CLOCK_UNTRUSTED or FileNotFoundError:DOA_UNAVAILABLE, so the real cause is visible on the same screen. Ground does not use detection.state. This is a misleading label, not a broken feature, so …

#### SRC-5 · LOW · uncertain · robustness
**Values in CSV/status fields the app never uses reject the whole record (no DoA) or the whole status (DAQ UNKNOWN)**  
`src/rdf_node/source.py:53`

- **Masalah:** parse_csv range-checks acquisition_latency_ms (0..3600000), latitude/longitude and gps/compass heading (0..360). None of these fields are read anywhere else in src/ or web/; grep confirms they are only produced at source.py:53-57. Any out-of-range or non-numeric value fails the whole record and blocks all DoA. Examples are a negative latency from clock skew, a negative compass offset, 360.5, an empty lat/lon when GPS is absent, or 'nan'. Likewise parse_status calls integer() on daq_num_dropped_frames and data_frame_index whenever the key exists. A JSON null or a negative counter therefore raises, the entire status.json is discarded (STATUS_UNAVAILABLE), and the daq_ok/sync flags are lost too. A missing key, by contrast, is treated as unknown (None). dropped_frames is not used for gating at all.
- **Dampak:** If the deployed engine emits any such value (exact upstream formatting is not verifiable in this repo), LIVE DoA never becomes valid, or DAQ stays UNKNOWN, because of metadata the bridge discards anyway.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced with scratchpad/audit/unused_fields.py. Latency -3, compass -15 and gps heading 360.5 give NUMBER_OUT_OF_RANGE. latitude '' gives "could not convert string to float: ''". longitude 'nan' gives NUMBER_OUT_OF_RANGE. In status.json, daq_num_dropped_frames null or data_frame_index null raise TypeError, and daq_num_dropped_frames -1 raises INTEGER_OUT_OF_RANGE. All are rejected, while the same keys missing are accepted.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Parse unused metadata leniently: store None on failure and never fail the record. Keep strict checks only for timestamp, DoA, confidence, power, frequency and the 360 values. In parse_status, treat null or invalid counters the same as a missing key (None) instead of rejecting the whole status object.
- **Verifikasi:** The mechanism is accurate. source.py:53-56 range-checks latency, lat/lon and the two headings, which are never used anywhere else, and source.py:107-108 call integer() on dropped_frames/frame_index whenever the key exists, so null raises TypeError and the whole status is dropped. Whether this ever fires depends on the real upstream formatting, which is not in this repo. KrakenSDR normally writes numeric defaults (0.0 lat/lon, numeric heading and latency), and the planning doc (section 4.4) deliberately asks the adapter to validate finite values and field counts. Neither the auditor nor I could show a realistic trigger, so this is a robustness hardening suggestion rather than a demonstrated …

#### MON-2 · LOW · confirmed · robustness
**Clock trust is re-sampled only every 30 monitor loops; one failed or slow timedatectl call blocks LIVE for 30+ s**  
`src/rdf_node/monitor.py:68`

- **Masalah:** clock_trusted is updated only when n%30==0. run() returns None on any non-zero exit or after its 2 s timeout, for example while systemd-timedated is being D-Bus activated on a loaded Pi. That single failure sets clock_trusted=False and clock_state=UNTRUSTED for the next ~30 loops (at least 30 s, longer when ping/udevadm add delay). view() then adds CLOCK_UNTRUSTED, detection goes invalid, and the scheduler pauses Bulk angular for a further resume_stable_seconds. Likewise, after NTP first syncs at boot, LIVE can start up to ~30 s late.
- **Dampak:** Occasional 50 s or longer outages of LIVE detection and angular streaming caused by a single transient tool failure.
- **Bukti:** Code trace: monitor.py:64-69 together with run() at monitor.py:10-14, then source.py:227 and agent.py:421/464. Not reproduced on hardware.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Re-check the clock every few seconds, and keep the previous value on a None/timeout result until N consecutive failures (or treat it as UNKNOWN rather than flipping to untrusted).
- **Verifikasi:** monitor.py:64-69 samples timedatectl only when n%30==0, and a None result (non-zero exit or the 2 s timeout in run()) sets clock_trusted=False and UNTRUSTED until the next sample, at least 30 loops later. Each loop is 1 s plus probe time, so ping and udevadm make it longer. source.py:227 then invalidates detection, and agent.py:421/464 adds the 20 s Bulk resume wait. The boot case is certain: if the agent starts before NTP sync, LIVE stays blocked for up to about 30 s after sync. The case of a single transient timedatectl failure is plausible but was not reproduced. Low severity is appropriate.

#### SRC-6 · LOW · confirmed · ux
**ADC overdrive is parsed from status.json but never shown, alerted, or sent**  
`src/rdf_node/source.py:110`

- **Masalah:** parse_status extracts daq_status.adc_overdrive and view() puts it in snapshot.daq.adc_overdrive. web/app.js, the agent alerts, telemetry/health and telemetry/health/detail never read it (grep finds it only in source.py). An overdriven ADC, which makes DoA unreliable, is invisible to the operator and to Ground even when the engine reports it.
- **Dampak:** RF saturation goes unnoticed. The planning design expected 'ovr' in health detail.
- **Bukti:** grep -rn overdrive src web matches only source.py:110 and source.py:252.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Show ADC overdrive in the System tab (Frame/Delay/IQ row), raise a warning alert when it is truthy, and add an 'ovr' field to telemetry/health/detail (update docs/MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md).
- **Verifikasi:** grep confirms adc_overdrive appears only at source.py:110 and :252 (plus the demo writer at cli.py:199). app.js, the agent alerts and the health/detail payload (agent.py:375-380) never use it. Both planning docs list ADC overdrive for the RDF/DAQ panel and an 'ovr' field in health detail (Dashboard...md:402, Rancangan...md:615/742). The as-built MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY does not promise it. This is a real gap between plan and implementation (data is parsed and then discarded), but it is a missing indicator rather than wrong behavior, so low.

#### MON-3 · LOW · confirmed · doc-mismatch
**T900 USB identity uses a hard-coded, undocumented physical USB path, so other ports or a Pi 4 show a permanent IDENTITY_MISMATCH warning**  
`config/example.yaml:18`

- **Masalah:** Monitor reports usb=PRESENT only if ID_PATH equals link.expected_usb_path (default 'platform-xhci-hcd.0-usb-0:2:1.0', which is one specific port on one board), along with VID/PID. Neither setup nor install sets this value. Neither the README nor docs/OPERATIONS.md explain how to obtain or configure it, or how to create the /dev/t900 alias (the installer explicitly does no udev changes). Any other port or Pi model gives IDENTITY_MISMATCH, and tabAlerts (app.js:268) then puts a permanent warning badge on the Link tab even while PPP and MQTT work.
- **Dampak:** A false link warning that operators learn to ignore, or confusion during field setup.
- **Bukti:** grep for expected_usb_path/serial_alias finds only config/example.yaml:15-18 and monitor.py:120-126. OPERATIONS.md:11 mentions only 'physical path sesuai'.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Have `rdf-node setup` read ID_PATH/VID/PID of the chosen serial device via udevadm and store them, and document the udev alias plus these keys in OPERATIONS.md.
- **Verifikasi:** monitor.py:124-126 requires the exact ID_PATH from link.expected_usb_path (default 'platform-xhci-hcd.0-usb-0:2:1.0', config/example.yaml:18), otherwise it reports IDENTITY_MISMATCH. If the /dev/t900 alias is absent it reports MISSING. setup (cli.py:55-81) never sets these keys, and README/OPERATIONS.md never name serial_alias or expected_usb_path or explain how to obtain them (OPERATIONS.md:11 only says 'physical path sesuai'). Any other port or board makes tabAlerts (app.js:268) put a permanent warn badge on the Link tab, even while PPP and MQTT are fine. The values are configurable, so this is a documentation and setup gap, low.

#### SRC-7 · LOW · refuted · ux
**`rdf-node doctor` reports the source READABLE when run with sudo, even if rdf-edge cannot read it, and uses different parsing than the service**  
`src/rdf_node/cli.py:108`

- **Masalah:** doctor has to run as root, because /etc/rdf-node/config.yaml is 0640 root:rdf-edge. Root reads _share regardless of ACLs, so doctor prints 'Source: READABLE' even when setup's grant_read was declined and the service gets PermissionError:DOA_UNAVAILABLE. doctor also parses settings with plain json.loads (the service uses strict_json, which rejects duplicate keys and NaN) and reads status.json with a fixed 16384 limit instead of source.max_record_bytes. The preflight can therefore pass while the running service reports CONFIG_UNAVAILABLE or a read error.
- **Dampak:** Misleading preflight during installation. The most common deployment failure (missing read ACL for rdf-edge) is not detected.
- **Bukti:** Code trace: cli.py:108-121 vs source.py:139-156 and install.sh:45/57 (config mode 0640).
- **Saran perbaikan:** Have doctor test readability as rdf-edge, for example os.access with a setuid/setgid probe or `runuser -u rdf-edge -- test -r`, and reuse Source's exact parsing (strict_json, max_record_bytes) for all three files.
- **Verifikasi:** The core premise is wrong. The documented invocation is `sudo -u rdf-edge rdf-node doctor` (README.md:168, README.md:396 troubleshooting row 'Permission source', OPERATIONS.md:37), not plain sudo. It runs as the service user, who can read /etc/rdf-node/config.yaml through its 0640 root:rdf-edge mode (install.sh:45-57), so it does test source readability as rdf-edge and catches a missing ACL. doctor does not call require_root. What remains is minor: json.loads vs strict_json differ only for duplicate keys or NaN literals, and the fixed 16384 status limit equals the default max_record_bytes. That residue is not a meaningful functional defect.

### Agent orchestration

_The Edge orchestration layer (agent.py scheduler/collector/inbound threads, codec.py, plus how it drives source/mqtt/control/monitor) is solid at the payload level. Field-by-field, the health, health/detail, doa (ok=1/ok=0), diagnostic/doa, angular, diagnostic/angular, state, ACK, settings/reported and availability/LWT payloads match docs/PROTOCOL.md and MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md, and loop exceptions are contained so no thread dies silently. The real problems are in timing and state propagation. Bulk Angular pauses for 20 s whenever a single QoS1 PUBACK takes more than 500 ms. Capabilities and availability are published once and then go stale. The fire-once settings report can permanently wedge Ground's "Refresh config". Panel-saved MQTT settings silently override later config.yaml or import-bundle provisioning. Diagnostic DoA is either rejected by Ground or shown as fresh when it is hours old. All findings below were reproduced with scripts in the scratchpad audit/ directory using the loopback broker fixture, except where noted as a code trace; no repository file was modified._

#### AGT-1 · HIGH · confirmed · bug
**Ground 'Refresh config' is permanently wedged (SETTINGS_REQUEST_PENDING) after one lost settings/reported or a config.get the Edge never received**  
`src/rdf_node/ground.py:342`

- **Masalah:** Edge treats settings/reported as fire-once. control.py:67-75 builds the payload once and agent.py:385-392 clears settings_request_id as soon as the outbox accepts it. If the Control link drops before PUBACK, the outbox is cleared (mqtt.py:267) and nothing retries. Ground sets settings_request_id when it publishes cmd/config/get (ground.py:374). It clears it only in three cases: on the matching settings/reported, on a terminal non-APPLIED ack/config (ground.py:241-243), or when state.sid changes (ground.py:132). There is no timeout. Edge acks config.get as APPLIED before the report is sent, so a lost report leaves Ground pending forever. The same happens when the operator presses Refresh while the Edge Control client is offline. Ground still holds the retained state, and config.get does not require fresh health (ground.py:325), so the command is published and lost (clean start, no replay) and no ACK ever arrives. Every later Refresh is rejected with SETTINGS_REQUEST_PENDING (ground.py:342-343) until the Edge process restarts and gets a new sid.
- **Dampak:** On a flaky T900 link a common event permanently disables the raw-settings view and the safe-settings form baseline on Ground. Recovery requires restarting the Edge service.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced with scratchpad/audit/repro_settings_wedge.py (Ground with a stubbed client). Case 1: config.get returns REQUESTED, then ack/config APPLIED arrives with no settings/reported, followed by fresh health from the same sid. The second Refresh returns {'stage':'REJECTED','error':'SETTINGS_REQUEST_PENDING'}. Case 2: Refresh while the Edge is offline returns REQUESTED with no ACK; Refresh after the Edge reconnects with the same sid returns SETTINGS_REQUEST_PENDING.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Give Ground's pending config.get a deadline, e.g. clear it when the command's expires_ms plus the 30 s report expiry has passed, or allow a new request with a new id. Optionally have Edge keep the report until PUBACK and re-offer it after reconnect while it is still within 30 s.
- **Verifikasi:** Re-ran scratchpad repro_settings_wedge.py and got the same result: the first Refresh returns REQUESTED, ack/config APPLIED arrives, fresh health follows, and the next Refresh returns SETTINGS_REQUEST_PENDING. The offline-Edge case behaves the same. In code, Ground clears settings_request_id in only three places (ground.py:132, 152, 241-243), none on a timer. Edge acks APPLIED (control.py:74-80) before the report is queued. The scheduler drops its pending id once the outbox accepts the report (agent.py:388-392). A disconnect then clears the outbox (mqtt.py:267) with no replay. The wedge has more triggers than the auditor lists: (a) Ground's own MQTT reconnect while the report is in flight …

#### AGT-2 · MEDIUM · confirmed · bug
**A single QoS1 PUBACK slower than 500 ms pauses Bulk Angular for resume_stable_seconds (20 s) and drops the queued frame**  
`src/rdf_node/agent.py:424`

- **Masalah:** The scheduler sets reason='CONTROL_BACKLOG' whenever ctrl.pending_age_ms>500. pending_age_ms is non-zero for the whole time a QoS1 publish waits for its PUBACK. When that happens, lines 461-464 clear the Bulk outbox and push bulk_resume_after to now+20 s. Normal Edge traffic includes QoS1 publishes: the periodic state every 60 s (line 382), every state change (run/daq/clock/cfg/profile, rate-limited to 1/s), and every command ACK. On a 15 kbit/s PPP/T900 link, a PUBACK round trip easily exceeds 500 ms, and certainly does while a ~3 KB Bulk frame (~1.6 s of link time) is in the PPP queue. Each such PUBACK stops Angular for 20 s. DAQ or clock state flapping retriggers it continuously.
- **Dampak:** The 360-degree graph on Ground goes stale for 20-40 s at a time in normal operation on the target link. Any operator command (ACK) also stops the graph. The cause is the system's own traffic, not real congestion.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced with scratchpad/audit/repro_backlog.py, using a broker fixture that delays only PUBACK by 700 ms and the default resume_stable_seconds=20. Angular streamed at t=23.6, 32.6 and 41.5 s. One QoS1 ACK at t=45.0 s produced bulk_reason CONTROL_BACKLOG at 45.6 s, then WAIT_STABLE. The periodic 60-s state at 61.7 s produced another CONTROL_BACKLOG. No Angular frame was published after 41.5 s for the rest of the 30-s window. In steady state with no commands, about 20 of every 60 s are lost.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Do not treat one in-flight QoS1 publish as backlog. Use outbox depth or the age of queued messages, a much higher threshold derived from the link budget, or skip the resume_stable penalty for CONTROL_BACKLOG and just defer the next Bulk send.
- **Verifikasi:** Re-ran repro_backlog.py (PUBACK delayed 700 ms). Angular went out at 23.6, 32.6 and 41.6 s. The QoS1 ACK at 45.0 s produced CONTROL_BACKLOG then WAIT_STABLE, the 60-s state at 61.7 s produced it again, and no Angular frame followed. In code, agent.py:424 compares ctrl.pending_age_ms, the age of the single in-flight publish until PUBACK (mqtt.py:339), against 500 ms. agent.py:461-464 then clears the Bulk outbox and adds resume_stable_seconds (20 s). That is single-message RTT, not queue backlog. Whether it fires in the field depends on real T900 RTT, which is unmeasured (TEST_REPORT.md:59). It is plausible at the documented 15 kbit/s, where one 2,988 B Bulk frame holds the link about 1.6 s, …

#### AGT-3 · MEDIUM · confirmed · bug
**Capabilities are published only on Control (re)connect, so helper/target readiness changes never reach Ground and its buttons stay wrongly disabled or enabled**  
`src/rdf_node/agent.py:360`

- **Masalah:** The 'caps' publish happens only inside `if ctrl.generation!=self._generation`. capabilities() is derived from helper_status, which the collector refreshes every 20 polls (~5 s) with a 1-s timeout (agent.py:231-233). If the helper is not up yet when Control connects, the retained capabilities carry helper_available=false and all remote_* fields false. That stays until the next MQTT reconnect. Triggers include rdf-edge.service having no After=rdf-control-helper.service ordering, a helper restart (Restart=on-failure), or a transient 1-s status timeout. The reverse also happens: the helper going away is never announced. Ground's UI enables Start/Stop/Restart/Reboot/Shutdown/PPP/config-patch strictly from these fields (web/ground.js:19-43, 148, 300-310). The local Edge snapshot recomputes capabilities every poll, so the panel and Ground disagree.
- **Dampak:** Ground operators cannot issue lifecycle, reboot, shutdown, PPP or config commands even though the Edge would accept them, or they see controls enabled after the helper died. Recovery needs an unrelated MQTT reconnect.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced with scratchpad/audit/repro_caps_avail.py. At connect the published caps had helper_available=False, remote_reboot=False and remote_processing=False. After helper_status became available, the local snapshot showed True/True/True 8 s later, but only 1 capabilities message had been published and it still said remote_reboot=False and remote_processing=False.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Re-publish capabilities, retained QoS1, whenever the capabilities dict (excluding volatile fields) differs from the last one sent, for example by checking it in the scheduler loop like state. Also add After=/Wants=rdf-control-helper.service to rdf-edge.service.
- **Verifikasi:** The only capabilities publish is agent.py:360, inside the `ctrl.generation!=self._generation` branch. MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md:56 documents this, but it still causes a functional fault: capabilities() (agent.py:296-322) depends on helper_status, which _collect refreshes every 20 polls (agent.py:231-233). The helper is a single-threaded UnixStreamServer (helper.py:375), so status calls can also time out while it is busy. rdf-edge.service has only After=network.target, with no ordering on rdf-control-helper. ground.js mutationBlockReason/updateSettingsButton (lines 17-26, 297-300) enable buttons strictly from the retained caps. A realistic trigger: the commissioning flow of editing the helper …

#### AGT-5 · MEDIUM · confirmed · contract-mismatch
**diagnostic/doa forwards Python exception text as a validation reason, so Ground rejects the diagnostic exactly when the CSV source is broken**  
`src/rdf_node/source.py:194`

- **Masalah:** Source.poll stores str(e)[:64] of any ValueError as self.error. This includes built-in messages such as "could not convert string to float: ''", "invalid literal for int() with base 10: ...", and UnicodeDecodeError text. view() adds that string to reasons, and diagnostic_view() upper-cases it into validation_reasons (source.py:258). The agent publishes it unchanged (agent.py:417). Ground requires every reason to match [A-Z0-9_:-]{1,64} (ground.py:202-206) and drops the whole message as BAD_DIAGNOSTIC_DOA. A single non-numeric or empty field in DOA_value.html (for example an empty confidence column, or a frequency written as '433920000.0') therefore removes the only DoA information Ground could still show.
- **Dampak:** When the source format breaks, Ground shows no diagnostic DoA at all, so the operator loses the evidence needed to diagnose the problem remotely.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced with scratchpad/audit/repro_diag_reason.py, using an empty confidence column, a valid doa.xml, and a live broker with Edge and Ground running. Edge published validation_reasons ['DIAGNOSTIC_UNVERIFIED', "COULD NOT CONVERT STRING TO FLOAT: ''", ...]. Ground ended with diagnostic_doa=None and rejected=2, and a direct ground.receive raised ValueError BAD_DIAGNOSTIC_DOA.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Map parser exceptions to fixed codes in Source.poll, e.g. 'CSV_VALUE_INVALID' for float/int conversion errors and 'CSV_ENCODING_INVALID' for decode errors. Alternatively sanitize reasons in diagnostic_view with re.sub('[^A-Z0-9_:-]','_',...).
- **Verifikasi:** Verified directly. source.py:194 stores str(e)[:64] for any ValueError. finite() and integer() (util.py:36, 44) let float() and int() raise built-in messages, and UnicodeDecodeError is a ValueError subclass. Upper-cased by diagnostic_view (source.py:258), the messages become "COULD NOT CONVERT STRING TO FLOAT: ''", "INVALID LITERAL FOR INT() WITH BASE 10: '433920000.0'" and "'UTF-8' CODEC CAN'T DECODE BYTE 0XFF...". All three fail Ground's [A-Z0-9_:-]{1,64} check (ground.py:204), which raises BAD_DIAGNOSTIC_DOA and drops the whole diagnostic message. The trigger needs a malformed numeric field or bad encoding, an edge case, but exactly when the diagnostic is most needed. Medium fits.

Re-provisioning therefore silently has no effect, and doctor prints the config values, …

#### AGT-4 · LOW · confirmed · data-correctness
**Retained availability stays online:true after a graceful stop, reboot, or MQTT disable/reconfigure**  
`src/rdf_node/agent.py:203`

- **Masalah:** Agent.stop() and _apply_pending_mqtt() (agent.py:181-182) stop the clients gracefully. Client._session then sends DISCONNECT with reason 0x00 (mqtt.py:462-465), which tells the broker to discard the Last Will. The Agent never publishes an explicit retained online:false first. After `systemctl stop rdf-edge`, a Ground-commanded reboot (systemd sends SIGTERM, so the shutdown is graceful), switching MQTT off in the panel, or pointing the panel at another broker, the broker keeps {online:true} for the old sid indefinitely. The docs describe availability as online while connected and LWT on loss. Note that the bundled Ground ignores the availability topic entirely (ground.py has no handler), so the users affected are the existing third-party Ground dashboards this package is meant to feed.
- **Dampak:** Dashboards show the node online during reboots, service stops, or after MQTT is disabled or moved. This hides real outages from the operator.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced with scratchpad/audit/repro_caps_avail.py. After agent.stop(), broker.retained['sdr/v2/uav-01/availability'] was b'{"v":2,"sid":"9d18f825","online":true,...}' and the broker saw no offline message. The fixture, like Mosquitto, suppresses the Will on a normal DISCONNECT.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Before stopping the Control client, publish retained availability {online:false, reason:'SHUTDOWN'|'RECONFIGURED'} and wait briefly for its PUBACK. Alternatively send DISCONNECT with reason code 0x04 (Disconnect with Will Message) on stop and reconfigure.
- **Verifikasi:** Agent.stop() and _apply_pending_mqtt() call Client.stop(). The session loop then sends a normal DISCONNECT 0xE0 0x00 (mqtt.py:461-466), which suppresses the Will, and no explicit online:false is published first. SIGTERM goes through the graceful path (cli.py:227-231), so systemctl stop, reboot and shutdown all leave the retained {online:true} for a dead sid. This contradicts the documented topic semantics (MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md:58, 352-362). Lowered to low because the bundled Ground ignores availability entirely: receive() has no branch for it. The docs also tell consumers to treat it as a hint only and to check state/health/timestamps (MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md:86, 366).

#### AGT-7 · LOW · confirmed · ux
**diagnostic/doa heartbeat repeats the last doa.xml sample forever (even after the file is gone), and Ground shows it as RECEIVED rather than stale**  
`src/rdf_node/source.py:264`

- **Masalah:** diagnostic_view() reports available=bool(rec), and the last parsed doa.xml record is never discarded. The scheduler (agent.py:409) therefore re-publishes the same sample every 3 s indefinitely, including after doa.xml is deleted or the SDR stops. PROTOCOL.md and MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md say it is sent while the XML is available. Each re-send advances q, so Ground refreshes diagnostic_seen. Ground computes stale only from reception age (ground.py:264, received_age>5000 ms), and ground.js:248 shows 'UNVERIFIED / RECEIVED' as the headline. The DOA_XML_STALE/UNAVAILABLE reasons appear only in the small reasons line.
- **Dampak:** An operator at Ground sees an old raw bearing presented as just received, which is misleading during exactly the outages the diagnostic path is meant for.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced with scratchpad/audit/repro_xml_heartbeat.py, using a doa.xml with TIME one hour old that was then deleted. Edge published 3 diagnostic/doa messages in the 10 s after deletion. The Ground snapshot showed stale=False, received_age_ms=2777, source age 3612 s, and reasons ['DIAGNOSTIC_UNVERIFIED','DOA_XML_UNAVAILABLE','DOA_XML_STALE'].
- **Saran perbaikan:** Stop the heartbeat, or mark available=false, once doa.xml is missing or older than a bound. On Ground, derive stale from source_timestamp_ms (or from DOA_XML_STALE/UNAVAILABLE in the reasons) in addition to reception age.
- **Verifikasi:** diagnostic_record is never cleared (source.py:195-207). diagnostic_view reports available=bool(rec) (source.py:264), so after doa.xml is deleted the scheduler keeps re-publishing every 3 s (agent.py:409-418). That contradicts 'selama XML tersedia' (sent while the XML is available) in MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md:50 and PROTOCOL.md:168. Ground sets stale only from received age (ground.py:264), and ground.js:248 shows 'UNVERIFIED / RECEIVED'. However, repeating an unchanged, old sample while the file exists is explicitly documented ('Diulang meski sampel tidak berubah', i.e. repeated even if the sample is unchanged). Ground also shows the source TIME, and the reasons line shows …

#### AGT-8 · LOW · confirmed · robustness
**A backward wall-clock step locks out every new DoA record until timestamps pass the previous maximum**  
`src/rdf_node/source.py:182`

- **Masalah:** Source.poll raises SOURCE_TIME_REGRESSED for any record whose timestamp is lower than record_signature[0], the highest timestamp seen. It never resets that baseline. After a backward clock step on the Pi, for example NTP correcting a fast RTC or a manual date fix, every new sample is rejected and the stale old record is kept. DoA and Angular stay invalid for the full size of the step, which can be minutes to hours, until the process restarts. doa.xml has the same behavior (source.py:198-199).
- **Dampak:** After a clock correction, detection is unavailable for as long as the clock moved back, with no operator-visible explanation beyond the reason code.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced with scratchpad/audit/repro_regress.py. After a first record at t, five new records at t-120 s plus k*500 ms all produced error SOURCE_TIME_REGRESSED, record q stayed at 1, and valid was False.
- **Saran perbaikan:** When regression persists, for example across N consecutive new samples or when a clock step is detected via monotonic/wall delta, re-baseline record_signature and invalidate the old record instead of rejecting forever.
- **Verifikasi:** record_signature and diagnostic_signature are written only on acceptance (source.py:187, 201) and never reset outside __init__. Every record with timestamp < record_signature[0] raises SOURCE_TIME_REGRESSED (source.py:182-183), so a backward wall-clock step locks out new DoA until timestamps pass the old maximum or the process restarts. On a Pi the usual correction (fake-hwclock/NTP) steps forward, so backward steps are uncommon (a manual date fix, or chrony correcting a fast clock). Low is right.

#### AGT-10 · LOW · confirmed · contract-mismatch
**Edge panel MQTT topic list omits telemetry/diagnostic/angular, so its tracked delivery state is never shown**  
`web/app.js:46`

- **Masalah:** The Agent records per-topic delivery for 'telemetry/diagnostic/angular' (agent.py:457 via _offer/_topic_delivery_update), and the topic is documented in MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md. The panel's outbound mqttTopics list (web/app.js:46-58) has no row for it, so updateTopicStatuses() never renders its SENT, ERROR or OUTBOX_EXPIRED state. That is the only on-device way to see whether diagnostic curves leave the node while normal Angular is blocked.
- **Dampak:** Operators cannot verify the diagnostic Angular fallback from the touchscreen, and expiry failures stay invisible.
- **Bukti:** Code trace: the outbound list contains availability, capabilities, state, settings/reported, telemetry/health, telemetry/health/detail, telemetry/doa, telemetry/diagnostic/doa, telemetry/angular, ack/config and ack/operation, with no telemetry/diagnostic/angular. A scheduler run with a blocked Bulk (scratchpad/audit/repro_diag_angular.py) recorded 2 of 5 diagnostic-angular attempts as OUTBOX_EXPIRED; the panel has no row to show them.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Add a ['telemetry/diagnostic/angular','QoS 0 / JSON / Control', ...] row to the outbound topic group.
- **Verifikasi:** web/app.js:46-58 has no 'telemetry/diagnostic/angular' row, so updateTopicStatuses (app.js:136-137) never renders the delivery state that agent.py records for it via _offer. The gap is worse than the auditor states: tabAlerts (app.js:271) checks every entry in mqtt_topic_delivery. A non-skipped ERROR on diagnostic/angular, such as OUTBOX_EXPIRED or a connection-reset failure, therefore turns the Data tab badge red while every visible row in that tab (index.html:44) looks fine. This only happens while the diagnostic fallback is active, so low, though the unexplained red badge is a real touchscreen UX wart.

#### AGT-11 · LOW · confirmed · bug
**Persisted profile in the journal silently overrides telemetry.profile from config.yaml**  
`src/rdf_node/agent.py:31`

- **Masalah:** Agent.__init__ loads self.profile from journal key 'profile' and falls back to cfg['telemetry']['profile'] only when that key is missing or invalid. After any stream.set, from the panel or from Ground, editing telemetry.profile in /etc/rdf-node/config.yaml and restarting has no effect. Nothing documents this precedence, and neither the panel nor doctor shows that the config value is ignored.
- **Dampak:** Deployment-time profile changes (for example forcing 'control' to save bandwidth) do not take effect, which confuses the operator.
- **Bukti:** Code trace at agent.py:31-32; control.py:161 persists the value with a.journal.set('profile', ...).
- **Saran perbaikan:** Store the config value alongside the persisted profile and let a changed config value win, or document the precedence and show the profile's source in the panel.
- **Verifikasi:** agent.py:31-32 prefers the journal 'profile' key, written by control.py:161, over cfg telemetry.profile, so after any stream.set an edit to config.yaml has no effect. Nothing documents this. doctor prints cfg['telemetry']['profile'] (cli.py doctor), which can disagree with the effective profile. Persisting a runtime choice is arguably intentional, and the panel shows the effective profile and can change it, so low.

#### AGT-9 · LOW · refuted · ux
**Demo mode rejects profile changes (stream.set) although they touch no hardware**  
`src/rdf_node/control.py:82`

- **Masalah:** CommandManager.submit raises DEMO_NO_HARDWARE_CONTROL for every non-read operation in demo, including stream.set. stream.set only changes Agent.profile and the scheduler. The Edge panel's profile modal (web/app.js:741-753) is fully usable in demo but every choice fails, so CONTROL and GRAPH profile behavior (Angular pause and pacing) cannot be demonstrated.
- **Dampak:** The demo misrepresents the product: a pure software setting appears broken.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced with scratchpad/audit/repro_demo.py: Agent(demo=True) commands.submit(stream.set profile=control) returned {'stage':'REJECTED','result':{'error':'DEMO_NO_HARDWARE_CONTROL'}}.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Exempt stream.set from the demo hardware block, or disable the profile button in DEMO mode with an explanatory label.
- **Verifikasi:** control.py:82 does reject stream.set in demo with DEMO_NO_HARDWARE_CONTROL, but this matches demo's design: every mutation is blocked and the CLI banner says nothing is changed. The stated impact is wrong. Demo loads example.yaml, where mqtt.enabled is false (config/example.yaml:23, cli.py:213 'MQTT OFF'). _schedule therefore never gets past MQTT_NOT_READY in demo, and no Angular pause or pacing exists to demonstrate. Accepting the profile would only change a label. At most a stylistic nit, not a functional bug.

### MQTT transport

_The MQTT 5 client (mqtt.py) and RFC 6455 transport (mqtt_ws.py) hold up at the wire level. A fuzz run fed a real 360-value Angular PUBLISH (2644 B) through the TCP reader and through random WebSocket fragmentation with interleaved pings, using random chunk sizes, and every packet came through intact. QoS, retain and expiry flags match docs/MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md, SUBSCRIBE options (retain handling 2, retain-as-published) are correct, and test_network plus test_websocket pass (26 tests). The real defects are in the connection lifecycle and in how other modules use client state. The reconnect backoff never resets after a stable session, and it never backs off when a session fails after CONNACK. A silently dead link is not detected while QoS0 telemetry is flowing. A graceful stop suppresses the Last Will, so the retained `availability` stays online. The Agent reads normal QoS1 PUBACK round-trip time as backlog and pauses Angular for 20 s. Separately, Ground's config refresh locks up for good after one lost `settings/reported`, and neither panel shows why MQTT is in ERROR._

#### MQTT-1 · MEDIUM · confirmed · bug
**Reconnect backoff never resets after a stable session; it ratchets up to reconnect_max (30 s) and stays there**  
`src/rdf_node/mqtt.py:274`

- **Masalah:** In Client._run (lines 272-279) the only reset is `if last_success and now-last_success<20: delay=1`, and it is checked AFTER waiting `delay`. When a session that lasted more than 20 s drops, the code sets delay=max(delay,2), waits, then doubles. By then now-last_success is always more than 20, so delay is never reset. So delay doubles on every drop (2, 4, 8, 16, 30 s) even when hours of healthy connection separate the drops. After a single outage of about 30 s, or about 5 separate link blips, every later disconnect waits the full reconnect_max (default 30 s, plus jitter) before the first reconnect attempt.
- **Dampak:** On the PPP/T900 radio link, where short drops are the normal failure mode, every blip after the first few becomes a 30 s+ MQTT outage instead of about 2 s. The panel shows ERROR, Ground sees HEALTH STALE and rejects commands with NODE_NOT_FRESH, and Angular then waits another resume_stable_seconds (20 s) after reconnect, so the graph is dark for about 50 s per blip.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced with a deterministic fake-clock simulation that drives the real Client._run (scratchpad/audit/backoff_sim.py). Scenario A (6 failed attempts, then three 10-minute stable sessions that each drop): the wait after each stable session is 30.0 s, 30.0 s, 30.0 s. Scenario C (fresh client, two stable 10-minute sessions that drop): waits are 2.0 s then 4.0 s, still growing.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Reset delay to its initial value when a session reached CONNACK and stayed up for a minimum stable time (for example, set delay=1 in _session right after CONNACK once the session has lasted N seconds, or in _run whenever last_success falls inside the session that just ended and it lasted longer than the threshold). Add a unit test of the backoff schedule using a fake clock.
- **Verifikasi:** mqtt.py:274-279: the only reset to delay=1 happens when now-last_success<20 AFTER the wait. A session that lived >20 s therefore never resets, and delay keeps doubling up to reconnect_max. The reset logic is effectively inverted: short-lived sessions reset to 1 s and long healthy sessions ratchet. I re-ran backoff_sim.py, which drives the real Client._run with a fake clock. Scenario A gave waits 30/30/30 s after three 10-minute sessions; scenario C gave 2 s then 4 s after stable sessions. reconnect.reset is never called from production code. Lowered to medium: the link always recovers, and the extra delay per drop is bounded by reconnect_max (default 30 s, configurable 5-120). TCP sessions …

#### MQTT-2 · MEDIUM · confirmed · robustness
**Failures after CONNACK reconnect every ~1 s forever with no backoff (SUBACK denial, PUBACK >= 0x80, SERVER_PACKET_LIMIT, session takeover)**  
`src/rdf_node/mqtt.py:278`

- **Masalah:** Every CONNACK sets last_success (line 410). Any failure shortly afterwards then hits `now-last_success<20 -> delay=1`, so the client reconnects about every 1.0-1.3 s indefinitely. Several triggers are deterministic and repeat on every attempt: a broker ACL that denies one of the 11 command subscriptions (SUBACK_REJECTED, line 420); a QoS1 publish rejected with reason >= 0x80, for example 0x87 on `capabilities`/`state` or 0x97 quota (line 347), which tears down the whole connection instead of failing one message; a broker Maximum Packet Size below the 2.6-3 KB Angular frame (SERVER_PACKET_LIMIT, line 358), which kills the Bulk session on every frame; and two processes using the same client ID. Ground's client ID is fixed to `{node_id}-ground` (ground.py:34), so a second Ground instance causes a session-takeover war in which both clients flap about once per second.
- **Dampak:** Each reconnect costs a TLS (and WebSocket) handshake over a ~15 kbit/s radio link, drops the whole outbox, churns the retained Last Will/availability on every abrupt close, and keeps Control from ever becoming READY. One misconfigured ACL entry or one oversize frame turns into a permanent connect/disconnect loop instead of a stable, visible error.
- **Bukti:** Backoff simulation Scenario B: repeated post-CONNACK failures give waits of 1.0 s, 1.0 s, 1.0 s and so on. Real fixture repro (scratchpad/audit/storm_real.py): Broker(deny_sub='ppp') with Edge-like subscriptions recorded 18 CONNECTs in 20 s (client.reconnects=17).
- **Saran perbaikan:** Apply exponential backoff based on how long the session lasted, not on CONNACK time. Treat a single PUBACK rejection or an oversize packet as a per-message failure (notify on_error, keep the connection). Make the Ground client ID configurable or add an instance suffix.
- **Verifikasi:** CONNACK sets last_success (mqtt.py:410). Any deterministic failure shortly after it hits the `<20 -> delay=1` reset at 278, so the client loops at about 1.0-1.3 s with no backoff. backoff_sim scenario B shows 1.0 s waits indefinitely. I re-ran storm_real.py (fixture deny_sub on one command subscription): 17 CONNECTs in 20 s, error SUBACK_REJECTED. A PUBACK reason >=0x80 raises PUBLISH_REJECTED (344-347) and tears down the session; the existing test test_puback_rejection only checks the callback. If an ACL denies `availability` or `capabilities`, which are published at every generation (agent.py:359-360), that also becomes a permanent loop. On a ~1.9 KB/s link, a TLS handshake every second …

#### MQTT-3 · MEDIUM · confirmed · bug
**Normal QoS1 PUBACK round-trip > 500 ms is treated as CONTROL_BACKLOG and pauses Angular for resume_stable_seconds (20 s)**  
`src/rdf_node/agent.py:424`

- **Masalah:** Client.pending_age_ms (mqtt.py:339) is the age of the single in-flight publish. For QoS1 that includes waiting for the PUBACK, i.e. the full link RTT plus any bytes already queued on the shared PPP link. The scheduler sets reason='CONTROL_BACKLOG' whenever ctrl.pending_age_ms>500. Lines 461-464 then clear the Bulk outbox and push bulk_resume_after out by resume_stable_seconds. So every QoS1 publish whose PUBACK takes more than 500 ms (the periodic 60 s `state`, every state change, availability/capabilities, ACKs) stops the Angular stream for 20 s. On a slow radio link the Bulk Angular frame itself (~3 KB, ~1.6 s at 15 kbit/s) delays any concurrent Control PUBACK beyond 500 ms, so the stream throttles itself.
- **Dampak:** If the Edge->broker RTT exceeds about 500 ms, which is plausible on a T900/PPP link especially while a Bulk frame is in transit, the Angular graph is paused at least 20 s of every 60 s (about 33%), and more with state changes and ACKs. A dropped queued frame shows as 'DILEWATI BULK_PAUSED_CONTROL_BACKLOG'. This depends on real link latency, which was not measured on hardware.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced with the Agent plus the fixture broker behind a delay proxy (scratchpad/audit/latency_agent_natural.py), default resume_stable_seconds=20, 300 ms one-way delay, no simulated state changes. bulk_reason went None -> CONTROL_BACKLOG at t=61.9 s (the 60 s state refresh), then WAIT_STABLE until t=82.0 s. max pending_age_ms was 586. With a 50 ms delay, CONTROL_BACKLOG never occurred.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Measure backlog by queued work, not round-trip time: for example outbox depth, the age of the oldest queued (not in-flight) Control message, or time-to-write excluding the PUBACK wait. Alternatively, scale the threshold to the observed PUBACK RTT. Do not reset the 20 s stabilisation window for transient backlog.
- **Verifikasi:** Client.pending_age_ms (mqtt.py:339) is measured from pop until the PUBACK result (343), so for QoS1 it includes the full RTT. agent.py:424 then treats >500 ms as CONTROL_BACKLOG: lines 461-464 clear the bulk outbox and push bulk_resume_after out by 20 s. The planning docs intend 'estimated control backlog >500 ms', not RTT. I re-ran latency_agent_natural.py (300 ms one-way, 90 s): CONTROL_BACKLOG at t=61.9 (the 60 s state refresh) then WAIT_STABLE until 82.0, max pending_age 589 ms, and only 6 angular frames in 90 s. The mechanism is real. A ~3 KB bulk frame (~1.6 s at 15 kbit/s) on the shared PPP link also delays any concurrent Control PUBACK beyond 500 ms. Lowered to medium because …

#### MQTT-6 · MEDIUM · confirmed · bug
**Ground 'Refresh config' is blocked forever (SETTINGS_REQUEST_PENDING) after a single lost settings/reported message**  
`src/rdf_node/ground.py:342`

- **Masalah:** Ground sets settings_request_id when it queues config.get (line 374) and refuses any new config.get while it is set (342-343). It is cleared only by a matching settings/reported, by a terminal non-APPLIED ack/config (241-243), or by a new Edge sid. Edge ACKs config.get as APPLIED immediately (control.py:74-79) and publishes the report separately and only once. Edge clears its own pending ID as soon as the outbox accepts it (agent.py:388-392), so it cannot retry. The report is lost on any Edge or Ground MQTT reconnect in that window (outbox cleared, clean start, no replay), on expiry, or if it arrives more than 30 s late (_fresh raises without clearing). No timeout exists.
- **Dampak:** After one dropped message on the radio link, the operator can no longer fetch native settings from Ground until Ground or the Edge Agent restarts. The UI gives only a pending error, with no way to clear it.
- **Bukti:** Repro (scratchpad/audit/settings_stuck.py): first config.get returns REQUESTED. An ack/config APPLIED arrives with no settings/reported. Journal stage: APPLIED, settings_request_id still set. Second config.get returns {'stage':'REJECTED','error':'SETTINGS_REQUEST_PENDING'}. grep confirms there is no time-based clearing.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Expire settings_request_id after the report TTL (for example 30-45 s after issue, or after APPLIED + 30 s). Also clear it when the MQTT generation changes, and let the operator retry.
- **Verifikasi:** I re-ran settings_stuck.py: after ack/config APPLIED with no settings/reported, settings_request_id stays set and the second config.get returns REJECTED SETTINGS_REQUEST_PENDING (ground.py:342-343). It is cleared only at 152 (matching report), 243 (terminal non-APPLIED ack) or 132 (new sid). Edge clears its own pending ID once the outbox accepts it (agent.py:388-392) and never retries. There is no time-based expiry. Further wedge paths: a Ground reconnect clears the outbox after client.offer accepted the command (ground.py:370 passes no on_error, so the journal stays REQUESTED); a lost REJECTED ack; or a report older than 30 s, where _fresh raises at 144 without clearing. Recovery requires …

#### MQTT-7 · MEDIUM · confirmed · ux
**Panels never show the MQTT error reason or READY; broker CONNACK reason codes are collapsed into CONNACK_REJECTED**  
`web/app.js:309`

- **Masalah:** Client.status() returns state (CONNECTED/CONNECTING/ERROR), ready, and error (for example CONNACK_REJECTED, TLS_CERTIFICATE_VERIFY_FAILED, SUBACK_REJECTED, PING_TIMEOUT, FILENOTFOUNDERROR). The Edge panel only renders `.state` (app.js:235, 289, 307, 309) and never reads `mqtt_control.error` or `.ready`. Ground preview does the same (ground.js:577-581). mqtt.py:405 also maps every non-zero CONNACK reason (0x84 protocol, 0x85 client id, 0x86 bad credentials, 0x87 not authorized, 0x88 server unavailable) to the same CONNACK_REJECTED. MQTT_GROUND.md acceptance step 1 tells operators to 'Pastikan kedua client READY pada tab Link', but the Link tab never shows READY, and it shows CONNECTED while SUBACKs are still pending.
- **Dampak:** On the 480x320 touchscreen an operator who enters a wrong password, a wrong CA/TLS setting or a wrong WebSocket path sees only 'ERROR / ERROR' and cannot tell the causes apart without SSH or the raw API. The documented acceptance check cannot be performed from the UI.
- **Bukti:** grep over web/app.js finds no MQTT `.error` or `ready` usage; the only `.error` references are topic delivery and command results. Code trace at mqtt.py:405 and mqtt.py:234-236.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Show the error code (and READY vs CONNECTED) in the Link tab row and the MQTT settings modal. Preserve the CONNACK reason as CONNACK_REJECTED_0X86 and similar. Align the docs with what the UI actually shows.
- **Verifikasi:** web/app.js renders only `.state` for MQTT (140, 235, 267, 289, 307, 309). No MQTT `.error` or `.ready` is displayed, and agent._snapshot alerts (agent.py:265-273) contain no MQTT alert, so the error code never reaches the touchscreen. mqtt.py:234 reports CONNECTED after CONNACK even while SUBACKs are pending, and mqtt.py:405 collapses every CONNACK reason into CONNACK_REJECTED. MQTT_GROUND.md:118 asks operators to check READY on the Link tab, and the UI cannot show it. Minor inaccuracy: ground.js does read `mqtt_control.ready` for gating and shows 'MQTT Control belum tersambung.' (lines 21, 149, 302, 311), but like the Edge panel it displays only state, never the error code (577-581, …

#### MQTT-4 · LOW · confirmed · robustness
**Dead link goes undetected while QoS0 telemetry flows: PINGREQ is only sent after keepalive seconds of TX silence and last_rx is never used**  
`src/rdf_node/mqtt.py:332`

- **Masalah:** PINGREQ is queued only when now-last_mqtt_tx>=keepalive. The Edge Control client publishes telemetry/health every 1 s and Bulk publishes Angular every ~9 s (keepalive 15 s), so neither ever sends PINGREQ during normal operation. last_rx is updated (lines 309, 385) but never read. QoS0 writes 'succeed' into the kernel buffer, so a half-open TCP connection (PPP down, radio out of range, broker host gone without RST) is only detected by a QoS1 publish hitting the 10 s PUBLISH_PROGRESS_TIMEOUT, or by the socket send buffer filling up. The Bulk client sends only QoS0, so it relies on buffer exhaustion alone.
- **Dampak:** After a radio dropout the Edge panel keeps showing MQTT CONNECTED and topic delivery 'SENT' for tens of seconds. Control detection depends on the next QoS1 `state` (up to 60 s + 10 s) and Bulk on buffer exhaustion. All telemetry in that window is lost silently. This contradicts acceptance step 7 in MQTT_GROUND.md ('lihat stale/lost').
- **Bukti:** Repro (scratchpad/audit/halfopen.py), keepalive=5 s, 1 Hz QoS0 publishes. Broker PINGREQ count after 12 s: 0. After the proxy silently blackholed the link, the client stayed connected=True, state=CONNECTED for 40 s and reported all 40 QoS0 publishes as sent. Only a QoS1 publish then detected it (10.1 s, PUBLISH_PROGRESS_TIMEOUT).
- **Saran perbaikan:** Send PINGREQ when no packet has been received for keepalive seconds (as paho does with last_msg_in), or always send PINGREQ every keepalive interval. Use last_rx to detect silence. Optionally set TCP_USER_TIMEOUT/keepalive socket options.
- **Verifikasi:** mqtt.py:332 queues PINGREQ only after keepalive seconds of TX silence. Every publish write updates last_mqtt_tx (375; current[4]=True at 361), and last_rx (309/385) is never read. Edge Control publishes health every 1 s, so it never pings. Dead-link detection relies on a QoS1 PUBACK timeout (the state refresh every 60 s, plus a 10 s PUBLISH_PROGRESS_TIMEOUT) or on the send buffer filling up. Lowered to low because: (1) the Edge panel has an independent PPP interface state and a peer ping probe (monitor.py:16-19, 141) that shows NO_REPLY or DOWN, so the operator is not left with only 'MQTT CONNECTED'; (2) Ground's client is mostly silent on TX, so it pings and detects the loss within about …

#### MQTT-5 · LOW · confirmed · contract-mismatch
**Graceful stop sends DISCONNECT reason 0x00, so the Last Will is discarded and retained availability stays {online:true} after stop/reboot/shutdown/MQTT reconfigure**  
`src/rdf_node/mqtt.py:464`

- **Masalah:** On stop_event the client sends `b'\xe0\x00'`, a normal DISCONNECT. Per MQTT 5 §3.14.4 the broker then deletes the Will without publishing it. agent.stop() runs on SIGTERM (cli.py:226-231), which covers systemctl stop, OS reboot/shutdown triggered by the Ground reboot/shutdown commands, and _apply_pending_mqtt when the operator saves new MQTT settings. In all of these the retained `availability` on the broker keeps the last {online:true}, and no explicit offline message is published. MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md says availability is online when ready and a Last Will when the connection is lost, and that Ground should display it.
- **Dampak:** Any Ground or dashboard that follows the documented availability semantics shows the node online after it was deliberately stopped, rebooted or shut down, or after its broker was changed. This is especially misleading right after a shutdown command. The bundled Ground currently ignores availability entirely, so its own preview is unaffected.
- **Bukti:** Repro (scratchpad/audit/will_repro.py): retained availability before stop was {"v":2,"online":true}; after Client.stop() it was still {"v":2,"online":true}. Raw capture of the bytes sent on stop: e000.
- **Saran perbaikan:** On graceful stop, publish a retained {online:false, reason:'STOPPED'} (QoS1) before DISCONNECT, or send DISCONNECT with reason 0x04 'Disconnect with Will Message'. Update the fixture to honour reason 0x04 and add a test.
- **Verifikasi:** mqtt.py:464 sends b'\xe0\x00', a DISCONNECT with Remaining Length 0 (reason 0x00). Per MQTT 5 §3.1.2.5 the broker discards the Will, and no explicit offline message is published, so the retained availability stays {online:true} after agent.stop(). That covers SIGTERM via cli.py:226-231 and _apply_pending_mqtt client.stop at agent.py:182. The code fact is certain. Lowered to low because: the bundled Ground ignores `availability` (ground.py receive has no branch for it); MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md documents the Will only for 'koneksi hilang' and calls availability a last-value hint, not a heartbeat, to be cross-checked with state/health; and for OS reboot/shutdown the outcome races with PPP …

#### MQTT-8 · LOW · confirmed · robustness
**WebSocket handshake rejects valid 101 responses that repeat a header (for example two Set-Cookie lines)**  
`src/rdf_node/mqtt_ws.py:148`

- **Masalah:** client_handshake raises WEBSOCKET_BAD_HANDSHAKE whenever a header name appears twice. HTTP permits repeated fields, and Set-Cookie is routinely repeated by reverse proxies and load balancers (for example AWSALB + AWSALBCORS) in front of MQTT-over-WSS brokers. Only the security-relevant upgrade headers need to be unique.
- **Dampak:** WSS through a common proxy or load balancer fails permanently. Combined with MQTT-2 this becomes a steady reconnect loop. It does not affect a direct Mosquitto listener.
- **Bukti:** Repro (scratchpad/audit/ws_dupheader.py): a correct 101 response with valid Accept/Protocol plus two Set-Cookie headers gives 'rejected: WEBSOCKET_BAD_HANDSHAKE'.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Reject duplicates only for Upgrade, Connection, Sec-WebSocket-Accept, Sec-WebSocket-Protocol and Sec-WebSocket-Extensions, and ignore or merge the other headers.
- **Verifikasi:** mqtt_ws.py:148 raises WEBSOCKET_BAD_HANDSHAKE when any header name repeats. I re-ran ws_dupheader.py: a valid 101 response with two Set-Cookie lines is rejected. HTTP allows repeated Set-Cookie. Low is correct: the deployment targets a Ground Mosquitto over a private PPP/T900 link, where a cookie-setting load balancer in front of the broker is unlikely. When it does occur, the failure is permanent and, combined with MQTT-2, loops.

#### MQTT-9 · LOW · confirmed · test-gap
**No tests cover connection-lifecycle behaviour (backoff schedule, TCP keepalive, Will on graceful vs abrupt close, half-open detection, PUBACK timeout, oversize packet)**  
`tests/test_network.py:47`

- **Masalah:** test_network.py covers round-trips, SUBACK denial, auth, TLS and WSS close, but not the reconnect delay sequence, PINGREQ cadence over TCP (only the WSS variant), Will publication on abrupt close or its suppression on graceful DISCONNECT, PUBLISH_PROGRESS_TIMEOUT, SERVER_PACKET_LIMIT, or the CONTROL_BACKLOG/pending_age interaction. broker_fixture.py treats any DISCONNECT as graceful regardless of reason code, never sends session-takeover DISCONNECT, and ignores message expiry, so fixes for MQTT-1, MQTT-2, MQTT-4 and MQTT-5 cannot be verified with the current fixture.
- **Dampak:** Regressions in reconnect and liveness behaviour, the most important properties on a lossy radio link, are not caught before device acceptance.
- **Bukti:** Read of tests/test_network.py (26 network/websocket tests pass) and broker_fixture.py:214 (`elif kind==14: graceful=True`). Every defect above went undetected by the suite.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Add fake-clock unit tests for _run backoff, a TCP keepalive test that publishes QoS0 continuously, a blackhole-proxy half-open test, and Will tests. Extend the fixture to honour DISCONNECT reason 0x04 and to support client-ID takeover.
- **Verifikasi:** tests/test_network.py has no test for the reconnect delay sequence, TCP PINGREQ cadence under continuous publishing (only the WSS variant at line 92-104), Will on abrupt close versus graceful stop, PUBLISH_PROGRESS_TIMEOUT, SERVER_PACKET_LIMIT, or CONTROL_BACKLOG. grep finds none of those identifiers in tests/. broker_fixture.py:214 treats any DISCONNECT as graceful, and the fixture has no client-ID takeover or expiry handling. This is accurate but derivative of MQTT-1/2/4/5 and not itself a functional defect, so it stays low.

### Ground receiver & UI

_The Ground receiver (ground.py) and preview (ground.html/js/css) work for the happy path. Run end-to-end against the loopback broker fixture, an Edge Agent with a faked helper and Chromium, these all worked: LIVE angular with flags=63, config.get to settings/reported, config.patch to PERSISTED_UNVERIFIED, ppp.restart to APPLIED after newer health, reboot and shutdown prepare/execute with challenge, and stream.set. Every fetch() in ground.js matches a route in api.py, and field names line up. Session/sequence/flag validation of incoming telemetry is mostly sound. The main problems are elsewhere. There is no expiry or reconciliation for commands that get no reply, so one lost settings report blocks Refresh config. In the default config (authority/angle not verified) the preview never shows the 360 curve it receives. Browser-side state is fragile: a PPP request that never reached the server permanently blocks PPP restart, and some frequencies can never be entered. Rejection messages are overwritten within 1 s. Capabilities can stay stale for a whole MQTT connection. A Ground restart or Edge agent restart rewrites unrelated PPP and shutdown records._

#### GRD-2 · HIGH · confirmed · bug
**A config.get with no matching settings/reported permanently blocks 'Refresh config' (SETTINGS_REQUEST_PENDING)**  
`src/rdf_node/ground.py:342`

- **Masalah:** submit_command sets self.settings_request_id when config.get is queued (line 374). That ID is cleared in only two places: when a settings/reported that passes every check arrives (line 152), or when ack/config brings a terminal stage other than APPLIED (lines 241-243). Nothing clears it on expiry (expires_ms is only 15 s), and the Ground preview has no cancel path. It therefore stays set for the rest of the Edge session when: the command or its ack is lost on the radio link or while Ground is reconnecting (clean start, no offline replay); Edge acks APPLIED but the report is lost; or the report fails Ground validation, e.g. a timestamp skew outside -1..+30 s raises SOURCE_TIMESTAMP_NOT_FRESH at line 144 before the ID is cleared. After that, every later config.get is rejected at line 342-343. Only a new Edge sid (Edge agent restart) resets it (line 132). The 'Refresh config' button is also never disabled, so clicking it while the Edge is offline queues a request that can never be answered.
- **Dampak:** On a lossy PPP/T900 link, one lost or late message breaks the native-settings view and the settings-form prefill until someone restarts the Edge agent.
- **Bukti:** scratchpad/audit/ground/repro_ground.py (R1): APPLIED ack plus a settings/reported 31 s old gives 'SOURCE_TIMESTAMP_NOT_FRESH'. A second config.get with now_ms patched +1 h returns {'stage':'REJECTED','error':'SETTINGS_REQUEST_PENDING'}. Reproduced live too: with the Edge agent stopped, the first Refresh gives REQUESTED; the second gives POST /api/v2/commands 400 SETTINGS_REQUEST_PENDING (overwrite.js output).
- **Saran perbaikan:** Clear settings_request_id when the request's expires_ms (plus the 30 s report expiry) has passed, and mark the journal entry EXPIRED/OUTCOME_UNKNOWN. Clear it before validating a report whose id matches. Expose the pending ID and age in /api/v2/config so the UI can show it.
- **Verifikasi:** Confirmed. settings_request_id is set at ground.py:374. It is cleared only at line 152 (a valid report), lines 241-243 (a terminal non-APPLIED ack/config) or line 132 (sid change).
