Nothing checks expiry. I re-ran the auditor's R1 and got SETTINGS_REQUEST_PENDING one hour later. A common trigger: config.get skips the health check (ground.py:325), and the Refresh button is never gated. If the operator clicks Refresh while the PPP link is down, Ground's own client to its local broker is still ready, so the command is published with clean start and no session (mqtt.py connect_packet flags=2, expiry 0) and dropped. The Edge then reconnects with the same sid, and every later Refresh is rejected. …

#### GRD-1 · MEDIUM · confirmed · ux
**Ground preview never draws the 360-point curve in the default (authority/angle unverified) configuration**  
`web/ground.js:628`

- **Masalah:** config/example.yaml ships authority_verified=false and angle_verified=false. In that case Edge publishes telemetry/angular with flags=23 (parsed|fresh|daq|config). ground.py:100-107 routes these frames (and all telemetry/diagnostic/angular frames, ground.py:81-89) into self.diagnostic_angular, and angular_view() (ground.py:277) returns null. ground.js only ever fetches /api/v2/angular/latest (line 628) and never calls /api/v2/angular/diagnostic/latest. So the polar plot stays empty forever, with the text 'Belum ada kurva lengkap. Tunggu telemetry, source gate, dan interval.', even though a full 360-sample frame arrives every ~9 s. The same happens whenever the source is invalid (stopped SDR, DAQ degraded), which is exactly when the diagnostic angular topic is used. docs/MQTT_GROUND.md:123 says 'Ground menampilkan keduanya sebagai UNVERIFIED' for regular DoA ok=0 and the partial-flag angular frames, but only the DoA side is shown. Second problem if this gets wired up: diagnostic_angular_view() marks frames stale once source age > freshness.doa_ms (2500 ms, ground.py:298), while these frames arrive every 6-30 s, so the view would read STALE most of the time.
- **Dampak:** On a fresh deployment, or whenever the SDR source is degraded, the Ground operator never sees the angular spectrum, although the data is being received and paid for in link budget. The UI text wrongly suggests the operator just needs to wait.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced live with harness scratchpad/audit/ground/harness.py (VERIFIED=0). GET /api/v2/angular/latest returned null, while GET /api/v2/angular/diagnostic/latest returned a 360-value frame with flags 23. Playwright screenshot s1-desktop.png shows the empty polar grid and the 'Belum ada kurva lengkap' text. grep confirms ground.js has no reference to angular/diagnostic.
- **Saran perbaikan:** When /api/v2/angular/latest is null (or stale), fetch /api/v2/angular/diagnostic/latest and draw it in a clearly separate UNVERIFIED style (e.g. amber dashed, no DoA needle) with its validation_reasons. Base diagnostic staleness on the expected publish interval (e.g. >= 10-35 s), not freshness.doa_ms.
- **Verifikasi:** Confirmed. When flags are 23 (authority/angle not verified), ground.py:100-107 sends the frame to diagnostic_angular, so angular_view() (ground.py:277) returns None. ground.js:628 only ever fetches /api/v2/angular/latest; grep finds no 'angular/diagnostic' anywhere in web/. MQTT_GROUND.md:123 (acceptance step 4) says Ground shows the partial-flag angular as UNVERIFIED, so implementation and docs disagree. Downgraded to medium because this happens in the pre-commissioning state: README section 5 makes enabling LIVE (authority/angle VERIFIED) a normal install step. Once commissioned, flags=63 and the curve draws. When the source later degrades, the last LIVE frame is still drawn, as STALE. …

#### GRD-3 · MEDIUM · confirmed · bug
**A PPP restart POST that never reaches the Ground API leaves a browser-only OUTCOME_UNKNOWN record that blocks all future PPP restarts**  
`web/ground.js:229`

- **Masalah:** In requestPppRestart, any error without a 4xx status is recorded as OUTCOME_UNKNOWN and saved to localStorage (lines 228-236). That covers a network error, a fetch timeout, or a 503 with an empty body from the max_clients limiter, where r.json() throws. If the request never reached the server, the Ground journal has no such ID: refreshPppHistory gets 404 from /api/v2/operation/result and silently ignores it (line 186), so the record stays unresolved forever. On every later click, previous=pppRecords.filter(pppIsUnresolved) is non-empty, so the UI sends confirm_previous_unknown:true (lines 202, 213). Because the server has no pending PPP operation, ground.py:344-345 rejects it with PPP_RESTART_CONFIRMATION_NOT_REQUIRED. The catch block does not handle this code, so the phantom record is never resolved and every retry fails the same way, including after a page reload.
- **Dampak:** After a single transient HTTP failure, PPP restart (the remote link-recovery action) is permanently unusable from that browser profile until its site data is cleared by hand.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced with scratchpad/audit/ground/ppp_phantom.js against the live harness. The POST to /api/v2/commands was aborted once with Playwright route('connectionrefused'), leaving the local record OUTCOME_UNKNOWN 'Failed to fetch' and 0 ppp.restart operations in the Ground journal. The next two attempts gave 'POST /commands 400 {"stage":"REJECTED","error":"PPP_RESTART_CONFIRMATION_NOT_REQUIRED"}', and after reload localStorage still held the phantom record.
- **Saran perbaikan:** When /api/v2/operation/result returns 404 for a locally unresolved PPP record, drop it or mark it NOT_SUBMITTED. Send confirm_previous_unknown only when the server reported pending operations (e.g. from operations/latest or a dedicated pending-PPP endpoint). Treat PPP_RESTART_CONFIRMATION_NOT_REQUIRED as a signal to clear local unresolved records and retry once without the flag after user confirmation.
- **Verifikasi:** Code trace confirms. At ground.js:229 a status-less error makes the record OUTCOME_UNKNOWN. The 404 from operation/result is swallowed at :186. pppIsUnresolved keeps the record, so :202/:213 always send confirm_previous_unknown:true. With no pending PPP operation on the server, ground.py:344-345 returns PPP_RESTART_CONFIRMATION_NOT_REQUIRED. The catch at :231 does not handle that code, and old.confirmedBy is only set on REQUESTED (:221), so the phantom record persists in localStorage forever. Downgraded to medium: the trigger is an HTTP failure against the loopback Ground API (e.g. rdf-ground restarting at the moment of the click; with max_clients=16 a 503 is unlikely), which is rare. The …

#### GRD-4 · MEDIUM · confirmed · bug
**Settings form rejects many valid frequencies (e.g. 128.05, 256.1, 512.05 MHz) through float MHz->Hz conversion, blocking every settings change at those frequencies**  
`web/ground.js:347`

- **Masalah:** validateSettings computes hz=mhz*1e6 and requires Number.isSafeInteger(hz). For many decimal MHz values the IEEE product is not an integer: 128.05*1e6 = 128050000.00000001, 256.1*1e6 = 256100000.00000003, 512.05*1e6 = 512049999.99999994. renderSettings pre-fills the field with String(frequency_hz/1e6) (line 292). So when the node is tuned to such a frequency, changing only gain or squelch fails client-side with 'Frekuensi harus 24 sampai 1766 MHz dan tepat dalam Hz.' The operator cannot type the frequency in any way that passes, and blanking it is rejected because the base is set. About 2.2% of 12.5 kHz channels in 24-1766 MHz are affected (3,135 of 139,361).
- **Dampak:** From Ground, operators cannot set these channels, and cannot change gain, bandwidth or squelch while the node sits on one of them.
- **Bukti:** node script: for 12.5 kHz grid, 3135/139361 values fail; 128050000 -> '128.05' -> 128050000.00000001 (isSafeInteger false). Reproduced live: harness with CENTER_MHZ=128.05, Refresh config, change gain to 20.7, Apply gives frequency-error 'Frekuensi harus 24 sampai 1766 MHz dan tepat dalam Hz.' and nothing is sent (flow.js f3 output).
- **Saran perbaikan:** Convert with rounding and a tolerance: hz=Math.round(mhz*1e6), then accept if Math.abs(mhz*1e6-hz)<1e-3. Or parse the decimal string into integer Hz without floating-point arithmetic.
- **Verifikasi:** Reproduced with node: 128.05*1e6=128050000.00000001, 256.1*1e6=256100000.00000003, 512.05*1e6=512049999.99999994, all failing Number.isSafeInteger. 3135/139361 channels on the 12.5 kHz grid fail the String(hz/1e6) round trip that renderSettings uses (ground.js:292). validateSettings (:347-349) then rejects them, and the field is MHz-only, so no input passes. The rejection blocks every settings change (gain, bandwidth, squelch) while the node is tuned to such a frequency. Medium is fair.

#### GRD-5 · MEDIUM · confirmed · ux
**Command rejection reasons shown in 'Operasi terakhir' are overwritten by the next poll within ~1 s**  
`web/ground.js:610`

- **Masalah:** command() (line 270), the reboot flow (line 491) and the shutdown flow (lines 496, 523-536) write their error or result into #operation. Every poll (1 s) then unconditionally replaces #operation with snapshot.last_operation (line 610) whenever any journal entry exists. Ground-local rejections (400 from submit_command) and HTTP errors create no journal entry, so the operator sees the error for under a second, followed by an older, unrelated operation that may well show APPLIED. Examples: SETTINGS_REQUEST_PENDING, NODE_NOT_FRESH, UNSUPPORTED_OPERATION, 'Challenge tidak tersedia', 'Status shutdown tidak tersedia'. NODE_NOT_FRESH is also returned when Ground's own MQTT client is not ready (ground.py:324), which misleads further.
- **Dampak:** Operators cannot tell why a click did nothing and may believe a stale APPLIED entry is the result of their action.
- **Bukti:** overwrite.js against live harness: after the second Refresh, #operation='SETTINGS_REQUEST_PENDING' at t+205 ms and t+411 ms, then from t+616 ms it shows the previous config.get REQUESTED JSON again. The same happened for a 403 on Restart RDF in outage.js.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Show command/flow errors in a separate status element that persists until the next user action. Or stop overwriting #operation with last_operation for N seconds after a local error, or unless last_operation.updated_ms is newer than the error. Return a distinct MQTT_NOT_READY code for the client.ready check.
- **Verifikasi:** Confirmed. command() (ground.js:270) and the reboot/shutdown flows write errors into #operation. poll() at :610 overwrites #operation with snapshot.last_operation every ~1 s whenever any journal entry exists. Ground-local rejections in submit_command (ground.py:322-363) return before journal.accept, so they create no entry. The previous, unrelated operation therefore replaces the error within a second. NODE_NOT_FRESH for !client.ready (ground.py:324) is also accurate.

#### GRD-6 · MEDIUM · confirmed · contract-mismatch
**Capabilities are published only on MQTT (re)connect, so Ground keeps stale remote_* flags and disables all actions until the next reconnect**  
`src/rdf_node/agent.py:360`

- **Masalah:** Edge publishes the retained capabilities message only inside 'if ctrl.generation!=self._generation' (agent.py:356-361). The remote_* fields come from helper_status, which is polled every 20 collector ticks (~5 s, line 231) and set to {} on any helper error. If the helper is not answering when the Control client connects, all remote_* flags (except remote_commands) go out false and are never republished while the connection stays up. Triggers include: rdf-edge.service not ordered after rdf-control-helper.service, 'controls approve' restarting both units at once, or a 1 s helper timeout. ground.py:134 stores them, and ground.js:23, 155 and 310 then disable Start/Stop/Restart/Reboot/Shutdown/PPP/Settings with 'Helper Edge belum tersedia untuk aksi host.' even after the helper is healthy.
- **Dampak:** All Ground remote operations can stay unavailable indefinitely on a stable link after an ordinary admin restart, with a misleading reason shown.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced with scratchpad/audit/ground/harness_helperdelay.py (fake helper refuses for the first 4 s). After 25 s, Ground /api/v2/snapshot capabilities had helper_available False and remote_reboot/shutdown/ppp/processing/config_patch False, while agent.capabilities()['remote_reboot'] was True. Playwright showed start/stop/restart/reboot/shutdown/ppp-restart/settings-apply all disabled.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Republish capabilities whenever the capabilities() dict changes (compare against the last published value), or periodically like state. Add After=/Wants=rdf-control-helper.service to rdf-edge.service.
- **Verifikasi:** Confirmed. The capabilities offer happens only inside the generation-change branch (agent.py:356-361), and nothing republishes it. remote_* is derived from helper_status, which is polled only every 20 collector ticks (agent.py:231-233; poll_ms 250, so about 5 s) and set to {} on any error. The helper is a single-threaded UnixStreamServer (helper.py:375-379) and the call has a 1 s timeout. rdf-edge.service has only After=network.target, with no ordering on the helper. 'controls approve' restarts both units together (cli.py:174). If MQTT becomes ready before the next successful status poll, retained capabilities go out with remote_* false and stay that way. ground.js:23/155/310 then disable …

#### GRD-7 · MEDIUM · confirmed · bug
**Commands without an ACK stay REQUESTED/ACCEPTED forever; the documented operation.get re-query is not implemented**  
`src/rdf_node/ground.py:370`

- **Masalah:** submit_command marks the journal REQUESTED and calls client.offer without on_error (line 370). If the message is then dropped (outbox cleared on disconnect, 15 s outbox expiry) or Edge's ACK is lost (QoS1 acks expire after 30 s, Ground uses clean start with no offline replay), nothing ever moves the operation to a terminal stage. No path compares expires_ms, and acks are only accepted when request.sid matches (line 236), so if the Edge agent restarts mid-operation, even a resent ack is ignored. docs/MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md:101 ('Ground perlu menyimpan ID operation dan query ulang bila reconnect melewatkan ACK') and docs/CONTROL.md:176 ('Ground dapat query operation.get') describe a recovery path that does not exist: Ground never sends operation.get, and an operation.get reply only updates its own record, not the target. Effects: snapshot.last_operation shows e.g. 'service.restart REQUESTED' indefinitely; an unacked shutdown.execute keeps pending-shutdowns true forever; an unacked ppp.restart requires the extra confirmation for every later restart.
- **Dampak:** The operations view shows misleading non-terminal states after any link flap, and follow-up actions get extra gates and warnings.
- **Bukti:** repro_ground.py (R4): service.restart submitted, no ack; with now_ms +1 h, snapshot last_operation is still 'REQUESTED service.restart'. An unacked system.shutdown.execute gives pending_shutdowns True. Code: grep shows no expires_ms comparison or operation.get usage in ground.py/ground.js.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Pass on_error to client.offer and mark LOCAL_SEND_FAILED/OUTCOME_UNKNOWN. Periodically, and after each reconnect, send operation.get for non-terminal IDs and apply the returned target stage. When expires_ms + ack expiry has passed without ACCEPTED, mark EXPIRED (never sent) or OUTCOME_UNKNOWN.
- **Verifikasi:** Confirmed. The client.offer call at ground.py:370 has no on_error. No code compares expires_ms, and nothing sends or reconciles operation.get: an operation.get ack only updates its own journal record, and the target stays as it was. Acks are applied only when request sid matches (:236). I re-ran R4: service.restart is still REQUESTED an hour later, and an unacked shutdown.execute leaves pending_shutdowns true. MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md:101 says Ground must store IDs and re-query after a missed ACK, which the built-in Ground does not do. This is especially likely for ppp.restart, whose ack can be lost when the PPP link drops (Client._run clears the outbox on disconnect).

#### GRD-9 · MEDIUM · confirmed · data-correctness
**Old shutdown records are re-stamped on every sid change, hijack 'Operasi terakhir', and the Ground 'shutdown uncertain' state can never be cleared**  
`src/rdf_node/ground.py:122`

- **Masalah:** On every sid change (Edge agent restart, Ground restart) the state handler calls journal.update(...,'OUTCOME_UNKNOWN',...) for every pending shutdown. This includes records that are already OUTCOME_UNKNOWN from weeks ago, and update() bumps updated_ms. Those old shutdowns jump to the top of journal.latest(1), so snapshot.last_operation (shown in 'Operasi terakhir') becomes an old system.shutdown.execute. ground.js:610 rememberShutdownState() then sets the browser's shutdown-uncertain flag again. Also, journal.pending_shutdowns() counts OUTCOME_UNKNOWN as pending (journal.py:59-64), Ground has no reconcile path, and ground.py:221 refuses any later ack for it. After the first-ever Ground shutdown, /api/v2/operations/pending-shutdowns is true forever and every later shutdown shows the 'Hasil shutdown sebelumnya belum pasti' warning. The UI says 'jalankan sudo rdf-node controls shutdown-reconcile pada Pi', but that only clears the Edge helper intent, not Ground's journal.
- **Dampak:** The operator sees misleading 'last operation' entries, and the shutdown warning becomes permanent noise that trains operators to click through it.
- **Bukti:** repro_ground.py (R2b): last_operation before the Edge agent restart is stream.set. After a state message with a new sid it is 'system.shutdown.execute OUTCOME_UNKNOWN', and pending_shutdowns is still True. Live: after a UI shutdown, a second Shutdown click showed the uncertain-shutdown confirm dialog (flow.js f5).
- **Saran perbaikan:** Update only records whose stage is not already OUTCOME_UNKNOWN, and do not bump updated_ms for unchanged stages. Provide a Ground-side acknowledge/reconcile action (or reconcile when Edge reports no active shutdown intent). Make the UI guidance match what actually clears the Ground flag.
- **Verifikasi:** Confirmed. pending_shutdowns() (journal.py:59-64) includes OUTCOME_UNKNOWN records. ground.py:122-123 calls update() on all of them on every sid change and on the first state after a Ground restart, which bumps updated_ms. latest(1) (ORDER BY updated DESC) then returns the old shutdown as last_operation. Re-running R2b showed stream.set replaced by 'system.shutdown.execute OUTCOME_UNKNOWN'. A normal successful Ground shutdown always ends this way (SHUTDOWN_SCHEDULED, then a new sid, then OUTCOME_UNKNOWN), and Ground has no prune and no reconcile. Keeping unknown shutdowns pending appears intentional (an Edge-side test asserts it). Still, the re-stamping and last_operation hijack are real …

#### GRD-10 · MEDIUM · confirmed · ux
**LIVE angular curve is pushed toward STALE by Ground's 10 s threshold against Edge's ~9 s Bulk pacing**  
`src/rdf_node/ground.py:280`

- **Masalah:** angular_view() flags SOURCE_STALE once source age > 10000 ms. With the default balanced profile and bulk_budget_bytes_s=350, Edge paces a ~2.9 KB frame every max(4, (len+topic+160)/350) ≈ 8.98 s (agent.py:488-490; docs say ~8.96 s). FLAG_FRESH frames may already be up to 2.5 s old at publish, and a ~3 KB QoS0 PUBLISH needs about 1.6 s of serialization on a ~15 kbit/s T900. So the displayed frame regularly exceeds 10 s before the next one arrives, and the plot greys out and reads 'STALE' for part of each cycle. Any payload above ~3.3 KB (values with more digits) makes the pacing period alone exceed 10 s, so every frame goes stale before replacement. graph_u8 has the same Bulk-limited pacing.
- **Dampak:** In normal field operation the LIVE spectrum flickers between RECEIVED and STALE, which undermines trust in the indicator.
- **Bukti:** Live harness (VERIFIED=1, loopback, no radio latency): 40 s sampling of /api/v2/angular/latest showed frame intervals of 8.98-8.99 s and a max source_age_ms of 9247, only ~750 ms below the stale limit. Arithmetic for T900 latency and the 2.5 s freshness allowance pushes past 10 s.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Derive the LIVE angular stale threshold from the active profile and pacing (e.g. max(10 s, 2 × expected period + allowed source age)), possibly published in capabilities or state, or make it configurable on Ground.
- **Verifikasi:** Confirmed analytically. Pacing is (len+topic+160)/350, about 8.97 s for the documented 2,946 B frame (agent.py:488-490; MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md:141). Just before the next frame arrives, the displayed frame's age is period + next-frame latency + source age at publish (up to 2.5 s allowed by FLAG_FRESH). angular_view flags SOURCE_STALE above 10000 ms (ground.py:280). Any latency plus source age above ~1 s therefore produces STALE for part of each cycle. On the documented 15 kbit/s link, serializing a ~3 KB PUBLISH alone takes ~1.6 s, so the flicker is expected in the field. The auditor's loopback measurement (max 9247 ms) is consistent with this. Not verified on a real radio link.

#### GRD-11 · MEDIUM · confirmed · contract-mismatch
**Ground rejects telemetry/diagnostic/doa when Edge validation reasons contain raw parser messages**  
`src/rdf_node/ground.py:204`

- **Masalah:** Ground requires every validation reason to match [A-Z0-9_:-]{1,64}, otherwise it raises BAD_DIAGNOSTIC_DOA. Edge builds the reasons from source.view(), where self.error=str(e)[:64] for any ValueError (source.py:194), and diagnostic_view() just upper-cases them (source.py:258). Python messages such as "could not convert string to float: ''" (an empty or garbled numeric CSV field), "invalid literal for int()...", or UnicodeDecodeError text contain spaces and quotes. So when DOA_value.html is malformed in these ways, which is exactly when the XML diagnostic path is needed, Ground drops every diagnostic DoA message, and the panel shows nothing or an aging stale sample.
- **Dampak:** The diagnostic DoA channel goes silent on Ground during common source-format failures.
- **Bukti:** repro_ground.py (R3): a CSV row with an empty latitude field gives Edge reasons ['DIAGNOSTIC_UNVERIFIED', "COULD NOT CONVERT STRING TO FLOAT: ''", 'NO_RECORD'], and Ground.receive raises 'BAD_DIAGNOSTIC_DOA'.
- **Saran perbaikan:** On Edge, map parser exceptions to stable codes (e.g. 'CSV_NUMBER_INVALID', 'CSV_UTF8_INVALID') before they enter reasons. On Ground, sanitize or drop non-conforming reasons instead of rejecting the whole message.
- **Verifikasi:** Confirmed. source.py:194 stores str(e)[:64] for any ValueError (float('')/int() messages; UnicodeDecodeError is also a ValueError). view() adds self.error to reasons (source.py:222), and diagnostic_view upper-cases it (:258). Ground's regex [A-Z0-9_:-]{1,64} (ground.py:204) then rejects the whole message as BAD_DIAGNOSTIC_DOA. Re-running R3 showed "COULD NOT CONVERT STRING TO FLOAT: ''" being rejected. The regular DoA path is also suppressed in this state (reasons are not UNVERIFIED-only), so Ground gets no DoA at all. Requires a malformed CSV field, so medium (edge case).

#### GRD-12 · MEDIUM · confirmed · ux
**Expired or lost Ground login session is never detected; the UI keeps showing 'GROUND LOGIN AKTIF' and enabled buttons**  
`web/ground.js:473`

- **Masalah:** csrf is read once at page load (line 656) or at login (line 473) and never re-checked. The session idles out after api.session_seconds (600 s) because snapshot and GET polling do not refresh it (api.py:96-101 refreshes only on _admin_session calls), and it is lost whenever rdf-ground restarts (sessions live in memory). After either, every action returns 403 AUTHENTICATION_AND_CSRF_REQUIRED, but the auth text and button availability still say ready. Combined with GRD-5, the 403 text disappears within a second, so a legitimate operator click appears to do nothing.
- **Dampak:** After idle time or a Ground service restart, operator actions silently fail without any prompt to log in again.
- **Bukti:** outage.js: after ctx.clearCookies() (equivalent to an expired or reset server session) the UI still showed 'GROUND LOGIN AKTIF ...' and all buttons enabled. Clicking Restart RDF gave POST /api/v2/commands 403 {"error":"AUTHENTICATION_AND_CSRF_REQUIRED"}, and #operation then reverted to the previous stream.set APPLIED.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Poll /api/v2/session (or check authenticated on each snapshot poll). On 401/403 from a POST, clear csrf, reset authstate to logged-out, and show a persistent 're-login required' message.
- **Verifikasi:** Confirmed. Auth.session slides expiry only when _admin_session runs (api.py:96-101): GET /api/v2/session, which ground.js calls only at load (:656), and POSTs. Polling uses unauthenticated GETs, so the session and the cookie (Max-Age 600) expire after 10 minutes without a POST. Sessions are also in memory and lost on an rdf-ground restart. ground.js never re-checks; csrf stays set and mutationBlockReason reports ready. The resulting 403 text is overwritten within ~1 s (GRD-5). This is a functional UX failure, not a security point.

#### GRD-8 · LOW · confirmed · data-correctness
**Ground restart marks an in-progress PPP restart OUTCOME_UNKNOWN 'PPP_RESTART_SESSION_CHANGED' although the Edge session did not change**  
`src/rdf_node/ground.py:121`

- **Masalah:** The session-change reconciliation runs whenever self.node_state.get('sid')!=sid. After a Ground process restart node_state is {}, so the first (retained) state message for the unchanged Edge session triggers it. Every PPP_RESTART_REQUESTED operation becomes OUTCOME_UNKNOWN with reason PPP_RESTART_SESSION_CHANGED (lines 124-126), and every pending shutdown is rewritten as well. Fresh same-session health can no longer move the PPP operation to APPLIED, and the next PPP restart requires the 'confirm previous unknown' path. This contradicts docs/PROTOCOL.md:239-240: APPLIED after newer health 'pada sesi node yang sama'.
- **Dampak:** Restarting rdf-ground or rebooting the Ubuntu host during a PPP recovery records a wrong reason and forces extra operator confirmations.
- **Bukti:** repro_ground.py (R2): before the Ground restart the stage is PPP_RESTART_REQUESTED. A new Ground instance on the same journal then receives retained state with the same sid, and the stage becomes OUTCOME_UNKNOWN with reason PPP_RESTART_SESSION_CHANGED. Fresh same-session health leaves it at OUTCOME_UNKNOWN.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Persist the last seen (sid, boot) in the Ground journal kv. Reconcile only when a known previous sid actually differs, not when node_state is empty after start.
- **Verifikasi:** Confirmed. After a Ground restart node_state is {}, so the retained state for the same sid passes the check at ground.py:121, and every pending PPP restart becomes OUTCOME_UNKNOWN with PPP_RESTART_SESSION_CHANGED. Re-running R2 reproduced this, and later same-session health cannot move it to APPLIED. The code could compare each op's request['sid'] with the new sid but does not. The existing test test_session_change_keeps_ppp_restart_unknown covers only a real sid change. Downgraded to low: the window is narrow (Ground restart between the PPP_RESTART_REQUESTED ack and the next fresh health). The cost is a wrong reason string plus one extra confirmation on the next PPP restart.

#### GRD-13 · LOW · confirmed · ux
**Lifecycle and host actions are blocked in the UI whenever the node's config revision is unknown, although Edge accepts them**  
`web/ground.js:22`

- **Masalah:** mutationBlockReason requires Number.isSafeInteger(snapshot.config.sdr_revision) for Start/Stop/Restart/Reboot/Shutdown. state.cfg is null whenever settings.json has not been read successfully since the Edge agent started (missing or corrupt settings, engine never ran). Edge would accept these commands (control.py:87 compares base_rev None == revision None), and Ground fills base_rev itself (ground.py:368). The UI therefore blocks exactly the recovery actions (Start RDF, Reboot Pi) that an operator needs when the SDR side is broken, showing 'Revision config node belum tersedia.'
- **Dampak:** Remote recovery of a node with a broken or missing SDR settings file is impossible from the Ground UI.
- **Bukti:** Code trace: ground.js:22, control.py:87, ground.py:368. Source.revision stays None until the first successful settings.json read (source.py:124,144).
- **Saran perbaikan:** Require sdr_revision only for config.patch. Allow lifecycle and host actions with a null revision, since the backend already supplies state.cfg.
- **Verifikasi:** Confirmed. ground.js:22 requires Number.isSafeInteger(sdr_revision) for every lifecycle and host action. Source.revision stays None until settings.json is first read successfully (source.py:124,140-147), or permanently when share_dir is not configured. Edge accepts base_rev None == revision None (control.py:87), and Ground fills base_rev itself (ground.py:368). The Edge panel (app.js:732) sends null without blocking. Only affects a node whose settings file is missing or broken, so low.

#### GRD-14 · LOW · confirmed · ux
**Ground keeps all API-dependent buttons enabled and labelled 'siap' while the Ground API is unreachable**  
`web/ground.js:635`

- **Masalah:** apiSnapshotAvailable is set to true on the first successful poll (line 606) and never reset. The poll error path (lines 635-642) refreshes the button states using the last cached snapshot, so mutationBlockReason, updateSettingsButton and pppReadiness keep reporting 'Aksi lifecycle/host di atas siap', 'Settings siap', 'Restart siap', with every button enabled. Only the header pill and banner show GROUND API UNAVAILABLE.
- **Dampak:** Mixed signals during an outage, and clicks fail with network errors (feeding GRD-3 for PPP).
- **Bukti:** outage.js: with all /api/** requests aborted for 4 s, the pill showed 'GROUND API UNAVAILABLE', yet avail/ppp/settings texts still said ready and start/restart/reboot/shutdown/ppp-restart/settings-apply were all enabled.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Set apiSnapshotAvailable=false in the poll catch block before calling the update*Button functions.
- **Verifikasi:** Confirmed. apiSnapshotAvailable is set true at ground.js:606 and never reset. The catch at :635-642 calls the update functions using the cached snapshot, so buttons stay enabled and the availability texts still say ready while the pill shows GROUND API UNAVAILABLE. Low.

#### GRD-15 · LOW · confirmed · doc-mismatch
**health/detail and availability are subscribed but discarded; rejection reasons and counters are invisible to the operator**  
`src/rdf_node/ground.py:212`

- **Masalah:** telemetry/health/detail is stored in self.detail but never exposed by snapshot() or any API. That data covers USB count, DAQ sync, CPU, mem, disk_free, throttled, undervoltage, PPP tx/rx and parse/abort counters. The availability topic (including Edge's Last Will) falls through receive() with no handler. MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md:80 and 86 describe Ground using detail 'untuk diagnosis host' and showing availability. Separately, every rejected message only increments self.rejected, with no reason kept, and ground.js never shows link.rejected. So when, for example, the Ground clock is >1 s behind the Edge (health must satisfy -1000 <= now-t, ground.py:61), all health is rejected and the UI just says HEALTH STALE with no hint why.
- **Dampak:** Ground operators cannot see Pi undervoltage, throttling, low disk, link rate or connection loss, and cannot diagnose why telemetry is being dropped.
- **Bukti:** grep: self.detail is only assigned (ground.py:29,212). There is no 'availability' branch in receive(). ground.js has no reference to link.rejected or detail.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Expose detail (with age) and availability in snapshot and render them as tiles. Keep a small last-N rejection reason ring (code + topic + time) in snapshot and display it in the raw/diagnostic section.
- **Verifikasi:** Confirmed. self.detail is only assigned (ground.py:29,212) and is never included in snapshot() or any endpoint. receive() has no availability branch, so the Edge LWT (agent.py:123) is dropped silently. MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md:80/86 describe Ground using detail for host diagnosis and showing availability. The 'no rejection reason' part is documented as a limitation (MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md:563-564), so only the detail/availability gap counts. Low.

#### GRD-16 · LOW · confirmed · ux
**Settings form stops reflecting node values after a failed or adjusted apply; the raw settings JSON is not labelled with its revision**  
`web/ground.js:280`

- **Masalah:** Once the operator types in the form, settingsDirty stays true until the reported safe settings exactly equal settingsTarget (lines 280-284). After a 4xx rejection settingsTarget is reset to {} (line 446), so the condition can never hold. After an Edge FAILED, CONFLICT or engine-adjusted value, the target never matches. In both cases the form keeps the operator's typed values indefinitely, even after Refresh config, until the page is reloaded, and there is no reset button. Also, after a successful patch the revision changes and safe_settings becomes {}, while the 'Requested native settings (raw JSON)' block keeps showing the old report (e.g. uniform_gain 15.7 after gain was set to 20.7) with no revision or age indicator.
- **Dampak:** Operators can mistake typed or old values for the node's current settings.
- **Bukti:** Live flow (flow.js f1): after PERSISTED_UNVERIFIED revision 2, settings-reported still showed '"uniform_gain":15.7' while the node was at revision 2. Code trace of the settingsDirty, settingsTarget and renderSettings early return.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Clear settingsDirty and settingsTarget when an apply reaches a terminal stage, and add a 'Reset to reported' control. Show settings_revision vs sdr_revision and the report time next to the raw JSON, flagging it 'OUTDATED' when they differ.
- **Verifikasi:** Confirmed. After a 4xx, settingsTarget is reset to {} (ground.js:446) while settingsDirty stays true. The reset condition at :280 needs target.length, so renderSettings returns early at :284 forever, including after Refresh config. After a successful patch, config_view returns safe={} because the revision differs (ground.py:315) while settings_json still holds the old report, and #settings-reported shows it with no revision label. Keeping the operator's input after a rejection is arguably intended, but never re-syncing is a minor UX defect. Low.

#### GRD-17 · LOW · confirmed · ux
**Profile selector is not synced with the node profile, is never gated, and still labels a profile 'GRAPH U8'**  
`web/ground.html:28`

- **Masalah:** The <select id=profile> always starts at BALANCED and is never set from snapshot.node_state.profile, so it can disagree with the 'Profil' tile. The Set profil and Refresh config buttons are excluded from updateActionButtons, so they stay enabled when logged out, when health is stale, or when MQTT is down, and only fail after the click (with the message then overwritten, GRD-5). The option label 'GRAPH U8' suggests U8 quantization, which no longer exists (PROTOCOL.md:83, 212: JSON only, no Q16/U8).
- **Dampak:** Minor confusion about the active profile and actions that look available but fail.
- **Bukti:** ground.html:28, ground.js:474 and 539. Live: before login, refresh and setprofile were enabled while all other actions were disabled (shot.js output).
- **Saran perbaikan:** Initialize and refresh the select from node_state.profile when it is not being edited. Gate setprofile and refresh with the same readiness logic. Relabel to e.g. 'GRAPH (2 s)'.
- **Verifikasi:** Confirmed. ground.html:28 hard-codes the select options, and ground.js never sets #profile from node_state.profile. updateActionButtons (:27) covers only start/stop/restart/reboot, so refresh and setprofile are never disabled. The 'GRAPH U8' label mirrors the profile key graph_u8 (config.py:16), whose wire format is plain JSON (PROTOCOL.md). Cosmetic or minor, so low.

#### GRD-18 · LOW · confirmed · performance
**Ground journal is never pruned, and every health message triggers full-table scans**  
`src/rdf_node/ground.py:66`

- **Masalah:** Ground never calls journal.prune() (only agent.py does), so ground.sqlite3 grows without bound, one row per command including every config.get and stream.set. On every telemetry/health message (1 Hz), receive() runs journal.latest() (21 queries) and _reconcile_ppp_restart_health(), which calls pending_ppp_restarts(): SELECT of all rows plus JSON-decoding every request and result. pending_shutdowns() also scans all rows on every pending-shutdowns API call. Per-message CPU therefore grows linearly with the age of the installation.
- **Dampak:** Gradual CPU and latency growth on long-running Ground installs. Low risk at today's volumes.
- **Bukti:** grep: no prune call in ground.py. journal.py:65-75 loads and parses every row. ground.py:161 and 164 run per health message.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Call journal.prune() periodically from Ground._loop. Index or filter by op in SQL (store op as a column) instead of decoding every row.
- **Verifikasi:** Confirmed. prune() is called only from agent.py:235, never from Ground. Each health message runs journal.latest() (1+20 queries, ground.py:161) and pending_ppp_restarts(), which SELECTs every row and JSON-decodes them (journal.py:65-75). Ground also ignores the documented 2,000/30-day retention (CONTROL.md). At realistic command volumes this stays small, so low.

#### GRD-19 · LOW · refuted · test-gap
**No automated coverage for the Ground preview JS and for lost-ACK/expiry paths**  
`tests/test_system.py:600`

- **Masalah:** ground.js is only syntax-checked (check-package.sh). No test exercises: diagnostic angular rendering; frequency conversion; PPP localStorage reconciliation against a 404 result; error persistence vs polling; session expiry handling; or Ground behaviour when a command or settings report gets no reply (settings_request_id lifetime, non-terminal operations). GroundCommandTests covers only the happy and rejection paths with immediate acks. These gaps match the defects in GRD-1 to GRD-5, GRD-7 and GRD-12.
- **Dampak:** Regressions in operator-facing Ground flows go unnoticed.
- **Bukti:** Reviewed tests/test_system.py GroundCommandTests and EndToEndTests. No browser-level tests exist in tests/.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Add unit tests for settings_request_id expiry and unacked-command reconciliation. Add a small headless-browser or jsdom smoke test for ground.js covering the GRD-1/3/4/5 scenarios.
- **Verifikasi:** This is a meta-finding about missing tests, not a functional defect. It restates GRD-1/2/3/4/5/7/12, which are already reported on their own. The statement is accurate: there are no JS tests, and GroundCommandTests uses immediate acks. But it describes no new wrong behavior, and the audit rules exclude generic advice.

### Command, journal, helper

_The command pipeline (control.py CommandManager, the SQLite journal, the root helper, plus the Ground-side command/ack handling) works on the happy path: there is one worker, dedup by id, durable journaling before side effects, and a prepare/challenge/execute flow for reboot and shutdown. The main problems are around reconciliation and recovery after failures. Ground never expires or re-queries lost commands and ACKs, and a pending config.get can stay stuck forever. Shutdown uncertainty is permanent after the first shutdown. When the helper is unreachable, the result is classified as OUTCOME_UNKNOWN instead of FAILED. The retained capabilities never refresh after helper readiness changes. Clock-trust and single-worker gating block local recovery actions (shutdown, reboot, PPP restart) while the UI shows them as available. The Edge panel never shows why an operation failed. Several docs describe statuses, a recovery path and a local PPP-restart button that do not exist in the code._

#### CMD-2 · HIGH · confirmed · bug
**Ground config.get becomes permanently stuck in SETTINGS_REQUEST_PENDING after one lost or late settings report**  
`src/rdf_node/ground.py:342`

- **Masalah:** submit_command sets self.settings_request_id when config.get is queued (ground.py:374) and rejects every later config.get with SETTINGS_REQUEST_PENDING (ground.py:342-343). The id is cleared in only three cases: an accepted settings/reported (152), an ack/config with a terminal non-APPLIED status (241-243), or a node session change (132). In the normal success path, Edge first sends ack/config APPLIED, which does not clear it, and then publishes settings/reported separately. If that report is lost (link drop clears the Edge outbox), arrives after the 30 s freshness window (`_fresh(j['t'],30000)` raises), or the Ground command itself is dropped from its outbox (offer() has no on_error), nothing ever clears the id. There is no timeout.
- **Dampak:** The Ground 'Refresh config' button is dead until the Ground service restarts or the Edge agent restarts (new sid). The operator cannot see current RF settings, and the settings form cannot be repopulated from the node. Lossy T900 links make this easy to trigger.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced: scratchpad/audit/repro_ground_settings_stuck.py. First config.get REQUESTED, ack/config APPLIED delivered, late report rejected with SOURCE_TIMESTAMP_NOT_FRESH. Three later retries all return {'stage':'REJECTED','error':'SETTINGS_REQUEST_PENDING'}, and the journal shows the original as APPLIED.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Expire settings_request_id after the request's expires_ms plus the report expiry (~30-45 s). Also clear it in the ground loop when the corresponding ack/config is APPLIED but no report arrives within that window, and surface the timeout in the UI.
- **Verifikasi:** ground.py:342-343 rejects config.get while settings_request_id is set. The id is cleared only at :152 (accepted report), :241-243 (terminal non-APPLIED ack/config) and :132 (new sid). There is no timeout, and the Ground offer at :370 has no on_error. My own repro (verify_cmd/v_cmd2.py) uses a simpler trigger: Refresh is pressed while the node is offline. That is allowed because config.get skips the health gate (ground.py:325), and the Refresh button is never disabled (ground.js:474; updateActionButtons excludes it). The request is REQUESTED and is never answered. After the node returns with the same sid, every retry gets SETTINGS_REQUEST_PENDING and the row stays REQUESTED. Only a Ground …

#### CMD-1 · MEDIUM · confirmed · bug
**Retained capabilities are published once per MQTT connection and never refreshed when helper readiness changes**  
`src/rdf_node/agent.py:360`

- **Masalah:** capabilities() reads helper_status (helper_available, settings_target_available, lifecycle_target_available), and the collector refreshes helper_status every 20 polls (~5 s, agent.py:231-233). The retained `capabilities` message is only offered inside `if ctrl.generation!=self._generation` (agent.py:356-360), i.e. once per MQTT (re)connect. Trigger: the helper is not yet listening when Control connects. This can happen at boot, because rdf-edge.service has no After=rdf-control-helper.service. It can also happen after `controls approve`, which restarts both units at once (cli.py:174), or when a status RPC times out because the single-threaded helper is busy. Ground then keeps remote_reboot/remote_shutdown/remote_ppp_restart/remote_config_patch/remote_processing=false until the Edge MQTT session reconnects. The reverse also happens: if the helper dies later, Ground keeps showing actions as available, and they end up as OUTCOME_UNKNOWN (see CMD-4).
- **Dampak:** Ground operators may be unable to reboot, shut down, restart PPP, change settings or start/stop the SDR for an unbounded time after a normal boot or approval, and get no hint that the cause is a stale capability. Or they are offered actions the Edge can no longer perform.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced: scratchpad/audit/repro_caps_stale.py (broker fixture plus a fake helper socket that starts after connect). Retained caps at connect: helper_available=False, remote_reboot=False, remote_config_patch=False. 7 s later agent.capabilities() reports True/True/True but the retained message is still False. 'capabilities publishes: 1'. ground.js:16-23 mutationBlockReason disables the buttons from these fields ('Helper Edge belum tersedia untuk aksi host.').
- **Saran perbaikan:** Re-offer the retained `capabilities` whenever the capabilities dict changes (compare against the last published value in _schedule), not only on generation change. Also add After=/Wants=rdf-control-helper.service to rdf-edge.service.
- **Verifikasi:** agent.py:356-360 offers the retained capabilities only when ctrl.generation changes. capabilities() (agent.py:296-319) derives remote_* from helper_status, which _collect refreshes only every 20 polls (agent.py:231-233, poll_ms 250, so about 5 s) and sets to {} on any status RPC failure. I re-ran repro_caps_stale.py: the retained message says helper_available, remote_reboot and remote_config_patch are False, live capabilities() says True after 7 s, and there was exactly 1 publish. ground.js:16-23 disables actions from these fields. The race is real: cli.py:174 restarts the helper and edge units in parallel, and rdf-edge.service has no ordering on the helper. I lowered it to medium. At cold …

#### CMD-3 · MEDIUM · confirmed · data-correctness
**After any successful shutdown, 'previous shutdown uncertain' is permanent on both Edge and Ground; shutdown-reconcile cannot clear it**  
`src/rdf_node/journal.py:59`

- **Masalah:** pending_shutdowns() returns every system.shutdown.execute row whose stage is not FAILED/REJECTED/EXPIRED/CONFLICT/CANCELLED. On Edge, a completed shutdown (SHUTDOWN_SCHEDULED) is converted by recover() at the next power-on to OUTCOME_UNKNOWN 'AGENT_RESTARTED_RECONCILE_NO_REPLAY' (journal.py:85-86). On Ground, a session change sets it to OUTCOME_UNKNOWN (ground.py:122-123). prune() never deletes OUTCOME_UNKNOWN (journal.py:91). `rdf-node controls shutdown-reconcile` (cli.py:176-182) only clears the helper's intent file and never touches either journal. So /api/v2/operations/pending-shutdowns returns pending:true forever after the first shutdown. Both UIs then show the uncertainty warning on every later shutdown, with two extra native confirm() dialogs. The warning tells the operator to run shutdown-reconcile, which does not help.
- **Dampak:** The safety signal is always on, so operators learn to ignore it, which defeats its purpose. Every normal shutdown after the first one needs extra confirmations on the 480x320 kiosk, and the documented remedy does not work.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced: scratchpad/audit/repro_shutdown_pending.py. prepare APPLIED, execute SHUTDOWN_SCHEDULED, restart with a new boot id gives OUTCOME_UNKNOWN. pending_shutdowns stays True after helper._reconcile_shutdown() returned {'cleared':True,'reason':'NEW_BOOT'}, and still True after prune(max_rows=0,days=0). UI text: app.js:356-360 and ground.js:497,512,527.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Give pending shutdowns a terminal resolution. On Edge recover(), treat SHUTDOWN_SCHEDULED with a changed boot id as resolved (for example APPLIED with proof OS_BOOT_ID_CHANGED, or a distinct RESOLVED stage). Have shutdown-reconcile (or an authenticated API action) mark the journal rows resolved, and exclude resolved rows from pending_shutdowns().
- **Verifikasi:** I verified this with verify_cmd/v_cmd3.py. After SHUTDOWN_SCHEDULED, recover() with a new boot id rewrites the row to OUTCOME_UNKNOWN (journal.py:85-86). pending_shutdowns() counts OUTCOME_UNKNOWN as pending (journal.py:60-64), and prune(0,0) keeps it (journal.py:91). Reboot gets an OS_BOOT_ID_CHANGED resolution (journal.py:83-84), but shutdown has no equivalent. Ground does the same at ground.py:122-123. helper._reconcile_shutdown (helper.py:171-202) only deletes the intent file and never touches either journal. So /api/v2/operations/pending-shutdowns (api.py:140-141) returns true permanently. Both UIs then require the extra confirm() dialogs on every later shutdown (app.js:865,874; …

#### CMD-5 · MEDIUM · confirmed · contract-mismatch
**No working recovery for lost ACKs: Ground never re-queries, operation.get does not update the target, REQUESTED/ACCEPTED never expire**  
`src/rdf_node/ground.py:236`

- **Masalah:** The docs say Ground stores operation IDs and re-queries with operation.get when an ACK is missed (MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md:98,101; PROTOCOL.md:235 'Hasil tersedia lewat jurnal setelah reconnect'). In code: (1) ground.js and ground.py never issue operation.get automatically. (2) If issued manually, the ack/operation handler (ground.py:236-240) only updates the operation.get record itself; the embedded `result.operation` is never applied to the target row. (3) Ground commands are offered without on_error (ground.py:370), so a command dropped from the outbox leaves the row in REQUESTED. (4) REJECTED acks carrying a new sid are ignored because of the sid check. (5) On session change only shutdown and PPP rows are reconciled (ground.py:122-126). config.patch, processing.set, reboot and stream.set rows can stay REQUESTED/ACCEPTED forever. Separately, rejected commands are never journaled on Edge (control.py:118-122), so operation.get for them returns null.
- **Dampak:** After a link flap, Ground UIs show stale in-progress states indefinitely: settings proof panel, reboot wait, last_operation. The documented recovery path does not exist, so the operator has no way to learn the real outcome from Ground.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced: scratchpad/audit/repro_ground_opget.py (Edge and Ground wired directly). stream.set: Ground receives ACCEPTED, the APPLIED ack is dropped, Ground stage stays ACCEPTED. A manual operation.get returns APPLIED with {'operation':{...'stage':'APPLIED'}}, yet the Ground target stage is still 'ACCEPTED'.
- **Saran perbaikan:** When an operation.get ack arrives, apply result.operation (stage and result) to the matching target row, using the same terminal and monotonic rules as for normal ACKs. Periodically issue operation.get for non-terminal rows after reconnect. Mark REQUESTED rows EXPIRED once expires_ms has passed without any ACK.
- **Verifikasi:** I verified this with verify_cmd/v_cmd5.py. When an operation.get ack arrives carrying result.operation.stage=APPLIED, ground.py:213-240 updates only the operation.get row (lookup by j['id']), and the target row stays ACCEPTED. Neither ground.js nor ground.py ever issues operation.get (grep finds none). Ground commands are offered without on_error (ground.py:370-371), and the MQTT client clears its outbox on disconnect (mqtt.py:262-266), so REQUESTED rows can be orphaned. Session-change reconciliation covers only shutdown and PPP (ground.py:122-126). The docs (MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md:98,101) describe re-query on a missed ACK as the recovery path, and the bundled Ground cannot actually use it.

#### CMD-7 · MEDIUM · confirmed · ux
**A lifecycle start/restart that never verifies holds the single worker for up to 180 s, blocking reboot, shutdown and PPP restart (BUSY)**  
`src/rdf_node/control.py:195`

- **Masalah:** processing.set RUNNING and service.restart wait up to control.start_timeout_seconds (default 180 s, max 600) for an ACTIVE unit plus a fresh, healthy DAQ. Throughout that time busy=True, so every other mutation from Ground or the panel gets REJECTED 'BUSY' (control.py:112). There is no cancel operation. The snapshot also adds CONTROL_IN_PROGRESS to detection reasons (agent.py:243), so DoA stays invalid and bulk angular is paused for the whole window. The typical failure case (SDR hardware not producing healthy DAQ after Start/Restart) is exactly when the operator wants to reboot.
- **Dampak:** A failed SDR start locks out host recovery actions for up to 3 minutes (up to 10 with the max config), with only a bare 'BUSY' message.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced: scratchpad/audit/repro_busy.py (start_timeout_seconds reduced to 6 s, unit ACTIVE, no new status). During verification detection.valid=False with reason CONTROL_IN_PROGRESS, and reboot.prepare, shutdown.prepare, ppp.restart and stream.set all return {'error':'BUSY'}. The final result after 6.0 s is OUTCOME_UNKNOWN LIFECYCLE_VERIFICATION_TIMEOUT.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Release the worker after the helper call and run lifecycle verification asynchronously, or let host-level ops (reboot, shutdown, ppp.restart) pre-empt or cancel a pending verification (stage OUTCOME_UNKNOWN or CANCELLED). Do not mark detection invalid for lifecycle verification beyond the actual stop/start transition.
- **Verifikasi:** control.py:195-205 loops for up to start_timeout_seconds (default 180, config.yaml:55, max 600) while busy=True, and submit() returns BUSY for every other mutation (control.py:111-112). There is no cancel, and the docs say nothing about BUSY or this window. When an SDR start never reaches healthy DAQ, Stop, Reboot, Shutdown and Ground PPP restart are all locked out for 3 minutes. One part of the claim is weak: the CONTROL_IN_PROGRESS invalidation (source.py:235). In the failure case DAQ is already unhealthy, and in the success case the loop exits as soon as DAQ turns healthy, so that flag adds almost nothing. The lockout alone justifies medium.

#### CMD-8 · MEDIUM · confirmed · ux
**Edge panel never shows why an operation failed: only the op name and stage chip are rendered**  
`web/app.js:253`

- **Masalah:** Asynchronous outcomes (FAILED, OUTCOME_UNKNOWN, PERSISTED_UNVERIFIED) are only shown through renderOperation(), which writes op.op and op.stage into the CMD line and toasts `${op.op}: ${op.stage}`. The snapshot's last_operation.result contains the error or reason (for example LIFECYCLE_TARGET_NOT_READY, CONFIG_CHANGED_BEFORE_WRITE, INTEGER_OUT_OF_RANGE for an out-of-policy frequency, REBOOT_CHALLENGE_INVALID when the 30 s challenge lapsed, SHUTDOWN_ALREADY_SCHEDULED, NATIVE_RUNTIME_EVIDENCE_INCOMPLETE, LIFECYCLE_VERIFICATION_TIMEOUT), but it is never displayed. Only synchronous REJECTED responses reach #modalmsg.
- **Dampak:** Touchscreen operators see 'config.patch FAILED' or 'processing.set OUTCOME_UNKNOWN' with no way to diagnose on the device. They cannot tell a policy limit from a writer conflict, a missing target or a timeout.
- **Bukti:** Code trace: app.js:253-262 (renderOperation) uses only op.op and op.stage. The CMD line in index.html:19 has only #command and #cmdstage. No other code in app.js reads last_operation.result. The backend includes it (agent.py:286, CommandManager.public).
- **Saran perbaikan:** Show result.error or result.reason (and the proof state for config.patch) next to the stage chip and in the toast, using short Indonesian labels per error code.
- **Verifikasi:** renderOperation (app.js:253-262) renders only op.op and op.stage and toasts `${op.op}: ${op.stage}`. The CMD line (index.html:19) has only #command and #cmdstage. Nothing in app.js reads last_operation.result. Only the synchronous REJECTED path and the shutdown/reboot prepare flows show result.error (app.js:355,868,872). Asynchronous FAILED, OUTCOME_UNKNOWN and PERSISTED_UNVERIFIED outcomes for config.patch, processing.set and restart therefore show no reason on the touchscreen.

The CLI approval prompt (cli.py:166) and the docs (CONTROL.md:33-36, OPERATIONS.md:109-111, README.md:338-339) all say --ppp-restart enables the Edge panel action. #### CMD-11 · MEDIUM · confirmed · robustness
**Helper crash-loops at startup if reconciling the saved engine intent fails, removing all control**  
`src/rdf_node/helper.py:370`

- **Masalah:** serve() calls c.reconcile_intent() before binding the Unix socket, without any try/except. reconcile_intent() runs `systemctl --no-block start|stop <engine>` through _run(), which raises HelperError('SYSTEMD_ACTION_FAILED') on a non-zero exit and lets subprocess.TimeoutExpired propagate. If the audited engine unit was later removed or renamed (for example after an SDR reinstall), is masked, or systemctl is slow at boot, the helper exits. Restart=on-failure restarts it into the same failure, and the socket is never created.
- **Dampak:** Reboot, shutdown, PPP restart, settings and lifecycle become unavailable from both Ground and the panel (commands end OUTCOME_UNKNOWN, see CMD-4), with no clear indication in the UI.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced: scratchpad/audit/repro_helper_startup.py. With intent.json desired RUNNING and systemctl returning exit 5, reconcile_intent raised HelperError SYSTEMD_ACTION_FAILED. With a systemctl timeout it raised TimeoutExpired. serve() at helper.py:369-371 has no handler.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Wrap reconcile_intent() in try/except, log the failure, report it via state_info (e.g. intent_reconcile_error), and continue to bind the socket.
- **Verifikasi:** helper.py:369-370 calls c.reconcile_intent() before binding the socket, with no handler. reconcile_intent runs _run (helper.py:203-207, 236), which raises HelperError on a non-zero exit and lets TimeoutExpired propagate. I verified with verify_cmd/v_cmd11.py: with intent.json desired=RUNNING and systemctl exiting 5, it raises HelperError SYSTEMD_ACTION_FAILED. With Restart=on-failure and RestartSec=3 the helper restarts into the same failure, the socket never appears, and all lifecycle, settings, reboot, shutdown and PPP control is lost. The trigger (engine unit removed, renamed or masked after the lifecycle approval) is an edge case, so medium.

#### CMD-4 · LOW · confirmed · bug
**Helper not running or socket missing is reported as OUTCOME_UNKNOWN instead of FAILED**  
`src/rdf_node/control.py:143`

- **Masalah:** The worker maps any OSError to OUTCOME_UNKNOWN ('Helper timeout after side effect may mean unknown'). call_helper's connect() raises FileNotFoundError (no socket) or ConnectionRefusedError (stale socket, helper crashed) before any byte is sent, so nothing can have happened. These commands are still journaled as OUTCOME_UNKNOWN with error 'FILENOTFOUNDERROR' or 'CONNECTIONREFUSEDERROR'. For system.shutdown.execute, this also leaves a permanent 'pending shutdown' (CMD-3). Ground-origin commands are accepted even when helper_available is false, because control.py does not check it.
- **Dampak:** Operators see 'outcome unknown' amber states and are told not to retry for actions that definitely did not run. Ground's unresolved-PPP and unresolved-shutdown gates then demand extra confirmations. The error codes are raw Python exception names.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced: scratchpad/audit/repro_helper_down.py. config.patch, processing.set, system.reboot.prepare and system.shutdown.execute all go ACCEPTED -> OUTCOME_UNKNOWN {'error':'FILENOTFOUNDERROR'}, pending_shutdowns: True. With a dead socket file the result is OUTCOME_UNKNOWN {'error':'CONNECTIONREFUSEDERROR'}.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Classify connect-phase failures (FileNotFoundError, ConnectionRefusedError, PermissionError raised by connect) as FAILED with a stable code such as HELPER_UNAVAILABLE. Keep OUTCOME_UNKNOWN only for timeouts or resets after the request was sent. Optionally reject Ground commands up front when helper_status is empty.
- **Verifikasi:** call_helper (helper.py:110-112) raises an OSError subclass from connect() before anything is sent, and control.py:143 maps every OSError to OUTCOME_UNKNOWN. I reproduced this with verify_cmd/v_cmd4.py: processing.set and reboot.prepare with no helper socket end as OUTCOME_UNKNOWN with a raw exception-name error. The misclassification is real. I lowered it to low because the trigger is an edge case: the helper must be down while a command is sent. The UI flows also limit the damage: shutdown/reboot execute is only sent after prepare returns APPLIED with a challenge, so a dead helper stops at prepare and does not create a pending shutdown in practice. The visible effect is mostly amber …

#### CMD-6 · LOW · confirmed · ux
**Clock-untrusted gate rejects local shutdown, reboot, PPP restart and profile change while the panel shows them enabled**  
`src/rdf_node/control.py:85`

- **Masalah:** Every non-read command, including scheduler-only stream.set and the local recovery actions (ppp.restart, system.reboot.*, system.shutdown.*), is rejected with CLOCK_UNTRUSTED unless timedatectl reports NTPSynchronized=yes. capabilities() (agent.py:296-319) ignores clock trust, so the panel enables the buttons (app.js:796-805). On a Pi without an RTC, whose time source is NTP over the PPP link, the clock is untrusted exactly when the link is down: at boot without the link, or after the kernel sync flag lapses during a long outage. In that state the operator cannot gracefully shut down or reboot the Pi, or change the profile, from the touchscreen. Local panel timestamps come from the same host, so the gate adds nothing for local-admin.
- **Dampak:** In the field, when the link and NTP are down (the moment local recovery is most needed), the touchscreen cannot shut down or reboot the Pi or restart PPP. The only remaining option is pulling power.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced: scratchpad/audit/repro_clock.py. With clock_trusted=False, capabilities show shutdown/reboot/ppp_restart True, while stream.set, ppp.restart, system.shutdown.prepare and system.reboot.prepare all return REJECTED {'error':'CLOCK_UNTRUSTED'}.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Skip the clock gate for local-admin (or at least for stream.set, ppp.restart, reboot and shutdown), since the deadline is checked against the same host clock. Otherwise, expose clock trust in capabilities and disable the buttons with an explanation.
- **Verifikasi:** The code matches the claim. control.py:85 rejects every non-read op, including local-admin stream.set, reboot and shutdown, with CLOCK_UNTRUSTED. capabilities() (agent.py:296-319) ignores clock trust, so app.js:796-805 enables the buttons. The local issued_ms also comes from the same host clock (app.js:732). However, README.md:264-266 explicitly documents that deadline-bound commands are held while NTPSynchronized is not 'yes', and CONTROL.md:72 lists the clock check. So holding the commands is designed and documented behavior. What remains is a UX inconsistency: the buttons stay enabled and the operator gets a raw CLOCK_UNTRUSTED rejection. Severity is therefore low, not medium.

#### CMD-10 · LOW · confirmed · contract-mismatch
**After a commanded reboot or shutdown (graceful service stop), retained availability stays online:true**  
`src/rdf_node/agent.py:203`

- **Masalah:** Agent.stop() stops the MQTT clients, which send DISCONNECT with reason 0x00. Under MQTT 5 that tells the broker to discard the Last Will, so the 'CONNECTION_LOST' will is never published, and Agent never publishes an explicit offline availability. When systemd stops rdf-edge 5 s after REBOOT_SCHEDULED or SHUTDOWN_SCHEDULED, or on any service stop, the retained `availability` keeps saying {online:true}. `planned_reboot` is set in control.py:215 but never read, which suggests an unimplemented 'planned offline' publish. MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md:58/86 tells Ground dashboards to show availability as the connection status.
- **Dampak:** An existing Ground dashboard that follows the topic summary shows a shut-down or rebooting node as online indefinitely after the command pipeline turns it off.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced: scratchpad/audit/repro_availability.py (broker fixture). Retained availability before stop: {"online":true}. After a.stop() the retained value is still {"online":true}.
- **Saran perbaikan:** In Agent.stop(), publish a retained availability {online:false, reason:'PLANNED_REBOOT'|'PLANNED_SHUTDOWN'|'SERVICE_STOP'} (using planned_reboot or a shutdown flag) and wait briefly for its PUBACK before disconnecting. Alternatively, disconnect with reason 0x04 so the will is sent.
- **Verifikasi:** I re-ran repro_availability.py: after a graceful a.stop(), the retained availability is still online:true. On SIGTERM, cli.py:227-231 calls app.stop(), and the Client sends DISCONNECT 0x00 (mqtt.py ~452), which discards the will. No offline publish exists, and planned_reboot is written (control.py:215) but never read. I lowered it to low. MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md:58 defines the will only for a lost connection, and :101 says retained state/availability must still be checked for freshness and session (the payload carries t and sid). The bundled Ground ignores availability entirely. Also, if PPP goes down before rdf-edge stops during poweroff, the will is published anyway. Only naive external …

#### CMD-12 · LOW · confirmed · ux
**Ground read queries (config.get/operation.get) replace the Edge panel's 'last operation' and trigger green toasts**  
`src/rdf_node/agent.py:264`

- **Masalah:** READ_OPS are journaled as normal operations (control.py:74,79), and the snapshot's last_operation is simply journal.latest(1) (agent.py:264,286). Each time a Ground operator presses 'Refresh config', the Edge CMD line changes to 'config.get APPLIED' and renderOperation shows a green toast. This hides the outcome of the last real command, for example a FAILED Stop or an OUTCOME_UNKNOWN PPP restart. The Ground's own last_operation is affected the same way, and ground.js:611 then stops refreshing the settings status.
- **Dampak:** The local operator loses visibility of the last mutation's result and gets misleading success toasts triggered by remote read-only activity.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced: scratchpad/audit/repro_last_op.py. last_operation before: ppp.restart OUTCOME_UNKNOWN. After operation.get: last_operation is operation.get APPLIED. After config.get: config.get APPLIED.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Exclude READ_OPS (config.get, operation.get) from last_operation, for example latest mutation only, or skip journaling read queries in the operations table.
- **Verifikasi:** control.py:74 and :79 journal READ_OPS as APPLIED rows, and agent.py:264,286 sets last_operation to journal.latest(1) ordered by updated. A Ground 'Refresh config' or operation.get therefore replaces the Edge CMD line. APPLIED is in goodStages (app.js:241), so renderOperation shows a green toast. This hides the outcome of the last real mutation. The effect is minor UX, so low.

#### CMD-13 · LOW · confirmed · data-correctness
**Edge keeps PPP_RESTART_REQUESTED as non-terminal; an agent restart overwrites the systemd-accepted evidence with OUTCOME_UNKNOWN**  
`src/rdf_node/journal.py:85`

- **Masalah:** PPP_RESTART_REQUESTED is not in TERMINAL (journal.py:8). The Edge panel therefore shows it with a 'progress' chip forever (app.js:241-244), and prune() never removes it. On the next rdf-edge restart, recover() replaces the result {accepted_by_systemd, requested_ms, service} with {'reason':'AGENT_RESTARTED_RECONCILE_NO_REPLAY'} as OUTCOME_UNKNOWN, losing the proof that systemd accepted the restart.
- **Dampak:** Edge-side history misreports a PPP restart that systemd did accept, and the panel shows an operation as permanently in progress.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced: scratchpad/audit/repro_ppp_edge_stage.py. Edge last_operation PPP_RESTART_REQUESTED, still present after prune(0,0). After restart: OUTCOME_UNKNOWN {'reason':'AGENT_RESTARTED_RECONCILE_NO_REPLAY'}.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Treat PPP_RESTART_REQUESTED as a resolved stage on Edge: exclude it from recover() and keep its result. Show it with a neutral or terminal tone in the panel.
- **Verifikasi:** PPP_RESTART_REQUESTED is not in TERMINAL (journal.py:8), and the Edge never advances it, because only Ground reconciles it to APPLIED. On Edge, recover() (journal.py:80-86) therefore rewrites it to OUTCOME_UNKNOWN AGENT_RESTARTED_RECONCILE_NO_REPLAY and discards the accepted_by_systemd result. prune never removes it. stageTone (app.js:244) shows it with the 'progress' tone indefinitely. Ground ignores later non-PPP_RESTART_REQUESTED acks for such rows (ground.py:227), so the effect stays limited to Edge history and display. Low.
