### Edge API, config, CLI

_The Edge HTTP API (api.py), config loader/validator, CLI and their docs work for the main paths. example.yaml validates. Every fetch() in web/app.js matches an api.py route in method, path, body fields and response shape; I found no field-name mismatches. In a live in-process Edge harness with a loopback broker on port 18801, panel-written settings (MQTT, display, profile) were validated and persisted, and took effect without a restart. The real problems are elsewhere:
- Two sources of truth for MQTT: the panel's state file silently overrides config.yaml and `import-bundle`.
- Panel blind spots: an unlisted topic keeps the Data tab red, MQTT error reasons are never shown, the blank screen hides the stale-API alarm, and there is no PPP restart button.
- Telemetry profiles promise cadences the allowed Bulk budget can never reach.
- Smaller doctor, setup and docs mismatches.
The repository was not modified._

#### API-1 · MEDIUM · confirmed · data-correctness
**Panel-saved MQTT settings silently override config.yaml and import-bundle (enabled, host, port, TLS and credentials)**  
`src/rdf_node/agent.py:81`

- **Masalah:** At startup Agent.__init__ (agent.py:48-49) loads /var/lib/rdf-node/mqtt-ui-settings.json through _load_mqtt_settings (81-110). Whenever that file exists, its enabled/host/port/tls/transport/client_id replace the config.yaml values. Also, once the panel has stored CTRL or BULK credentials, control_custom/bulk_custom stay True forever (configure_mqtt 166-167): blank fields mean 'keep', and there is no way to go back to the provisioned files. import-bundle (cli.py:95-100) only rewrites config.yaml, prints 'TLS/credential terpasang' and asks for a restart. After the restart the old panel values still win. Likewise, README §4 and MQTT_GROUND.md tell operators to edit /etc/rdf-node/config.yaml for an existing broker, and those edits are ignored once the panel has ever saved MQTT settings. import-bundle also leaves mqtt.transport/websocket_path untouched, so a config previously set to websocket would try WSS against the provisioner's TCP/TLS listener. No doc mentions this precedence.
- **Dampak:** After provisioning or re-provisioning, Edge keeps using an old test broker, plaintext, old credentials, or stays disabled. Meanwhile the CLI reports success and `doctor` reports the config.yaml values, so Ground gets no telemetry and the cause is not visible.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced, scratchpad api-cli/t_precedence.py: the panel file has plaintext testbroker.local:1883; config is updated as import-bundle would (10.90.0.1:8883, tls true). The new Agent connects to {'host':'testbroker.local','port':1883,'tls':False}. api-cli/t_sticky.py: the panel saved enabled=false plus CTRL credentials, then saved again with blank credentials; config gets enabled:true plus new bundle credential files. After restart the effective enabled is False and control creds are mqtt-ui-control.json (bulk correctly uses the bundle file).
- **Saran perbaikan:** Define one precedence rule. Options: make import-bundle and config edits clear or rewrite mqtt-ui-settings.json and mqtt-ui-*.json (e.g. import-bundle deletes them), or store a config.yaml digest in the UI file and ignore the UI file when config.yaml changes. Add a 'use provisioned credentials' or reset control in Data > Atur, set transport=tcp in import-bundle, and document the precedence in OPERATIONS.md.
- **Verifikasi:** I re-ran t_precedence.py and t_sticky.py and got the same results. agent.py:48-49 calls _load_mqtt_settings (81-110), and whenever mqtt-ui-settings.json is valid it replaces enabled, host, port, transport and tls from config.yaml. control_custom and bulk_custom latch to True at agent.py:166-167, and nothing in the panel or CLI resets them. import_bundle (cli.py:95-100) only rewrites config.yaml and never touches the UI files or transport. No doc mentions this precedence: OPERATIONS.md:24 only names the file, and README:241 and MQTT_GROUND.md:49 tell operators to edit config.yaml. The effect is real and silent: after a restart MQTT stays disabled, or Control uses old custom credentials that …

#### API-2 · MEDIUM · confirmed · ux
**Data tab badge stays red because telemetry/diagnostic/angular is missing from the panel topic list**  
`web/app.js:271`

- **Masalah:** The Agent records delivery state for 12 outbound suffixes, including telemetry/diagnostic/angular (agent.py:457). The hard-coded mqttTopics list (app.js:46-59) has only 11 and omits telemetry/diagnostic/angular, so that topic has no row. tabAlerts (app.js:271) marks the Data tab 'bad' when any entry in mqtt_topic_delivery is ERROR and not a policy skip. After every MQTT (re)connect with a valid source, the single diagnostic angular frame offered during WAIT_STABLE expires in the Control outbox (OUTBOX_EXPIRED). It is never updated afterwards, because later discards find nothing in the outbox. Result: a permanent red dot on the Data tab while every visible topic shows TERKIRIM.
- **Dampak:** The operator sees a permanent 'ada masalah' alarm on the Data tab with nothing to explain it, which trains them to ignore real delivery failures.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced in an Edge harness (api-cli/edge_harness.py, port 18801, loopback broker), MQTT enabled through POST /api/v2/mqtt/settings. For 26 s the snapshot showed every other topic SENT, plus 'telemetry/diagnostic/angular': ('ERROR','OUTBOX_EXPIRED'), even after Bulk angular streamed normally (bulk_pause None). Running app.js deliverySkipped/tabAlerts in node (api-cli/tab.js) on that map gives {data:'bad'} and lists telemetry/diagnostic/angular as the only errored topic without a visible row.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Add telemetry/diagnostic/angular, with QoS and description, to mqttTopics. Build the list from the keys in mqtt_topic_delivery so unknown topics still get a row. Optionally treat OUTBOX_EXPIRED on diagnostic topics as skipped, or clear a diagnostic ERROR when the diagnostic is no longer needed.
- **Verifikasi:** Reproduced independently (verify-api/v_api2.py, loopback broker, valid source). About 4 s after configure_mqtt, telemetry/diagnostic/angular is ('ERROR','OUTBOX_EXPIRED'). It stays that way after Bulk resumes (bulk_pause None, telemetry/angular SENT). Outbox.discard (mqtt.py:185-188) only notifies when the key is still queued, so the later DIAGNOSTIC_NOT_NEEDED discard at agent.py:460 never clears the state. Only a reconfigure clears it (agent.py:191). app.js:46-59 has no row for this suffix. tabAlerts (app.js:271) scans every delivery entry, so the Data tab shows 'bad' while every visible row says TERKIRIM. This happens after every MQTT connect with a usable source.

#### API-4 · MEDIUM · confirmed · doc-mismatch
**`controls approve --ppp-restart` enables a local PPP restart that has no button in the Edge panel**  
`web/app.js:796`

- **Masalah:** The CLI prompt (cli.py:166) asks the operator to type 'T900 PPP RESTART' 'untuk mengizinkan restart PPP lokal dari panel Edge'. It then sets control.ppp_restart_enabled and helper allow_ppp_restart. capabilities() exposes ppp_restart (agent.py:318), and the API accepts a local-admin ppp.restart (control.py:97). README §7 and CONTROL.md say --ppp-restart enables the related Edge action. The Kontrol dialog (app.js:796-811), however, only builds Start/Stop/Restart/Frekuensi/Reboot/Shutdown, and nothing in app.js sends op 'ppp.restart'.
- **Dampak:** Operators who complete the PPP approval ritual find no way to restart the T900 link from the touchscreen, and only Ground can do it. When MQTT Control is down, which is exactly when a PPP restart is needed, Ground cannot reach the node.
- **Bukti:** grep finds no 'ppp.restart' command call in web/app.js (only topic-list text). Via the harness API, POST /api/v2/commands {op:'ppp.restart'} from local-admin is reachable and gated by CAPABILITY_DISABLED, which shows the backend path exists without any UI entry point.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Add a 'Restart PPP T900' action (with confirmation) to the Raspberry section of the Kontrol dialog, gated by caps.ppp_restart. Otherwise change the CLI prompt and docs to say the flag has no panel effect.
- **Verifikasi:** helper.py:261-262 shows that allow_ppp_restart gates only non-Ground origins, and control.py:93-98 shows that ppp_restart_enabled gates only the local-admin path. So `controls approve --ppp-restart` only enables a local-panel ppp.restart. The Kontrol dialog (app.js:796-809) builds only Start, Stop, Restart, Frekuensi, Reboot and Shutdown, and no code in app.js sends op 'ppp.restart'. Both the CLI prompt (cli.py:166, 'dari panel Edge') and README:338 promise an Edge action that has no button, so the approval step has no operator-visible effect.

#### API-5 · MEDIUM · confirmed · ux
**Panel shows only 'ERROR / ERROR' for MQTT failures and never the reason the backend already reports**  
`web/app.js:235`

- **Masalah:** Client.status() returns an error code (e.g. CONNECTIONREFUSEDERROR, TLS_ERROR, TLS_CERTIFICATE_VERIFY_FAILED, CONNACK_REJECTED, WEBSOCKET_HANDSHAKE_EOF) in snapshot.link.mqtt_control.error and mqtt_bulk.error. app.js reads only .state (renderData at 235, the Link rows at 309, the home MQTT stat at 289). The MQTT settings dialog also shows no connection result, and it ignores the trust_mode and credentials_set fields that GET /api/v2/mqtt/settings returns.
- **Dampak:** An operator configuring the broker from the 480x320 touchscreen cannot tell a wrong port from a bad password or a certificate problem without SSH. That is the main purpose of Data > Atur.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced (api-cli/t6.py): after a closed port, TLS against a plaintext broker, or WebSocket against a TCP broker were saved via the API, the snapshot reported ctrl/bulk ERROR with CONNECTIONREFUSEDERROR, TLS_ERROR and WEBSOCKET_HANDSHAKE_EOF respectively. grep shows app.js never reads mqtt_control.error, trust_mode or *_credentials_set.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Show link.mqtt_control.error and mqtt_bulk.error in the Data summary and Link rows, and in the settings dialog after a save. Also show 'akun tersimpan / belum' from control_credentials_set and bulk_credentials_set, and the trust mode.
- **Verifikasi:** Client.status() exposes error (mqtt.py:234, with values set at 252-259 such as TLS_CERTIFICATE_VERIFY_FAILED, TLS_ERROR and CONNACK_REJECTED). app.js reads only .state for mqtt_control and mqtt_bulk (lines 235, 289, 309). The only places it reads .error are per-topic delivery entries (115-125), and those exist only after a publish attempt, which never happens while the connection fails. The settings dialog (app.js:540-583) ignores trust_mode, ca_configured and *_credentials_set from mqtt_settings_view (agent.py:136-141). From the touchscreen an operator sees only 'ERROR / ERROR' and cannot tell a wrong port from a rejected password or a certificate failure. This is a real diagnosability gap …

#### API-6 · MEDIUM · confirmed · ux
**Blank (black) screen hides the 'SUMBER STATUS HILANG' stale-API alarm and can auto-engage while the API is dead**  
`web/app.js:411`

- **Masalah:** In the 250 ms interval (app.js:411), auto-blank fires when blank_after_seconds has elapsed and the last snapshot has no error alerts. It does not check the stale flag computed on the same line. The #blank overlay (z-index 20) sits above #stale (z-index 5). Wake-up only happens when a new error code appears in snapshot.active_alerts, which cannot happen while the API is unreachable or the snapshot is frozen.
- **Dampak:** If the backend or collector dies while the operator is away, the kiosk shows only a dim clock instead of the loss-of-status alarm. Status loss goes unnoticed until someone touches the screen.
- **Bukti:** Code trace: stale=Date.now()-lastProgress>5000 sets only $('stale').hidden. The branch `if(sec>0&&Date.now()-lastTouch>sec*1000&&!errors.size)enterBlank()` ignores stale, and errorAlertCodes() reads the last cached snapshot. style.css:172 gives #stale z-index 5 and style.css:307 gives #blank z-index 20.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Do not auto-blank while stale, and wake the screen (or draw the alarm on the blank overlay) when stale becomes true.
- **Verifikasi:** In the app.js:411 interval, the stale result only toggles #stale. The auto-blank branch checks sec, lastTouch and errors.size but not stale. While blank, the only automatic wake is a new error code from the last cached snapshot, which cannot change while the API is dead or the snapshot is frozen. In index.html:65-67, #blank (style.css:307, z-index 20, inset 0) sits above #stale (style.css:172, z-index 5). Once the screen is blank, or auto-blanks after the API dies (snapshot.config.preferences stays cached), the 'SUMBER STATUS HILANG' alarm that OPERATIONS.md:14-15 promises is invisible until someone touches the screen. Auto-blank is opt-in (blank_after_seconds defaults to 0), so this is an …

#### API-3 · LOW · confirmed · data-correctness
**Profiles BALANCED (>=4 s) and GRAPH (>=2 s) give the same ~9.3 s angular cadence; the allowed Bulk budget can never meet the labels**  
`src/rdf_node/config.py:66`

- **Masalah:** Angular frames are now about 3 KB of JSON (360 floats). The scheduler period is max(profile interval, (payload+topic+160)/bulk_budget_bytes_s) (agent.py:488-490). config.py caps bulk_budget_bytes_s at 500 (default 350). That gives at least about 6.5 s at the maximum allowed budget and about 9.3 s at the default. The 4 s ('balanced') and 2 s ('graph_u8') profile intervals in PROFILES (config.py:13-17) therefore never take effect. The panel profile picker (app.js:736-740) still offers 'BALANCED Grafik 360° ≥ 4 dtk' and 'GRAPH Grafik 360° ≥ 2 dtk' as distinct choices. Separately, every stream.set adds the 20 s resume_stable_seconds pause, so a profile switch first delays the graph by about 20-27 s.
- **Dampak:** Operators switching to GRAPH for a faster 360° plot get no change, only a 20+ s gap. The UI and README cadence claims are wrong for every allowed config.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced (api-cli/t5.py): with stream.set graph_u8 and then balanced, measured telemetry/angular sent_ms gaps were graph_u8 [26.7, 9.26, 9.25] s and balanced [21.4, 9.25, 9.25] s. The measured payload was about 3035 bytes.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Either derive and show the effective cadence (payload/budget) in the profile picker and Link tab, or make the profiles change something real (e.g. decimate or round values, or raise the permitted Bulk budget range to fit a JSON frame within the interval). Merge or rename graph_u8 if it can no longer differ from balanced.
- **Verifikasi:** The arithmetic holds. The period at agent.py:488-490 is max(interval, (~3.0 KB + topic + 160)/bulk_budget). The budget is capped at 500 (config.py:66), so the period is at least ~6.5 s and ~9.2 s at the default 350. The 4 s and 2 s minimums (config.py:15-16) therefore never apply, and balanced and graph_u8 behave identically. However, this is already documented: MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md:142 says graph_u8 has the 'format JSON dan pace sama dengan balanced', and README:283 and IMPLEMENTATION.md:240 say JSON size and the Bulk budget extend the interval. The panel labels say '≥ 4 dtk' and '≥ 2 dtk', which are technically true minimums. The 20 s pause after stream.set comes from the general …

#### API-7 · LOW · confirmed · robustness
**`doctor` reports config.yaml MQTT state and profile, not the effective runtime values**  
`src/rdf_node/cli.py:107`

- **Masalah:** doctor prints 'MQTT: configured/off' and 'Profile:' from config.yaml (cli.py:107-108). It checks ca_file and credential files from config only (124-125). The running Agent instead uses mqtt-ui-settings.json and mqtt-ui-*.json when the panel has saved MQTT, and journal key 'profile' after any stream.set (agent.py:31). doctor also prints 'ca_file : missing/not readable' when ca_file is null and the system trust store is in use, and it flags credential files as missing even when MQTT is off or anonymous.
- **Dampak:** The troubleshooting tool contradicts the panel and hides the cause of API-1.
- **Bukti:** Ran `python3 run.py doctor --config <temp>`: it printed 'MQTT: off' and 'ca_file : missing/not readable' with ca_file null. Code trace shows no read of state_dir/mqtt-ui-settings.json or the journal profile.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Have doctor load the same effective MQTT settings as Agent (reuse _load_mqtt_settings logic) and the journal profile, print 'system trust' for a null ca_file, and skip credential checks when MQTT is off.
- **Verifikasi:** doctor (cli.py:107-108, 124-125) prints the MQTT state and profile from config.yaml and checks credential and ca_file paths from config only. It never reads state_dir/mqtt-ui-settings.json or the journal 'profile' that Agent uses (agent.py:31, 48-49). With ca_file null (system trust, a valid mode per agent.py:136), it prints 'missing/not readable', and it flags credential files even when MQTT is off. It therefore misreports in exactly the API-1 situation. This is a minor tooling issue.

#### API-8 · LOW · confirmed · ux
**Home dial says 'TANPA DATA' (no data) for a fresh but unverified detection**  
`web/app.js:187`

- **Masalah:** updateDial receives unverified=showDiagnostic, which is true only when doa.xml diagnostics are available (app.js:284,293). On a default install (authority_verified/angle_verified false) with only DOA_value.html, detection.state is 'UNVERIFIED' with a fresh record. The dial note still reads 'TANPA DATA' and the aria-label says 'belum ada pengukuran valid'. Meanwhile the age line shows 'UNVERIFIED / 0.0 dtk', and Edge publishes telemetry/doa ok=0 with the raw angle to Ground.
- **Dampak:** During commissioning the operator is told there is no data while fresh data is flowing and only verification is missing, which invites wrong troubleshooting (checking the SDR or source path).
- **Bukti:** Harness with VERIFIED=0: the snapshot shows detection valid False, state UNVERIFIED, reasons [SOURCE_UNVERIFIED, ANGLE_UNVERIFIED], age 38 ms, diagnostic_doa.available False. That input makes app.js:187 render 'TANPA DATA'.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Pass unverified as true when detection.state==='UNVERIFIED' (or reasons contain only *_UNVERIFIED), and show 'UNVERIFIED' on the dial even without doa.xml.
- **Verifikasi:** render (app.js:284,293) passes unverified=showDiagnostic=!d.valid&&diag.available. Without doa.xml, diag.available is false (source.py:264), so updateDial sets the note to 'TANPA DATA' and the aria-label to 'belum ada pengukuran valid' (app.js:184,187). This happens even when detection.state is 'UNVERIFIED' (source.py:239) with a fresh record and Edge is publishing ok=0 DoA (agent.py:398-403). The age line shows 'UNVERIFIED / x dtk' at the same time, so the screen contradicts itself. The dial already has an 'UNVERIFIED' state that is simply not wired up for this case. Minor, but real.

#### API-10 · LOW · confirmed · contract-mismatch
**Config file validation and panel/docs disagree on anonymous MQTT**  
`src/rdf_node/config.py:100`

- **Masalah:** validate_config rejects mqtt.enabled:true without both credential files (MQTT_CREDENTIALS_REQUIRED, config.py:100-102). The panel path (configure_mqtt / _mqtt_transport_ready) accepts enabled with no credentials and connects anonymously. IMPLEMENTATION.md and MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md say 'Tanpa kredensial terkonfigurasi, client memakai anonymous CONNECT'. So a setup that works from the panel cannot be expressed in config.yaml, and the service refuses to start if someone tries.
- **Dampak:** A deployment that tries to mirror a working panel config into config.yaml fails to start; the startup error does not explain the inconsistency.
- **Bukti:** api-cli/cfgtests.py: {'mqtt':{'enabled':True}} gives ValueError MQTT_CREDENTIALS_REQUIRED. The existing test test_plain_tcp_settings_connect_without_credentials shows the panel path connecting anonymously.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Apply the same rule in both paths: either allow null credential files in validate_config or require credentials in configure_mqtt, and document whichever is chosen.
- **Verifikasi:** Verified: load_config with {'mqtt':{'enabled':True}} raises MQTT_CREDENTIALS_REQUIRED (config.py:100-102). The panel path (_mqtt_transport_ready, agent.py:77-79) accepts null credential paths and connects anonymously. IMPLEMENTATION.md:32 documents anonymous CONNECT 'tanpa kredensial terkonfigurasi'. A configuration that works from the panel cannot be expressed in config.yaml, and trying it stops the service from starting. This is a minor inconsistency.

#### API-11 · LOW · confirmed · ux
**Config range errors do not name the offending key**  
`src/rdf_node/config.py:69`

- **Masalah:** The numeric range loop calls util.finite, which raises a bare 'NUMBER_OUT_OF_RANGE' (also for every freshness.* key at line 76 and display.blank_after_seconds at 83). The service fails at start with no hint which of about 15 numeric keys is wrong. In contrast, type errors from _merge include the key path.
- **Dampak:** An edge service crash-loops after a config typo, and the operator has to bisect the YAML through a slow link.
- **Bukti:** api-cli/cfgtests.py: {'api':{'port':80}} and {'mqtt':{'port':0}} both give 'ValueError NUMBER_OUT_OF_RANGE', with no key.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Wrap each finite() call so it re-raises as f'{section}.{key}: NUMBER_OUT_OF_RANGE ({lo}..{hi})'.
- **Verifikasi:** Verified: api.port 80, mqtt.port 0, freshness.doa_ms 1 and control_budget_bytes_s 400 all raise a bare 'NUMBER_OUT_OF_RANGE' from util.finite (util.py:37-38), called in the config.py:61-69/76/83 loops without the key. Type errors from _merge do include the path. The edge service fails at startup with no indication of which key is wrong. Minor operator UX.

#### API-12 · LOW · confirmed · ux
**Display preference changes briefly revert because the next snapshot predates the save**  
`web/app.js:783`

- **Masalah:** POST /api/v2/display/preferences writes the journal (api.py:206), but /api/v2/snapshot serves the cached snapshot rebuilt every poll_ms (250 ms) by the collector. The panel applies the saved prefs locally (app.js:783). If the next 500 ms poll lands before the collector rebuilds, render() re-applies the old theme, accent or font, and the screen flashes back to the previous look for about 0.5 s.
- **Dampak:** Visual flicker and doubt about whether the setting saved on the touchscreen.
- **Bukti:** api-cli/t4.py: in 10 out of 10 saves, an immediate GET /api/v2/snapshot still returned the previous theme. With a 500 ms poll and a 250 ms rebuild, roughly a quarter of taps produce a visible revert flash.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Rebuild or patch the snapshot's config.preferences inside the POST handler, or have the panel ignore snapshot preferences older than its last successful save.
- **Verifikasi:** The POST handler (api.py:197-207) writes the journal only. GET /api/v2/snapshot returns the cached snapshot_data (api.py:132-133, agent.py:288-289), which is rebuilt every poll_ms (250 ms, plus helper latency) by _collect. The panel applies the saved prefs locally (app.js:778), but the next poll (500 ms cadence, app.js:376) calls render(), and app.js:317-319 re-applies snapshot.config.preferences. If that snapshot predates the save, or a poll was already in flight, the theme, accent or font flips back for one poll cycle. This is a brief cosmetic flicker.

#### API-13 · LOW · confirmed · data-correctness
**Disabling MQTT from the panel (or stopping the service) leaves retained availability 'online:true'**  
`src/rdf_node/agent.py:182`

- **Masalah:** _apply_pending_mqtt stops the old clients (agent.py:181-182), and Client._session then sends a normal DISCONNECT (mqtt.py:462-465). Under MQTT 5 the broker discards the Last Will on a normal DISCONNECT, and Edge never publishes an offline availability. The retained availability therefore stays {online:true} after the operator disables MQTT in Data > Atur or systemd stops rdf-edge. MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY describes availability as online when connected and Last Will when the connection is lost.
- **Dampak:** Any dashboard that uses the retained availability topic shows the node online indefinitely after a deliberate disconnect. The bundled Ground ignores availability, so the impact is limited to other consumers.
- **Bukti:** api-cli/t_disable.py with the loopback broker fixture: after configure_mqtt enabled=false, a.clients == {} while the broker's retained availability is still b'{"v":2,...,"online":true,...}'.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Before a deliberate stop, publish a retained availability {online:false, reason:'DISABLED'/'STOPPED'} (QoS 1, wait for PUBACK briefly), or disconnect with reason 0x04 so the will is sent.
- **Verifikasi:** _apply_pending_mqtt (agent.py:181-182) and Agent.stop (208) call Client.stop. The session then sends DISCONNECT with reason 0 (b'\xe0\x00', mqtt.py:462-465), so under MQTT 5 the broker drops the Will. Edge never publishes a retained offline availability, so the retained {online:true} persists after a deliberate disable or service stop. Bundled Ground subscribes to availability (ground.py:38) but never uses it, so only third-party consumers are affected. The docs describe the Will only for connection loss, so this is a stale-retained-state issue of low impact.

#### API-14 · LOW · confirmed · doc-mismatch
**`setup` prompt says an empty answer means 'local setup mode' but it keeps the saved source**  
`src/rdf_node/cli.py:62`

- **Masalah:** The prompt reads '(kosong = local setup mode)', which suggests Enter clears or skips the source. The code leaves cfg['source']['share_dir'] unchanged when the answer is empty (cli.py:63-65), and README:118 says Enter keeps the saved path. Also, `--yes` does not suppress the ACL prompt (cli.py:69), so the documented non-interactive run still blocks on input.
- **Dampak:** Operators re-running setup may believe pressing Enter removes the source, or get stuck in scripted runs.
- **Bukti:** Ran `printf 'n\n' | python3 run.py setup --config <temp> --share-dir ... --engine-unit rdfsdr.service --verify-source --yes`: it still asked 'Beri user rdf-edge akses baca ...? [y/N]'. The prompt text is at cli.py:62.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Change the prompt to '(kosong = pertahankan path tersimpan: <path>)' and let --yes, or a separate --no-grant-read, answer the ACL prompt.
- **Verifikasi:** On a re-run the prompt text at cli.py:62 ('kosong = local setup mode') contradicts the behavior at cli.py:63-65 (an empty answer keeps the saved share_dir) and README:117-118 ('Tekan Enter ... untuk mempertahankan path yang sudah tersimpan'). The --yes part is overstated: --yes is not documented as a fully non-interactive mode. It only answers the VERIFIED prompt (cli.py:77), and --grant-read exists for the ACL prompt, so the only real gap is that there is no non-interactive 'no' for the ACL prompt. This is a minor text mismatch.

#### API-16 · LOW · confirmed · robustness
**Kiosk URL hard-codes port 8790 although api.port is configurable**  
`config/example.yaml:57`

- **Masalah:** api.port is validated over 1024..65535 (config.py:62). deploy/kiosk/rdf-kiosk.service and rdf-kiosk hard-code RDF_NODE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8790, and the API's Host check (api.py:89-92) rejects any other port in the Host header. Changing api.port (e.g. to avoid a conflict) leaves the touchscreen on a dead or foreign page with no installer or doctor warning.
- **Dampak:** The panel goes blank or wrong after a supported config change.
- **Bukti:** deploy/kiosk/rdf-kiosk.service sets Environment=RDF_NODE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8790, and rdf-kiosk defaults to the same URL.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Have rdf-kiosk read api.port from /etc/rdf-node/config.yaml (or have the installer template it), and have doctor print the URL the kiosk should use.
- **Verifikasi:** api.port is configurable over 1024..65535 (config.py:62). deploy/kiosk/rdf-kiosk.service sets RDF_NODE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8790, and rdf-kiosk defaults to the same URL. _host (api.py:89-92) only accepts the server's own port. Changing api.port therefore leaves the kiosk on a dead URL unless the operator also overrides RDF_NODE_URL in a systemd drop-in. That override exists but is undocumented, and doctor and the installer give no warning. A minor deployment coupling.

#### API-17 · LOW · confirmed · ux
**Logout with an expired session opens the PIN login dialog; README calls the JSON angular 'compressed'**  
`web/app.js:521`

- **Masalah:** If the server-side session has already expired (600 s idle) while the panel still shows ADMIN, pressing 'Logout' gets 403 AUTHENTICATION_AND_CSRF_REQUIRED. The handler then calls login() (app.js:519-521), so the operator who wanted to log out is shown a PIN prompt. Separately, README.md:48 still says the Data list angular is 'terkompresi', while CLAUDE.md and PROTOCOL describe plain JSON with 360 values and no compression.
- **Dampak:** Confusing UI on a shared kiosk, and a misleading doc about payload size and format.
- **Bukti:** Code trace: $('admin').onclick → post('/api/v2/logout') → catch e.message==='AUTHENTICATION_AND_CSRF_REQUIRED' → login(). README.md:48 contains 'angular terkompresi'.
- **Saran perbaikan:** On a 403 during logout, clear local auth state and render without opening login(). Change README.md:48 to 'angular JSON 360 nilai (tanpa kompresi)'.
- **Verifikasi:** Code trace: keepAdminSessionAlive (app.js:384-393) checks the server session only while the user was active in the last 30 s. After more than 600 s idle the panel can still show ADMIN while the server session has expired. Tapping Logout then gets 403 AUTHENTICATION_AND_CSRF_REQUIRED, and app.js:519-521 calls login(), so a PIN dialog appears for a user who wanted to log out. Separately, README.md:48 says 'angular terkompresi', which contradicts the current plain JSON with 360 values (CLAUDE.md, IMPLEMENTATION.md:241 'tidak mengkuantisasi'). Both are minor.

#### API-9 · LOW · refuted · data-correctness
**Control budget range allows values that effectively disable diagnostic angular (~54 min period)**  
`src/rdf_node/config.py:65`

- **Masalah:** The diagnostic angular period is max(6 or 30, (payload+topic+160)/max(1, control_budget_bytes_s-baseline)), with baseline 800 when the source is valid or unverified-only and 510 when invalid (agent.py:450-456). config.py accepts control_budget_bytes_s from 500. At 500-800 the valid-but-blocked case gets 1 B/s, so the period is about 3237 s. At the default 850 it is about 65 s, not the documented 30 s minimum (MQTT_GROUND.md, MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md).
- **Dampak:** Within the allowed config range, Ground silently stops receiving the diagnostic 360° frame during setup or blocked periods, which the docs describe as a 6 s or 30 s minimum.
- **Bukti:** Computed from agent.py:450-456 with the measured 3035-byte payload: budget 500 gives 3237 s in both cases. Budget 850 gives 10 s (invalid) and 65 s (valid but blocked). Budget 600 gives 36 s and 3237 s.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Raise the control budget minimum above the baseline plus a useful headroom, or warn at load. Alternatively, document the real cadence formula next to the budget keys.
- **Verifikasi:** The default-budget claim is wrong. The docs do not promise 30 s at 850 B/s: MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md:51/144 and MQTT_GROUND.md:125 state that 6 s and 30 s are minimums that 'headroom Control dapat memperpanjang', and they explicitly give ~62.96 s at Control 850 B/s, matching agent.py:450-456. For budgets of 500-800 the long period is the documented headroom rule working as designed. MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md:202 notes that Control steady-state alone uses ~795 cost-B/s, so a 500 B/s budget has no room for a 3 KB frame. Throttling the optional diagnostic frame in that configuration is intended behavior, not a defect. At most, the validation lower bound sits below steady-state Control load, which is …

#### API-15 · LOW · refuted · contract-mismatch
**/api/v2/readyz always returns 200 regardless of readiness**  
`src/rdf_node/api.py:152`

- **Masalah:** readyz returns HTTP 200 with link, detection and capabilities even when detection is invalid, MQTT is disconnected or the snapshot is empty. Unlike healthz (503 when the snapshot is older than 5 s), it cannot be used as a readiness probe by systemd, monitoring or a legacy dashboard.
- **Dampak:** External monitors relying on HTTP status report the node as ready while nothing is being delivered.
- **Bukti:** Code trace at api.py:152: there is no conditional status code. With the demo, MQTT DISABLED and PPP DOWN, it still returned 200.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Return 503 unless Control MQTT is ready and the snapshot is fresh (or document readyz as informational only).
- **Verifikasi:** No as-built doc, unit, script or the panel uses /api/v2/readyz or defines a status-code contract for it. The only mention is the planning doc (docs/plans/...:949), which describes it as 'Status kesiapan subsistem | Detail reason codes', an informational detail endpoint. README:169 documents only healthz as the probe. With no consumer and no specified 503 semantics, a hypothetical monitor relying on its HTTP status is speculative, not a functional bug.

### Edge panel (kode)

_The Edge panel (web/index.html, web/app.js, web/style.css) is a plain-JS kiosk UI that polls /api/v2/snapshot about every 500 ms. I checked it with node --check, screenshots at 480x320 against a real demo server on port 28417 (since stopped), Playwright runs against a mocked API, and two small Python repros against the real Agent, Journal and test broker fixture. What works: the dial/needle/trail math (0 at top, clockwise, shortest-path rotation, trail cleared on an invalid sample or a new sid), the PIN flow, stale detection and Retry, the first-tap-only wake from the black screen, and the frequency patch payload. Render handled every snapshot key set to null or deleted, and every key set to a string except active_alerts. The problems are in notification and overlay layering, how fresh the status looks, small gaps against the Agent and docs, and touch details. An old operation toasts on every page load. Auto-blank hides the lost-API overlay. Outcome toasts sit under open modals. Frequencies are rounded to 1 kHz and that value is pre-filled into the keypad. Per-topic delivery status goes stale after the broker drops. One published topic is missing from the Data list. The shutdown 'uncertain' warning never clears. Text was reviewed in Indonesian; no repository files were changed._

#### EDGE-UI-2 · MEDIUM · confirmed · ux
**Auto-blank engages while the API is lost and hides the 'SUMBER STATUS HILANG' overlay; nothing wakes it**  
`web/app.js:411`

- **Masalah:** The 250 ms watchdog blanks the screen when blank_after_seconds>0, the idle time is exceeded, and errorAlertCodes() is empty. errorAlertCodes() reads the last cached snapshot, so a dead or frozen API never blocks blanking. Wake-up only happens for new error codes in a fresh snapshot, which cannot arrive while the API is down. #blank (z-index 20) covers #stale (z-index 5). Result: if the Agent or API stops, the kiosk goes, or stays, black with only a dim clock and never shows the lost-status warning. That contradicts the README rule that the screen does not auto-blank during an alarm.
- **Dampak:** An unattended display that lost its data source looks like a normal sleeping screen, so operators are not alerted that the shown status is no longer current.
- **Bukti:** Mocked API with blank_after_seconds=3: aborted /api/v2/snapshot at t=1 s. At t=7.5 s and t=10.5 s the state was {blank:true, stale:true}, so the stale overlay was under the black overlay and never woke (t2_blank.js case A, shots/blank-over-stale.png).
- **Saran perbaikan:** Treat stale (Date.now()-lastProgress>5000) as a blocking alarm: do not enter blank while stale, and call wakeScreen() when stale first becomes true.
- **Verifikasi:** The watchdog at app.js:411 checks errorAlertCodes(), which reads the cached snapshot, so a dead API never blocks auto-blank. Wake-up only happens for new error codes, which cannot arrive while the API is down. #blank has z-index 20 and #stale has z-index 5 (style.css:307,172). I re-ran t2_blank.js case A: at both 7.5 s and 10.5 s the state was {blank:true, stale:true}. Auto-blank is opt-in (blank_after_seconds default 0), but when enabled, losing the Agent/API (the one failure that can raise no Agent alarm) stays hidden behind the black screen. README:41-43 says error alarms prevent or wake the blank.

#### EDGE-UI-3 · MEDIUM · confirmed · ux
**Command outcome toasts appear beneath open modals; the modal keeps showing only 'ACCEPTED'**  
`web/style.css:102`

- **Masalah:** #toast has z-index 6 and #modal has z-index 10 with an opaque 0.88 overlay. After Terapkan (frequency), Start/Stop/Restart or profile selection, the modal stays open and #modalmsg shows the synchronous `${stage}: ${id}` (app.js:733), which is almost always 'ACCEPTED'. The real outcome (APPLIED, PERSISTED_UNVERIFIED, FAILED, OUTCOME_UNKNOWN) comes later through last_operation, and its toast is drawn under the modal and disappears after 4.5 s. Example: entering 1.5 MHz or 2000 MHz on the frequency keypad (no client range check; the helper allows 24-1766 MHz) gives ACCEPTED, then the helper fails with INTEGER_OUT_OF_RANGE, which the operator never sees while the dialog is open.
- **Dampak:** Operators believe a frequency change or lifecycle command went through when it failed or is unverified, and may leave the radio on the wrong frequency.
- **Bukti:** Mocked API: opened Kontrol > Frekuensi, tapped Terapkan, then changed last_operation for that id to FAILED. Result: {toastHidden:false, toastText:'config.patch: FAILED', topElement at toast centre:'dialoghead', modalmsg:'ACCEPTED: local-...'} (t5_toast_modal.js, shots/toast-under-modal.png). Helper range is in helper.py:28 (frequency_min_hz=24000000, frequency_max_hz=1766000000).
- **Saran perbaikan:** Raise the toast above the modal (z-index > 10). Better: track the submitted operation id inside the open modal and update #modalmsg when last_operation with that id reaches a terminal stage. Optionally show the 24-1766 MHz range on the keypad and disable Terapkan outside it.
- **Verifikasi:** control.submit returns {stage:'ACCEPTED'} for every async op (control.py:116-117), and command() writes that into #modalmsg (app.js:733). The real outcome arrives later only as a toast (z-index 6, style.css:102), which sits under #modal (z-index 10, style.css:179). The CMD chip is on the home page only. Client validation of config.patch is finite(-200,5e9) (control.py:104-110), while the helper enforces 24-1766 MHz (helper.py:28,41), so 1.5 MHz is ACCEPTED and then FAILS. I re-ran t5_toast_modal.js: toast 'config.patch: FAILED' is under 'dialoghead' while modalmsg still reads 'ACCEPTED: ...'.

#### EDGE-UI-5 · MEDIUM · confirmed · data-correctness
**Data tab per-topic status keeps showing green 'TERKIRIM' indefinitely after the broker is lost; age only in a hover tooltip**  
`web/app.js:132`

- **Masalah:** When the Control client is not ready, the Agent scheduler skips all periodic offers (agent.py:352-355), so mqtt_topic_delivery entries for telemetry/health, state and availability keep state SENT with an ever older updated_ms. setTopicDelivery puts the recency only in the cell's title and aria-label, which a touchscreen cannot show. With MQTT CTRL/BULK in ERROR, the rows still show green 'TERKIRIM / Socket lokal' and 'PUBACK broker' for availability and state. IMPLEMENTATION.md:208-216 describes sent_ms and updated_ms, but neither is visible.
- **Dampak:** Operators reading the Data tab are told telemetry is being delivered when nothing has been sent for minutes.
- **Bukti:** Real Agent plus tests/broker_fixture Broker (repro_stale_delivery.py): after broker.stop() and 20 s, ctrl state was ERROR, while availability, state and telemetry/health were still SENT with age_s=20.3. capabilities, health/detail and diagnostic/angular were ERROR OSERROR. The UI code path is app.js:116-135.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Show the age visibly (for example 'TERKIRIM 12 dtk lalu') and dim or mark entries older than a few intervals, or force all rows to a stale or neutral state whenever the owning client is not CONNECTED.
- **Verifikasi:** While ctrl is not ready, the scheduler skips all offers (agent.py:352-355) and only discards the pending 'diagnostic-angular' entry. Entries in mqtt_topic_delivery for availability, state and health keep state SENT (agent.py:320-331). setTopicDelivery puts the recency only in title/aria-label (app.js:131-134), which a touch kiosk cannot show. Mitigation: the Data page summary (index.html:42) shows CTRL/BULK ERROR directly above. The same staleness also occurs with MQTT healthy: telemetry/doa stays green TERKIRIM indefinitely once the source turns invalid and no DoA is published (agent.py:383-403). That makes this a notable UX problem on the delivery page.

#### EDGE-UI-6 · MEDIUM · confirmed · contract-mismatch
**telemetry/diagnostic/angular is published but missing from the Data topic list; its errors light a red Data-tab dot with no matching row**  
`web/app.js:55`

- **Masalah:** The Agent publishes telemetry/diagnostic/angular on the Control client (agent.py:457), and MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md lists it as an Edge->Ground topic. The mqttTopics outbound list (app.js:47-59) omits it. tabAlerts() still counts every key in mqtt_topic_delivery (app.js:271), so a non-skipped ERROR on that topic (OUTBOX_EXPIRED, MQTT_NOT_READY, connection errors from outbox.clear on disconnect) turns the Data tab dot red. No row in the Data tab shows GAGAL. When the source later becomes valid and Bulk streams, diagnostic/angular is not re-sent, so the red dot can persist indefinitely.
- **Dampak:** A persistent, unexplainable red alert on the Data tab erodes trust in alerts, and the diagnostic angular channel's delivery is invisible on the Edge.
- **Bukti:** Mocked API with delivery {'telemetry/diagnostic/angular':{state:'ERROR',error:'OUTBOX_EXPIRED'}} plus SENT for the others: Data nav dataset.alert='bad', while all 11 listed rows read TERKIRIM or BELUM DIKIRIM (t7_data.js, shots/data-red-dot.png). The broker-loss repro produced exactly this ERROR (diagnostic/angular ERROR OSERROR).
- **Saran perbaikan:** Add a 'telemetry/diagnostic/angular' row (Control, QoS 0, minimum 6 s / 30 s, UNVERIFIED) to mqttTopics. Optionally compute the Data alert only from listed suffixes.
- **Verifikasi:** agent.py:457 publishes telemetry/diagnostic/angular on Control, and docs/MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md:51 and PROTOCOL.md:15 list it. mqttTopics (app.js:46-59) omits it, but tabAlerts counts every delivery key (app.js:271). Only DIAGNOSTIC_NOT_NEEDED and BULK_PAUSED_* are treated as skipped. OUTBOX_EXPIRED (expiry=3 on a ~3 KB payload over the T900 budget) and MQTT_NOT_READY (agent.py:353) both count as errors. outbox.discard() is a no-op when nothing is pending (mqtt.py:185-188), so once Bulk streams normally the ERROR entry is never overwritten. I re-ran t7_data.js: dataAlert='bad' and no listed row shows GAGAL.

#### EDGE-UI-7 · MEDIUM · confirmed · bug
**After the first successful shutdown, every later panel shutdown shows two 'belum pasti' confirms forever; the advised reconcile does not clear it**  
`web/app.js:865`

- **Masalah:** prepareShutdown asks the operator to confirm twice (app.js:865 and 874) whenever /api/v2/operations/pending-shutdowns returns true. That endpoint is purely journal-based (journal.py:59-64): any system.shutdown.execute whose stage is not a definite failure counts. A successful shutdown leaves SHUTDOWN_SCHEDULED, which journal.recover() turns into OUTCOME_UNKNOWN on the next boot. prune() never deletes OUTCOME_UNKNOWN, so pending stays true forever. The dialogs and showShutdownOutcome (app.js:359) tell the operator to run 'sudo rdf-node controls shutdown-reconcile'. That command only removes the helper intent file (helper.py:171-199) and never touches the Agent journal, so the warning never goes away. The extra native confirm() dialogs also use up the 30 s challenge window between prepare and execute (helper.py:318).
- **Dampak:** The local shutdown flow permanently shows a 'previous shutdown uncertain' warning that operators learn to ignore. Following the documented remedy has no effect, and slow confirmation can expire the challenge, giving SHUTDOWN_CHALLENGE_INVALID.
- **Bukti:** repro_shutdown_sticky.py: Journal accepts system.shutdown.execute, sets SHUTDOWN_SCHEDULED, recover('boot-B') gives ['OUTCOME_UNKNOWN'], prune(max_rows=0, days=0) leaves pending_shutdowns()=[{'id':'local-x','stage':'OUTCOME_UNKNOWN'}]. Code trace of app.js:862-874 and cli.py:176-182.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Let the Agent resolve shutdown journal entries on a new boot (OUTCOME_UNKNOWN with boot_before different from the current boot, or a reconcile hook that marks them), or exclude entries from earlier boots from pending-shutdowns. Make the UI text match what actually clears the state, and use in-panel confirmations instead of native confirm().
- **Verifikasi:** pending_shutdowns (journal.py:59-64) counts every system.shutdown.execute row not in the failure set. SHUTDOWN_SCHEDULED is not TERMINAL, so recover() on any Agent start turns it into OUTCOME_UNKNOWN. REBOOT_SCHEDULED is resolved to APPLIED instead. prune() explicitly excludes OUTCOME_UNKNOWN (journal.py:91). cli.py:176-182 and helper._reconcile_shutdown (helper.py:171-199) touch only the helper intent file, never the Agent journal. I re-ran repro_shutdown_sticky.py: still pending after recover plus prune(0,0). As a result, after any shutdown (local or Ground-issued) the panel always shows both native confirm() prompts (app.js:865,874). showShutdownOutcome also reports a plain REJECTED …

#### EDGE-UI-8 · MEDIUM · confirmed · doc-mismatch
**Edge home 'TANPA DATA' and '--' while a fresh but unverified CSV DoA exists (default install); README says raw/UNVERIFIED angles stay numeric**  
`web/app.js:293`

- **Masalah:** When the only failing gates are SOURCE_UNVERIFIED and/or ANGLE_UNVERIFIED, which is the default config until 'setup --verify-source', detection.valid is false and raw_doa_deg is present. The panel only shows a raw angle when diagnostic_doa (doa.xml) is available (app.js:284). Otherwise the angle shows '--' and the dial note says 'TANPA DATA' (app.js:187), because updateDial gets unverified=showDiagnostic. Meanwhile the Agent sends this raw angle to Ground as telemetry/doa ok=0 (agent.py:398-403). README:31-33 says raw/UNVERIFIED angles remain numbers, and README §5 asks the operator to check orientation before typing VERIFIED, which this screen does not support without doa.xml.
- **Dampak:** During commissioning the Edge display claims there is no data, and the operator cannot read the raw angle locally to check orientation, although Ground receives it.
- **Bukti:** Mocked snapshot with valid:false, reasons:['SOURCE_UNVERIFIED','ANGLE_UNVERIFIED'], state:'UNVERIFIED', raw_doa_deg:224.1, source_age_ms:120 and diagnostic unavailable rendered: angle '--', dial note 'TANPA DATA', dial state 'none' (t6_detection.js, shots/home-unverified.png).
- **Saran perbaikan:** When detection.state is UNVERIFIED and raw_doa_deg is a number, show it as 'DOA RAW / UNVERIFIED' (warn colour, needle hidden) like the doa.xml path, and set the dial note to UNVERIFIED instead of TANPA DATA.
- **Verifikasi:** source.py:239-240 gives state 'UNVERIFIED', relative_doa_deg=None and raw_doa_deg=the value whenever SOURCE/ANGLE_UNVERIFIED applies. That is the default, since example.yaml:8-9 has authority_verified and angle_verified false. app.js:284/291 only shows a raw angle when diagnostic_doa (doa.xml) is available. Otherwise it shows '--', and updateDial(null,false) sets the note 'TANPA DATA' (app.js:187). Meanwhile agent.py:398-403 sends the raw angle to Ground with ok=0. README:31-33 says raw/UNVERIFIED angles stay numeric. I re-ran t6_detection.js: angle '--', note 'TANPA DATA', age 'UNVERIFIED / 0.1 dtk'.

#### EDGE-UI-1 · LOW · confirmed · bug
**Last journal operation pops a 'finished' toast on every panel load or reload**  
`web/app.js:893`

- **Masalah:** At startup, the /api/v2/session promise resolves before the first snapshot and calls render(snapshot) while snapshot is still {}. renderOperation(undefined) then sets seenOperation=null (app.js:259). When the first real snapshot arrives, last_operation (the newest journal row, possibly days old) has a key that differs from null, so showToast() fires as if it had just finished. Effects: after every kiosk start, Chromium restart or reboot the operator sees, for example, 'system.reboot.execute: APPLIED' in green or 'config.patch: FAILED' in red over the status bar for 4.5 s. The same empty render briefly shows warning dots on Link, Sistem and Config and forces the dark theme before the stored theme is applied.
- **Dampak:** Operators see stale completion or failure notifications that look current, for example right after a reboot, which can prompt wrong follow-up actions or retries.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced against the real demo: logged in by curl, POSTed a local operation.get (journaled APPLIED), then loaded http://127.0.0.1:28417/ three times; each load showed toast 'operation.get: APPLIED' (shots/toast-real.png). The mocked API gave the same result with snapshot delays of 0 and 150 ms (t1_toast.js). Scripts and shots are under /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-rdf-indicator-raspberry/67b9b04a-8e9b-512a-b5a0-e93d5cca1a8a/scratchpad/audit/edge-ui/.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Skip render() in the session callback until a snapshot has been received (or only update the admin chip and buttons there). Alternatively, initialise seenOperation only from a snapshot that has snapshot_seq.
- **Verifikasi:** app.js:893 calls render(snapshot) with snapshot={} when /api/v2/session resolves. poll() (app.js:369) waits for checkShutdownHistory() before its first render, so the session render almost always runs first. renderOperation(undefined) then sets seenOperation=null (app.js:258-259), and the first real snapshot's last_operation looks new and raises a toast. This defeats the guard at app.js:259, which was meant to suppress a toast on load. I re-ran t1_toast.js and both delays showed 'system.reboot.execute: APPLIED'. journal.recover() (journal.py:76-86) also refreshes updated_ms on recovered rows, so after an Agent restart the recovered op becomes last_operation and is toasted. Downgraded to …

#### EDGE-UI-4 · LOW · confirmed · data-correctness
**VFO frequency is displayed and pre-filled at 1 kHz resolution; tapping Terapkan without editing retunes 12.5/6.25 kHz channels**  
`web/app.js:817`

- **Masalah:** Home and focus show fmt(frequency_hz/1e6,3) (app.js:297), and the frequency keypad pre-fills (frequency_hz/1e6).toFixed(3) with Terapkan already enabled (app.js:825). A channel such as 145.8125 MHz displays as 145.813. If the operator opens Frekuensi and taps Terapkan to re-apply, the patch sends 145813000 Hz for both center and VFO0, a 500 Hz retune. The keypad itself accepts 6 decimals, so only the display and pre-fill are lossy.
- **Dampak:** Wrong frequency shown to operators, and a one-tap accidental retune off-channel on common narrowband channel rasters.
- **Bukti:** Mocked API with detection.frequency_hz=145812500: home shows '145.813'; keypad pre-fill '145.813' with Terapkan enabled; tapping Terapkan without editing POSTed changes {center_frequency_hz:145813000, vfo0_frequency_hz:145813000} (t14_freqprec.js).
- **Saran perbaikan:** Format MHz without losing Hz (for example trim trailing zeros from the 6-decimal value), pre-fill from config.safe_settings.vfo0_frequency_hz at full precision, and keep Terapkan disabled until the operator edits the value.
- **Verifikasi:** app.js:297 shows fmt(frequency_hz/1e6,3), and app.js:817 pre-fills toFixed(3). In refresh() (app.js:825) Terapkan is enabled for the untouched pre-fill. I re-ran t14_freqprec.js: 145812500 Hz displays as 145.813, and tapping Terapkan without editing posted 145813000 for both center and VFO0. The problem is real. Downgraded to low because the retune needs an explicit Terapkan tap, and a 500 Hz offset is small relative to typical VFO bandwidth (demo uses 12.5 kHz). The main effect is that 12.5/6.25 kHz raster channels display ambiguously.

#### EDGE-UI-10 · LOW · confirmed · ux
**Horizontal swipe does not change tabs over the Data topic list (most of the Data page)**  
`web/style.css:146`

- **Masalah:** .page has touch-action:pan-y, but .topic-list is its own scroll container with the default touch-action:auto. A horizontal drag that starts on the list is claimed by the browser and fires pointercancel, which clears the swipe state (app.js:427). That list is 152 of the Data page's 244 px, so swiping back from Data mostly does nothing.
- **Dampak:** Inconsistent page swiping on the touchscreen; operators must use the nav bar on the Data tab.
