The same swipe over the Data page title switched to 'config'. Swipes over the dial and the Kontrol button behaved correctly (no focus or modal opened).
- **Saran perbaikan:** Add touch-action:pan-y to .topic-list (and #modalbody if swipe is ever wanted there).
- **Verifikasi:** .topic-list (style.css:146) is a scroll container with default touch-action, while pointercancel clears the swipe state (app.js:427). I re-ran t9_swipe.js: a right swipe over #topiclist gave 'pointerdown:SUMMARY,pointercancel:SUMMARY' and stayed on 'data'. The same swipe over the page title switched to 'config'. The nav bar still works, so impact is minor.

#### EDGE-UI-11 · LOW · confirmed · ux
**Ground-issued PPP restart leaves the CMD chip pulsing 'in progress' forever with no notification**  
`web/app.js:241`

- **Masalah:** control.py:157 returns the terminal worker stage PPP_RESTART_REQUESTED. stageTone() does not list it in good, bad or warn, so the chip uses the 'progress' class with an endless pulse animation, and no toast fires (toasts are only for non-progress tones). The Edge journal never advances this stage, so the chip pulses until another operation or an Agent restart.
- **Dampak:** The Edge display implies a PPP restart is still running long after systemd accepted it.
- **Bukti:** Mocked last_operation {op:'ppp.restart', stage:'PPP_RESTART_REQUESTED'}: chip 'stagechip progress', toast null, unchanged 5 s later (t13_stage.js).
- **Saran perbaikan:** Add PPP_RESTART_REQUESTED to warnStages (accepted, outcome unverified), matching ground.js wording.
- **Verifikasi:** control.py:157 returns the worker's final stage PPP_RESTART_REQUESTED, which is not in goodStages, badStages or warnStages (app.js:241-244). It therefore gets the pulsing 'progress' class (style.css:100), and no toast is shown (app.js:262). The Edge journal does not advance it: it is not in TERMINAL, and only recover() on Agent restart changes it. I re-ran t13_stage.js: chip 'stagechip progress', toast null, unchanged 5 s later. Ground's ground.js:49,106 treats this stage as unresolved, not in-flight.

#### EDGE-UI-12 · LOW · confirmed · robustness
**Wall-clock (Date.now) timers misfire on an NTP clock step: immediate black screen and a stale flash**  
`web/app.js:411`

- **Masalah:** Idle blanking (Date.now()-lastTouch), the stale watchdog (Date.now()-lastProgress), the admin keepalive gate (app.js:386) and the trail age all use Date.now(). A Pi without an RTC typically steps its clock forward when NTP syncs over PPP after boot. The panel then instantly decides the user has been idle for hours and blanks, and shows the stale overlay for a tick.
- **Dampak:** The display unexpectedly goes black right after the link comes up, and the admin session keepalive stops refreshing.
- **Bukti:** Mocked page with Date.now offset (t12_clockjump.js): blank_after=900 s, operator touched 0.5 s earlier, then +6 h clock step. About 300 ms later {blank:true, stale:true}.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Use performance.now() for all elapsed-time logic (lastTouch, lastProgress, lastApi, admin refresh, trail ages). Keep Date.now() only for the displayed clock.
- **Verifikasi:** lastTouch, lastProgress, lastApi, the admin refresh gate and trail ages all use Date.now() (app.js:5,179,369,386,411). The project expects NTP sync after boot (clock_trusted gating, README:262), and a Pi without an RTC steps forward when timesyncd syncs. That instantly exceeds the blank threshold if auto-blank is on, and flashes the stale overlay until the next poll. The keepalive effect is negligible, because the next touch resets lastTouch on the new clock. Real but minor.

#### EDGE-UI-13 · LOW · confirmed · ux
**MQTT on-screen keyboard: edited text shrinks to 10 px, and the symbol layout pushes Spasi and backspace off-screen**  
`web/style.css:214`

- **Masalah:** When a field moves into #mqtt-keyboard-editor it loses the '.mqtt-settings-fields input{font-size:13px}' rule and inherits '.mqtt-settings-field{font-size:10px}', so the host or password being typed is rendered at 10 px on the 3.5" panel. In symbol mode ('?123') there are 5 rows of 44 px plus the bar and editor; the last row (~, Spasi, \, backspace) sits at y=311-355 in a 320 px viewport and needs a scroll inside the modal.
- **Dampak:** Hard to read and correct broker host and credentials on the kiosk, and correcting a symbol entry requires scrolling.
- **Bukti:** t11_kbd.js: input font-size 13px in the form vs 10px while edited. Symbol-mode backspace rect {top:311, bottom:355, viewport:320}. Screenshots shots/m-mqtt-kbd.png and shots/m-mqtt-kbd-sym.png.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Give '#mqtt-keyboard-editor input' an explicit font-size of 14-15px, and fold the symbol rows into 4 rows (for example move ~ and \ into existing rows) so the space and backspace row always fits.
- **Verifikasi:** style.css:15 sets font:inherit on inputs. The 13px rule (style.css:215) is scoped to .mqtt-settings-fields, and the editor rule (style.css:228) has no font-size, so a moved field inherits 10px from .mqtt-settings-field (style.css:214). In symbol mode there are 5x44 px rows (app.js:684-688) plus the 44 px bar and 44 px editor inside a dialog limited to 308 px. I re-ran t11_kbd.js: 13px in the form vs 10px while editing, and the backspace rect is at top 311 / bottom 355 in a 320 px viewport.

#### EDGE-UI-14 · LOW · confirmed · ux
**Header run badge and nav alert dots keep last-known (often green) values while the lost-API overlay is up**  
`web/style.css:172`

- **Masalah:** #stale is inset 28px 0 48px, leaving the header and nav visible. Nothing is re-rendered while the API fails, so the header badge keeps showing, for example, green 'RDF BERJALAN', and the tab alert dots keep their old state next to the 'SUMBER STATUS HILANG' card.
- **Dampak:** Mixed signals: a healthy-looking header over a 'status lost' warning.
- **Bukti:** Mocked API with snapshot aborted: shots/stale.png shows the green 'RDF BERJALAN' badge above the stale card (t3_stale.js).
- **Saran perbaikan:** When stale, set the run badge to a neutral 'STALE / --' state (and clear nav alert dots or mark them stale), or extend the overlay over the header.
- **Verifikasi:** #stale is inset 28px 0 48px (style.css:172), so the header #run badge and nav alert dots stay visible. Nothing re-renders while polls fail (app.js:370), so they keep their last state, for example a green 'RDF BERJALAN' next to the 'SUMBER STATUS HILANG' card. The overlay's own text says the data behind is not current, which mitigates this; it remains a minor mixed signal.

#### EDGE-UI-15 · LOW · confirmed · ux
**Profile dialog's 'AKTIF' badge does not follow a successful change**  
`web/app.js:746`

- **Masalah:** The profile list is built once from snapshot.config.profile when the dialog opens. After choosing GRAPH, the dialog shows 'ACCEPTED: id' and BALANCED keeps the AKTIF badge and highlight until the dialog is reopened, even after stream.set reaches APPLIED.
- **Dampak:** Operators may tap again or think the change failed.
- **Bukti:** Mocked flow (t4_modals.js): after tapping GRAPH, shots/m-profile-after.png still shows BALANCED as AKTIF with 'ACCEPTED: local-...'.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Re-render the option list from the latest snapshot on each poll while the dialog is open, or update it when last_operation for the submitted id becomes APPLIED.
- **Verifikasi:** The profile dialog (app.js:741-754) builds the list once from snapshot.config.profile, and the click handler only calls command(), which sets modalmsg to 'ACCEPTED: id'. Nothing re-renders the options, so the AKTIF badge and the .on highlight stay on the old profile until the dialog is reopened, even though stream.set applies almost immediately (control.py:161-163). This is a minor UX issue.

#### EDGE-UI-17 · LOW · confirmed · ux
**Untranslated or raw English strings in the Indonesian panel**  
`web/app.js:62`

- **Masalah:** Several user-visible strings are English or raw codes, against the project's Indonesian UI convention: Data-tab descriptions for cmd/config/patch, processing/set, service/restart, ppp/restart, reboot/* and shutdown/* (app.js:62-69); the PIN button 'Unlock' (app.js:484); Sistem rows printing raw 'true / true / true' and 'unknown / unknown' (app.js:313-314); DAQ 'UNKNOWN'/'DEGRADED' next to the translated 'SINKRON'; and alert-bar texts that are bare reason codes such as 'NO_FRESH_DOA' or 'SOURCE_UNVERIFIED' (agent.py:272 uses the code as text).
- **Dampak:** Operators who read only Indonesian get inconsistent or cryptic labels on key health rows.
- **Bukti:** Static review plus screenshots shots/system.png, shots/system-host.png and shots/home-stale-doa.png.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Translate the remaining descriptions and labels (for example 'Buka' for Unlock, 'ya/tidak/?' for booleans), and map common reason codes to short Indonesian texts in the alert bar.
- **Verifikasi:** The following are verified English or raw strings in an Indonesian UI, which CLAUDE.md requires: app.js:62-69 English descriptions, app.js:484 'Unlock', app.js:313 raw booleans from sync flags ('true / true / true'), and app.js:314 'unknown'. agent.py:272 also uses the reason code as alert text, so the home alert bar shows e.g. 'NO_FRESH_DOA' or 'SOURCE_UNVERIFIED'. Some of this is deliberate (stage codes are shown verbatim), but the alert-bar and boolean rows are user-facing. Minor.

#### EDGE-UI-18 · LOW · confirmed · doc-mismatch
**README describes the Data list as 'angular terkompresi'**  
`README.md:49`

- **Masalah:** README line 49 says the Data list covers outbound topics and 'angular terkompresi' (compressed angular). Current behaviour, per CLAUDE.md, PROTOCOL and the panel's own row text, is one uncompressed JSON PUBLISH with 360 values and no quantization.
- **Dampak:** Misleads integrators about the payload format.
- **Bukti:** Compared README.md:49 with app.js:56 ('Satu object per pesan, metadata dan tepat 360 nilai source') and the CLAUDE.md 'Current behavior' section.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Replace with 'angular JSON 360 nilai (tanpa kompresi/chunk)'.
- **Verifikasi:** README.md:48 (not 49) says 'angular terkompresi'. README.md:27 itself, CLAUDE.md 'Current behavior', PROTOCOL.md:81 and app.js:56 all describe one uncompressed JSON PUBLISH with 360 values. The doc is internally inconsistent.

#### EDGE-UI-19 · LOW · confirmed · bug
**A recurring error alarm does not wake a manually blanked screen if it was already active at blank time**  
`web/app.js:409`

- **Masalah:** enterBlank() records the error codes active at that moment in blankErrors and never updates them. If the operator blanks manually while, for example, HOT is active, HOT then clears and later returns, the wake check finds HOT already in blankErrors and the screen stays black. Only codes never seen at blank time wake it.
- **Dampak:** A returning overheat or under-voltage alarm can go unseen on a manually blanked display.
- **Bukti:** Mocked API (t2_blank.js case C): manual blank during HOT, HOT cleared for 1.5 s and re-raised, screen still blank. A new UNDERVOLTAGE code woke it.
- **Saran perbaikan:** While blank, remove codes from blankErrors when they disappear from the snapshot (blankErrors = intersection with current errors), so a re-raised alarm counts as new.
- **Verifikasi:** enterBlank() snapshots blankErrors once (app.js:409), and the wake check only fires for codes not in that set (app.js:411). The set is never pruned. Auto-blank only engages with zero errors, so this affects manual blank only. I re-ran t2_blank.js case C: manual blank during HOT, HOT cleared then re-raised, and the screen stays black. A new UNDERVOLTAGE code woke it. This is an edge case.

#### EDGE-UI-16 · LOW · refuted · doc-mismatch
**SHUTDOWN_SCHEDULED shown green with a 'good' toast although docs say it is not proof the host stopped**  
`web/app.js:241`

- **Masalah:** goodStages includes SHUTDOWN_SCHEDULED, so the CMD chip and toast are green. CONTROL.md step 6 and the panel's own shutdown modal text ('host belum terbukti mati') say this stage is not success; the code comment at app.js:251-252 says unproven outcomes stay amber.
- **Dampak:** Mixed messaging in a safety-relevant flow.
- **Bukti:** Mocked last_operation stage SHUTDOWN_SCHEDULED gave chip 'stagechip good' and toast class 'toast good' (t13_stage.js).
- **Saran perbaikan:** Move SHUTDOWN_SCHEDULED (and probably REBOOT_SCHEDULED) to warnStages.
- **Verifikasi:** The chip and toast show the stage verbatim ('SHUTDOWN_SCHEDULED', not 'APPLIED'). Green here means the request was scheduled, which is the successful terminal result of the execute command. The local shutdown flow immediately opens the explicit 'host belum terbukti mati' modal (app.js:356-357) over everything, and the host powers off 5 s later. No operator decision or state transition depends on this colour, so it is a colour-semantics nit, not a functional or doc-contract failure.

### Edge/Ground GUI (uji browser langsung)

_I tested the system live in Playwright Chromium. The Edge panel ran at 480x320 with touch, against the demo and against a non-demo Edge. I also ran a real loop: the non-demo Edge published through the loopback test broker to Ground, and I viewed the Ground preview at 1366x768. The core flows work: every tab and both Link/Sistem views, the dial and far-read mode, swipes, PIN login/logout, the three themes, accents, fonts, auto and manual black screen (fired at 60.2 s; wake taps are swallowed), the frequency keypad, MQTT save/persist with the on-screen keyboard, Ground->Edge stream.set (APPLIED end to end), and recovery from STALE after a server restart. There were no JS page errors in any phase, and nothing was outside the 480x320 screen or under 32 px. Every failed request came from servers I killed on purpose, or was an expected 4xx. The problems are in state presentation: an old operation pops up as a toast on every page load; the Profil dialog does not update after a change; the Data tab shows stale green TERKIRIM rows during a broker outage and hides MQTT error reasons and delivery codes; the symbol keyboard's last row is off-screen; the STALE warning is hidden behind dialogs and the black screen; and Ground ignores availability and health/detail although the docs describe them. All servers I started were stopped, and git status is clean._

#### GUI-2 · MEDIUM · confirmed · ux
**Profil dialog keeps the old profile marked AKTIF after a successful change**  
`web/app.js:751`

- **Masalah:** In Config > Profil, tapping an option calls command('stream.set') and only writes 'ACCEPTED: <id>' to modalmsg. The option list is built once from snapshot.config.profile when the dialog opens. It is never re-rendered, and the APPLIED result is never shown. After the Edge applies the change, the dialog still shows the previous profile with the AKTIF badge and highlight, and the message stays at ACCEPTED.
- **Dampak:** The operator is told the old profile is still active and may tap again or assume the change failed.
- **Bukti:** Live non-demo Edge (phase5.js): selected GRAPH, and /api/v2/config returned profile 'graph_u8' and Home showed 'stream.set APPLIED'. After 2 s the dialog still showed 'BALANCED AKTIF [on]' and 'ACCEPTED: local-…'. Screenshot: /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-rdf-indicator-raspberry/67b9b04a-8e9b-512a-b5a0-e93d5cca1a8a/scratchpad/audit/shots/gui-84-live-edge-profile-after.png
- **Saran perbaikan:** After a submit, poll /api/v2/operation/result (as the shutdown flow does), or re-render the option list from each new snapshot while the dialog is open. Show APPLIED/REJECTED and move the AKTIF badge.
- **Verifikasi:** Reproduced. After tapping GRAPH, snapshot.config.profile became 'graph_u8' and last_operation was stream.set APPLIED. After 2.5 s the dialog still showed 'BALANCED AKTIF [on]' and the message 'ACCEPTED: local-…'. The option list is built once from snapshot.config.profile (app.js:741-752). command() only writes `${stage}: ${id}`, and nothing re-renders the list or polls the result. The toast/CMD line (z-index 6) sits under the modal (z-index 10), so nothing visible corrects the AKTIF marker while the dialog is open.

#### GUI-4 · MEDIUM · confirmed · ux
**Panel never shows why MQTT is in ERROR**  
`web/app.js:235`

- **Masalah:** The snapshot carries link.mqtt_control.error and link.mqtt_bulk.error (for example CONNECTIONREFUSEDERROR or TIMEOUTERROR). No panel view renders them: Home shows 'MQTT ERROR', Link shows 'ERROR / ERROR' (app.js:309), Data shows 'ERROR / ERROR' (app.js:235), and far-read mode shows 'MQTT ERROR'. The operator cannot tell a refused connection, a timeout, an auth rejection or a TLS failure apart from the touchscreen.
- **Dampak:** After editing MQTT settings on the panel, the operator cannot diagnose a failing connection without shell access.
- **Bukti:** Live (phase4.js / phase6.js): /api/v2/snapshot returned mqtt_control.error='CONNECTIONREFUSEDERROR', while every panel text showed only 'ERROR'. Screenshots: /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-rdf-indicator-raspberry/67b9b04a-8e9b-512a-b5a0-e93d5cca1a8a/scratchpad/audit/shots/gui-88-live-edge-link-broker-down.png and gui-70-data-after-enable.png
- **Saran perbaikan:** Show the client error code, ideally translated, under the MQTT CTRL/BULK row on the Link and Data tabs, for example 'ERROR / ERROR (koneksi ditolak)'.
- **Verifikasi:** With the broker unreachable, the snapshot carried mqtt_control.error='CONNECTIONREFUSEDERROR' (mqtt.py:234 exposes it). grep shows app.js never reads mqtt_control.error or mqtt_bulk.error. renderData (app.js:229) and the Link row (app.js:309) render only `.state`. The panel MQTT settings dialog exists so operators can configure MQTT without a shell, yet refused, timeout, auth and TLS failures all look identical on the touchscreen. Medium is reasonable.

#### GUI-7 · MEDIUM · confirmed · ux
**STALE warning is hidden behind open dialogs and the black screen; the header keeps showing 'RDF BERJALAN'**  
`web/style.css:172`

- **Masalah:** #stale has z-index 5, #modal has 10 (style.css:179) and #blank has 20 (style.css:307). If the local API dies while any dialog (Kontrol, MQTT, Tampilan, PIN) is open, or while the black screen is up, the operator gets no sign of it. The black screen only auto-wakes for new error alerts taken from the last snapshot (app.js:411), and an API outage produces no new snapshot. Even with the overlay visible, the header badge stays green 'RDF BERJALAN' and the ADMIN chip stays shown, because the overlay only covers 28px..H-48px.
- **Dampak:** Total loss of status, the most severe panel state, can go unnoticed on an unattended kiosk sitting on the black screen or in a dialog.
- **Bukti:** phase3.js: with the Kontrol dialog open after a server kill, the stale overlay was hidden (elementFromPoint not in #stale). With the black screen entered and the server killed for 8 s, the blank stayed shown with stale=true underneath. Screenshots: /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-rdf-indicator-raspberry/67b9b04a-8e9b-512a-b5a0-e93d5cca1a8a/scratchpad/audit/shots/gui-54-stale-under-modal.png, gui-57-blank-during-outage.png, gui-51-stale-home.png
- **Saran perbaikan:** Treat staleness like a new error alert: wake the black screen and raise #stale above #modal, or show a stale banner inside the dialog. Neutralise the header run badge while stale.
- **Verifikasi:** The stacking is unambiguous: #stale has z-index 5 (style.css:172), #modal has z-index 10 with an 88%-opaque overlay (style.css:179), and #blank has z-index 20 and is opaque black (style.css:307), all siblings in main.shell. The watchdog (app.js:411) wakes the black screen only for new error alert codes taken from the last snapshot. An API outage produces no new snapshot, so a kiosk on the black screen keeps showing a normal-looking clock while a DAQ error would have woken it. The header badge sits outside the overlay inset (28px..H-48px) and keeps the last value. Medium for an edge-case failure of the stale indicator.

#### GUI-17 · MEDIUM · confirmed · doc-mismatch
**Ground ignores 'availability' and never exposes 'telemetry/health/detail', contrary to the docs**  
`src/rdf_node/ground.py:212`

- **Masalah:** Ground subscribes to availability (ground.py:38), but receive() has no branch for it, so the Edge online message and its LWT offline message are dropped. telemetry/health/detail is stored in self.detail (ground.py:212) but is not in snapshot() (ground.py:246-273) or any API, and ground.js has no view for it. docs/MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md:86 says Ground shows the availability status, and :82 says it uses health/detail for host/USB/sync/traffic diagnosis. When the Edge stopped, the Ground preview only switched to 'HEALTH STALE' after about 8-10 s. The tiles kept the last values (PROSES SDR RUNNING, DAQ HEALTHY, dimmed), and the big angle placeholder '--' stayed green.
- **Dampak:** Ground operators and integrators of the Ground local API cannot see node offline/LWT or host diagnostics (CPU, memory, under-voltage, USB, sync) that the Edge already sends.
- **Bukti:** Live loop (phase6.js): stopped the Edge. At 3 s Ground still showed 'HEALTH FRESH' and an angle. At 10 s it showed 'HEALTH STALE', and the snapshot had no availability or offline field. grep found 'availability' only in the subscription list and 'self.detail' only at assignment. Screenshot: /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-rdf-indicator-raspberry/67b9b04a-8e9b-512a-b5a0-e93d5cca1a8a/scratchpad/audit/shots/gui-92-ground-edge-stopped.png
- **Saran perbaikan:** Handle availability (store online/reason/time per sid) and health/detail in receive(), add both to snapshot(), and render an 'Edge online/offline' tile plus a host-detail tile group in ground.js.
- **Verifikasi:** Ground subscribes to 'availability' (ground.py:38), but receive() has no availability branch (ground.py:105-246): after the sid check it falls through the elif chain and is dropped. 'telemetry/health/detail' is stored in self.detail (ground.py:212) but is absent from snapshot() (ground.py:265-273), from every API route, and from ground.js. docs/MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md (Ground role table) says Ground shows availability as a hint and uses health/detail for host/USB/sync diagnosis. Offline detection still works through HEALTH STALE after about 8 s. However, Ground users cannot see LWT offline or Edge under-voltage, throttling, USB or sync detail that the Edge spends bandwidth sending. Medium is …

#### GUI-1 · LOW · confirmed · bug
**Every page load shows a toast for an old operation, as if it just happened**  
`web/app.js:893`

- **Masalah:** renderOperation() (app.js:253-263) is supposed to skip the toast on its first call by checking seenOperation===undefined. But the /api/v2/session promise at app.js:893 usually resolves before the first snapshot and calls render(snapshot) while snapshot is still {}. That sets seenOperation=null. The first real snapshot then has a different key (`id:stage`), so showToast() fires for the journal's latest operation however old it is. The toast covers the PPP/MQTT/DAQ/CONFIG REV status bar for 4.5 s. This happens on every kiosk start, Chromium restart or reload. A failed or REJECTED reboot/shutdown from hours ago would pop up red after each reload.
- **Dampak:** Operators see stale operation results presented as new events, and the main status bar is hidden for 4.5 s at every start.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced 8 out of 8 reloads on the live non-demo Edge (gui-live/p8.js). Each load showed the toast 'stream.set: APPLIED' for an operation 148-159 s old. Screenshot: /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-rdf-indicator-raspberry/67b9b04a-8e9b-512a-b5a0-e93d5cca1a8a/scratchpad/audit/shots/gui-89-live-edge-home-broker-down.png (the toast covers the status bar right after loading).
- **Saran perbaikan:** Do not call renderOperation from the pre-snapshot render. For example, skip the toast logic while snapshot.snapshot_seq is undefined, or treat the first render that has a real snapshot as the baseline (seenOperation stays undefined until a snapshot is present).
- **Verifikasi:** I reproduced this on a non-demo Edge on port 18802 after a journaled stream.set APPLIED: the toast 'stream.set: APPLIED' appeared about 30 ms after reload on 2 of 4 reloads. The cause is a race. The bootstrap at app.js:893 calls render(snapshot) while snapshot is still {}, so renderOperation (app.js:253-263) sets seenOperation=null. poll() needs two sequential fetches (snapshot, then pending-shutdowns) before its first render, so the session call often wins. The first real last_operation key then differs and fires showToast. The bug is real, but it is a one-time 4.5 s toast at page load, which on a kiosk is rare (boot or Chromium restart). Lowered to low.

#### GUI-3 · LOW · confirmed · ux
**Data tab keeps every topic as green 'TERKIRIM' while MQTT is down**  
`web/app.js:121`

- **Masalah:** Per-topic delivery cells show the last recorded publish result. When the Control client is not ready, the scheduler stops offering messages (agent.py:352-355), so no new PENDING/ERROR states are recorded and the old SENT entries stay. Recency is only in the cell's title/aria-label (app.js:132-134), which is not visible on a touchscreen. After the broker died, all published topics still showed green 'TERKIRIM / PUBACK broker' with nothing to show they are out of date.
- **Dampak:** The screen meant to show delivery reports successful delivery during an outage.
- **Bukti:** Live loop (phase6.js): killed the broker. The Data summary showed 'ERROR / ERROR', but rows showed availability/capabilities/state/telemetry/* -> 'TERKIRIM'. Screenshot: /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-rdf-indicator-raspberry/67b9b04a-8e9b-512a-b5a0-e93d5cca1a8a/scratchpad/audit/shots/gui-87-live-edge-data-broker-down.png
- **Saran perbaikan:** Grey out or relabel cells (e.g. 'TERAKHIR TERKIRIM x dtk lalu') when the owning client is not CONNECTED, or when updated_ms is older than the topic's expected period. Show the age as visible text.
- **Verifikasi:** The code path is correct. When ctrl is not ready, _schedule (agent.py:352-356) `continue`s without offering anything. Only messages still queued at connection loss get on_error (mqtt.py:262-266). mqtt_topic_delivery is cleared only on reconfigure (agent.py:183). So earlier SENT entries keep their green 'TERKIRIM', and age is shown only in title/aria-label (app.js:131-134). Lowered to low because the Data summary directly above the rows shows 'ERROR / ERROR' in red, and the Link tab badge turns red, so the outage is visible on the same screen. The rows lack visible staleness but are not the only signal.

#### GUI-5 · LOW · confirmed · ux
**On-screen keyboard symbol mode puts Backspace and Space off-screen**  
`web/app.js:688`

- **Masalah:** Symbol mode has 5 rows of 44 px keys (app.js:684-688). With the 44 px bar and 44 px editor field this needs 320 px in a 274 px body. The 5th row ('~', 'Spasi', '\', '⌫') sits at y=311-355, below the 320 px screen. Tapping where Backspace should be does nothing. The row only appears after the operator discovers that the keyboard body scrolls vertically. Letter and numeric modes fit.
- **Dampak:** Typing MQTT passwords or hosts with symbols becomes error-prone: the operator cannot delete a mistyped symbol or insert a space without finding a hidden scroll.
- **Bukti:** phase4b.js on the non-demo Edge: modalbody scrollHeight 320 vs clientHeight 274; row 5 rect top=311 bottom=355. Tapping the backspace position left 'uav-gui' unchanged. After a touch scroll of 46 px the row became visible. Screenshot: /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-rdf-indicator-raspberry/67b9b04a-8e9b-512a-b5a0-e93d5cca1a8a/scratchpad/audit/shots/gui-65-mqtt-keyboard-symbols.png
- **Saran perbaikan:** Fit symbol mode into 4 rows (for example, move ~ and \ into existing rows and put Space/⌫ in the 4th row), or shrink key height in symbol mode so all rows fit in the 274 px body.
- **Verifikasi:** Reproduced on a non-demo Edge. In symbol mode, modalbody is 320 px of content in a 274 px visible area (body rect 35-309), and row 5 ('~ Spasi \ ⌫') sits at y=311-355. elementFromPoint(400,316) returns the modal overlay, not the key. Letter mode fits (4 rows ending at 309). Lowered to low: the body can be scrolled to reach the row, Backspace and Space also exist in letter mode via the 'ABC' bar button, and only '~' and '\' are effectively hidden.

#### GUI-6 · LOW · confirmed · contract-mismatch
**Data tab badge turns red for telemetry/diagnostic/angular, but that topic has no row**  
`web/app.js:271`

- **Masalah:** tabAlerts() marks Data as 'bad' when any entry in link.mqtt_topic_delivery is a non-skipped ERROR. The Edge publishes telemetry/diagnostic/angular on Control (agent.py:457), and docs/MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md:51 documents it, but the static mqttTopics list (app.js:46-59) omits it. Its errors are therefore counted in the badge but appear nowhere. Errors such as OUTBOX_EXPIRED (3 s expiry for a ~3 KB frame on an 850 B/s Control budget) or MQTT_NOT_READY discards make the badge red while every visible row looks fine.
- **Dampak:** The operator sees a red problem indicator that cannot be explained on the tab it points to.
- **Bukti:** Live demo panel (phase3.js): injected a snapshot whose delivery map held only telemetry/diagnostic/angular {state:'ERROR', error:'OUTBOX_EXPIRED'}. Result: Data badge 'bad', aria 'Data, ada masalah', 0 error rows, topic not listed. The live Edge snapshot does contain 'telemetry/diagnostic/angular' entries. Screenshot: /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-rdf-indicator-raspberry/67b9b04a-8e9b-512a-b5a0-e93d5cca1a8a/scratchpad/audit/shots/gui-50-data-badge-no-row.png
- **Saran perbaikan:** Add telemetry/diagnostic/angular to mqttTopics, or render any unknown delivery keys dynamically. Alternatively, compute the badge only from topics that are listed.
- **Verifikasi:** The Edge publishes 'telemetry/diagnostic/angular' through _offer (agent.py:457-458), and docs/MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md:51 lists it. The static mqttTopics list (app.js:46-59) omits it, so updateTopicStatuses never shows it. tabAlerts (app.js:271) still counts every entry in mqtt_topic_delivery. A non-skipped ERROR on that topic (for example OUTBOX_EXPIRED, or a connection-reset failure of a queued frame) therefore turns the Data badge red with no row explaining it. Lowered to low: it needs a diagnostic-angular publish failure, which usually comes with other visible failures and tends to clear on the next diagnostic send.

The full code is only in title/aria-label, which a touch user cannot reach. Expanding the row shows only the static topic description, not the error.

Codes such as PUBLISH_REJECTED_0X87 (21 chars) or BULK_PAUSED_SOURCE_NOT_ELIGIBLE exceed about 105 px at 10px. Minor UX issue; low is correct.

#### GUI-9 · LOW · confirmed · ux
**Horizontal swipe does not work over the Data topic list (most of the Data page)**  
`web/style.css:146`

- **Masalah:** Pages set touch-action:pan-y, but .topic-list is its own scroll container (overflow-y:auto) with the default touch-action:auto. Chromium therefore claims horizontal gestures that start inside it and sends pointercancel instead of pointerup, so the swipe handler (app.js:426-437) never runs. The list covers about 152 of the 244 px Data page, so swiping right to go back to Config usually fails. Swipes work on every other page and on the Data summary strip.
- **Dampak:** Swipe navigation is inconsistent on the Data tab.
- **Bukti:** swipe_debug.js using CDP touch events: inside the topic list the events were 'pointerdown:touch:CODE | pointercancel:touch:CODE' and the tab stayed 'data'. On data-summary the events were pointerdown/pointerup and the tab became 'config'. Vertical touch scrolling of the list still works (scrollTop 0->105).
- **Saran perbaikan:** Add touch-action:pan-y to .topic-list (and to #modalbody if dialogs should also swipe).
- **Verifikasi:** Reproduced with CDP touch events. A horizontal swipe inside #topiclist logged ['pointerdown','pointercancel'] and the tab stayed 'data' in both directions. The same swipe on .data-summary logged pointerdown/pointerup and switched to 'config'. .page has touch-action:pan-y (style.css:39), but .topic-list is its own overflow-y:auto scroller with the default touch-action (style.css:146), so the browser takes over the gesture. Low.

#### GUI-10 · LOW · confirmed · ux
**UI keeps showing ADMIN after the server session is gone; Profil dialog then shows a raw auth code**  
`web/app.js:386`

- **Masalah:** keepAdminSessionAlive() only re-checks /api/v2/session when there was a touch in the last 30 s. After an Edge restart, or after the 600 s server idle expiry, the panel keeps the ADMIN chip, 'Logout' and 'Akses / helper = ADMIN' until someone touches the screen. In that state the Profil dialog's own handler (app.js:751) does not use the action() wrapper. It prints the raw 'AUTHENTICATION_AND_CSRF_REQUIRED' and never offers the PIN keypad, unlike Kontrol, Tampilan and MQTT, which re-prompt. Tapping 'Logout' with an expired session opens the PIN login dialog.
- **Dampak:** Access state is misreported, and one dialog strands the operator with an untranslated error.
- **Bukti:** phase3.js: after a restart, the server returned {authenticated:false} while the UI had authenticated=true and the chip shown, and it was still shown after 25 s idle. p9.js: Profil -> GRAPH after a server-side logout gave msg 'AUTHENTICATION_AND_CSRF_REQUIRED', title unchanged, uiAuth true. Screenshots: /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-rdf-indicator-raspberry/67b9b04a-8e9b-512a-b5a0-e93d5cca1a8a/scratchpad/audit/shots/gui-55-after-restart-config.png, gui-43-profil-session-expired.png
- **Saran perbaikan:** Re-check the session on each snapshot when the snapshot sid changes, and periodically even without activity (cheap GET). Route the Profil option handler through the same 403 handling as action().
- **Verifikasi:** Snapshot GETs do not refresh the session (api.py: only _admin_session extends it). keepAdminSessionAlive (app.js:375-384) re-checks only when lastTouch is within 30 s, so after a 600 s idle expiry or an Edge restart (sessions are in memory) the ADMIN chip stays until someone touches the screen. The Profil option handler (app.js:751) catches errors with a plain `$('modalmsg').textContent=e.message`. Unlike action(), it never resets auth or opens login(), so a raw 'AUTHENTICATION_AND_CSRF_REQUIRED' is shown. Mostly self-heals within 10 s after a touch, so low.

#### GUI-11 · LOW · confirmed · ux
**Edge frequency keypad accepts out-of-range values that the helper will reject**  
`web/app.js:825`

- **Masalah:** Terapkan is enabled for any value matching ^\d{1,4}(\.\d{1,6})?$ that is >0, so 0.5 MHz or 9999 MHz are accepted. The helper only allows 24-1766 MHz (helper.py:28,41), and the Ground form enforces min=24 max=1766. On the Edge, such a value is ACCEPTED, occupies the single command worker and pauses Bulk (CONTROL_IN_PROGRESS plus resume_stable_seconds) before failing in the helper. Five-digit input is also typed into the readout, but Terapkan is silently disabled with no hint.
- **Dampak:** An avoidable failed operation and a telemetry pause, with poor feedback.
- **Bukti:** phase2.js keypad run: '0.5' gave Terapkan enabled, and '12345' was shown with Terapkan disabled and no message. Screenshots: /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-rdf-indicator-raspberry/67b9b04a-8e9b-512a-b5a0-e93d5cca1a8a/scratchpad/audit/shots/gui-36-freq-5digits.png, gui-37-freq-entered.png. The helper range was confirmed by reading the code; an end-to-end write could not be run without the privileged helper.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Expose the helper's frequency range in capabilities or config, validate it in the keypad, and show a range hint ('24–1766 MHz') under the readout.
- **Verifikasi:** refresh() (app.js:825) enables Terapkan for anything matching ^\d{1,4}(\.\d{1,6})?$ and >0. The submit check in control.py:100-105/106-110 only applies finite(v,-200,5e9), so 0.5 MHz is ACCEPTED, queued, and then rejected by the helper's 24-1766 MHz range (helper.py:28,41). The worker's finally block (control.py) sets bulk_resume_after, causing a needless resume_stable pause. press() accepts up to 10 digits while the regex silently disables Terapkan for 5+ integer digits, with no hint. This needs the controlled mode with config_patch enabled; low impact.

#### GUI-12 · LOW · confirmed · ux
**MQTT dialog never shows whether CTRL/BULK credentials are set, and Simpan is below the fold**  
`web/app.js:566`

- **Masalah:** /api/v2/mqtt/settings returns control_credentials_set, bulk_credentials_set and trust_mode (agent.py:137-141), but the dialog only shows empty 'tidak diubah' fields. Enabling MQTT with only CTRL credentials saved 'Tersimpan' and left bulk_credentials_set=false, so the Bulk channel will use anonymous CONNECT. The panel gives no sign of this, although config-file validation (config.py:100-102) requires both credential files. The 11-field form is 385 px tall in a 244 px body, so the Simpan button (y=402) needs scrolling and is not visible when the dialog opens.
- **Dampak:** The operator cannot verify credential state from the panel, and a half-configured Bulk channel goes unnoticed until the broker rejects it.
- **Bukti:** phase4.js on the non-demo Edge: save with only CTRL user/pass returned 'Tersimpan; koneksi MQTT diperbarui…', and the API showed control_credentials_set true / bulk_credentials_set false. The reopened dialog showed both credential pairs blank. Screenshots: /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-rdf-indicator-raspberry/67b9b04a-8e9b-512a-b5a0-e93d5cca1a8a/scratchpad/audit/shots/gui-62-mqtt-dialog.png, gui-63-mqtt-dialog-scrolled.png
- **Saran perbaikan:** Show 'tersimpan / belum ada' badges per channel using *_credentials_set. Warn when enabling with a channel lacking credentials. Keep Simpan in a sticky footer.
- **Verifikasi:** Reproduced in the browser and via the API. The dialog body is 385 px of content in a 244 px visible area, with Simpan at y=402 below the body bottom (305). mqtt_settings_view returns control_credentials_set, bulk_credentials_set and trust_mode (agent.py:137-141), but app.js never reads them. POSTing enabled=true with only CTRL credentials returned bulk_credentials_set:false and was applied. Note that docs/MQTT_GROUND.md (around line 64) explicitly says a channel without credentials uses anonymous CONNECT, so the half-configured Bulk is documented behaviour, not a bug. The UX gap of no credential-state indication is real. Low.

#### GUI-13 · LOW · confirmed · ux
**Home banner says 'Status lokal normal' (green) while MQTT is ERROR and PPP is DOWN**  
`src/rdf_node/agent.py:265`

The only signs are the small 'MQTT ERROR' stat and the red Link badge.

- **Bukti:** Live (p7.js) with the broker killed: ppp 'DOWN', mqtt 'ERROR', alert 'Status lokal normal', class 'alert good'; focus alert ''. Screenshot: /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-rdf-indicator-raspberry/67b9b04a-8e9b-512a-b5a0-e93d5cca1a8a/scratchpad/audit/shots/gui-89-live-edge-home-broker-down.png

**Raw backend codes are shown to operators untranslated**  
`web/app.js:324`

Home alerts likewise show 'CLOCK_UNTRUSTED' as the alert text (agent.py:272 uses the reason code as text). In demo mode even a telemetry-profile change is rejected as 'no hardware control'.

The Home alert text was 'FileNotFoundError:DOA_UNAVAILABLE', because agent.py:272 uses the reason code as text. post() (app.js:324) turns raw error codes into messages. #### GUI-15 · LOW · confirmed · bug
**False 'API lokal tidak merespons' overlay flashes at load when the first snapshot takes >250 ms**  
`web/app.js:411`

- **Masalah:** lastProgress and lastApi start at 0 (app.js:5). The 250 ms watchdog (app.js:411) therefore shows the STALE overlay with 'API lokal tidak merespons.' whenever the first /api/v2/snapshot has not returned within 250 ms, even though the API is up. This is plausible on a loaded Pi at boot.
- **Dampak:** A spurious alarm flash at every slow start.
- **Bukti:** p10.js: delaying only the first snapshot by 400 ms made the stale overlay go visible at 266 ms and hide at 516 ms. Normal loads showed no flash.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Initialise lastProgress/lastApi to the page-load time, or suppress the overlay until 5 s after load or after the first failed poll.
- **Verifikasi:** lastProgress and lastApi start at 0 (app.js:5), and the 250 ms watchdog (app.js:411) computes Date.now()-0>5000, which is true. The stale overlay with 'API lokal tidak merespons.' therefore shows until the first snapshot lands. If the first /api/v2/snapshot takes more than about 250 ms, it flashes. Arguably it is not entirely false (no data has arrived yet), but the reason text is inaccurate while the API is up. Low.

#### GUI-16 · LOW · confirmed · ux
**'Coba lagi' (Retry) gives no visible feedback while the API is down**  
`web/app.js:368`

- **Masalah:** poll(true) sets 'Mencoba lagi...' and disables the button, but with the server down fetch fails immediately (ECONNREFUSED), and finally{} restores 'Coba lagi' within milliseconds. Background polling already retries every 500 ms. A tap appears to do nothing. The Ground preview's Retry behaves the same (ground.js:603/645).
- **Dampak:** Operators may think the button is broken.
- **Bukti:** phase3.js: 100 ms after tapping Retry with the server killed, the label was 'Coba lagi' and the button enabled. phase6.js (Ground): ['Coba lagi', false]. Screenshot: /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-rdf-indicator-raspberry/67b9b04a-8e9b-512a-b5a0-e93d5cca1a8a/scratchpad/audit/shots/gui-52-stale-retrying.png
- **Saran perbaikan:** Keep the 'Mencoba lagi...' state for a minimum time (for example 1 s) and show the result, such as 'Masih gagal: koneksi ditolak' with a timestamp.
- **Verifikasi:** poll(true) (app.js:362-372) sets 'Mencoba lagi...' and then resets it in finally. With the server down, fetch fails almost immediately (ECONNREFUSED), so the label flips back within milliseconds, and the background poll already retries every 500 ms. The button looks inert. Cosmetic, low.

#### GUI-18 · LOW · confirmed · ux
**Ground 'Profil telemetry' select does not reflect the node's current profile**  
`web/ground.html:28`

- **Masalah:** The select is static HTML that defaults to BALANCED and is never synced from snapshot.node_state.profile. Its value is whatever the user last picked. When the node was on graph_u8, the select still showed BALANCED.
- **Dampak:** The control suggests the wrong current profile, and pressing 'Set profil' with the default sends an unintended change.
- **Bukti:** phase5.js: the Ground health tile showed 'PROFIL: graph_u8' while #profile had value 'balanced'. Screenshot: /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-rdf-indicator-raspberry/67b9b04a-8e9b-512a-b5a0-e93d5cca1a8a/scratchpad/audit/shots/gui-90-ground-desktop.png
- **Saran perbaikan:** In poll(), set $('profile').value from node_state.profile unless the select is focused or was changed but not yet submitted.
- **Verifikasi:** The #profile select in ground.html is static HTML defaulting to BALANCED. ground.js only reads it on click (line 539) and shows node_state.profile only as a tile (line 589). Nothing syncs the select to the node's current profile. Sending still requires an explicit 'Set profil' press, so low.

#### GUI-19 · LOW · confirmed · contract-mismatch
**Ground connects to MQTT even when mqtt.enabled is false**  
`src/rdf_node/ground.py:34`

- **Masalah:** Ground always builds and starts an MQTT Client from cfg['mqtt'] (ground.py:34-41) without checking mqtt.enabled, which defaults to false in config/example.yaml. With the default config, Ground keeps reconnecting to 10.90.0.1:8883, shows 'MQTT CONNECTING/ERROR (TIMEOUTERROR)', and the key has no effect. The Edge honours the same key (agent.py:120).
- **Dampak:** The config key looks meaningful but is ignored, and an offline Ground preview reports a misleading MQTT ERROR.
- **Bukti:** Started Ground with a config that had no mqtt section (enabled false). The snapshot showed mqtt_control state CONNECTING, then ERROR 'TIMEOUTERROR' with reconnects increasing.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Skip creating or starting the client and report DISABLED when mqtt.enabled is false, or reject or document the key for the Ground role.
- **Verifikasi:** Ground.__init__ always builds the MQTT Client from cfg['mqtt'] (ground.py:34-39), and start() always starts it, with no check of mqtt.enabled. The Edge checks it in _new_mqtt_clients (agent.py:120). config/example.yaml defaults enabled:false, so the key has no effect on Ground. In practice provision-ground.sh:62 sets enabled=True, and a Ground without MQTT has no purpose, so the impact is a misleading MQTT ERROR on an unprovisioned or offline Ground. Low.

### Docs & deploy

_I compared the docs, deployment scripts, systemd/kiosk units and SHA256SUMS with the current code. SHA256SUMS matches every listed file. All shell scripts pass `bash -n`/`dash -n`, the units pass `systemd-analyze verify`, and check-package.sh is clean apart from the known root-only test failure. Topic names, QoS, retain, expiry and intervals in the docs match agent.py/ground.py, as do the CLI flags, API paths and ports, and the kiosk opens 127.0.0.1:8790 at 480x320 as documented. The real problems are in the lifecycle around the code. Upgrading from 1.0.0 crash-loops the Edge because of retired config keys. A Ground upgrade never restarts the receiver, rollback cannot work across the schema change, and the broker ACL and TLS certificate have no migration or renewal path. Several runtime interactions are also undocumented: retained capabilities go stale when the helper starts late, panel MQTT settings silently override import-bundle, the helper's UMask changes settings.json permissions, and the vcgencmd/undervoltage alert does not work under the rdf-edge account. Some doc claims about the bundled Ground are false: it never shows UNVERIFIED angular frames, and it does not recover lost results with operation.get, so Refresh config can stay blocked._

#### DEP-1 · HIGH · confirmed · bug
**Upgrading from 1.0.0 crash-loops rdf-edge (and later rdf-ground): retired config keys are rejected and the docs name only one of three**  
`src/rdf_node/config.py:24`

- **Masalah:** The 1.0.0 install.sh created config.yaml by dumping the full defaults (`c=load_config(); ... save_config(...)`), so every 1.0.0 /etc/rdf-node/config.yaml and /etc/rdf-ground/config.yaml contains `telemetry.require_ground_receipt_for_bulk`, `freshness.receipt_warn_ms` and `freshness.receipt_lost_ms`. The current strict `_merge` raises UNKNOWN_CONFIG_KEY for all three. install.sh keeps the existing config, then immediately runs `systemctl restart rdf-control-helper.service rdf-edge.service` (install.sh:88). rdf-edge fails at load_config and hits StartLimitBurst=10. The interactive `rdf-node setup` that follows (install.sh:91) also fails with a ValueError traceback. docs/OPERATIONS.md:242-244 tells the operator to remove only `telemetry.require_ground_receipt_for_bulk`, so after following the docs the Edge still fails on `freshness.receipt_warn_ms`. The Ground config has the same keys and fails at its next restart or reboot.
- **Dampak:** Following the documented upgrade path takes the Raspberry panel, telemetry and command channel down until someone hand-edits /etc/rdf-node/config.yaml. The Ground receiver fails the same way at its next reboot.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced. I took the 1.0.0 example.yaml (git 0aadd61), dumped it as 1.0.0 install.sh does, and loaded it with the current load_config: `telemetry.require_ground_receipt_for_bulk: UNKNOWN_CONFIG_KEY`. After deleting only that key, as OPERATIONS.md instructs: `freshness.receipt_warn_ms: UNKNOWN_CONFIG_KEY`. Script: scratchpad/audit (inline), files old-example.yaml and upgraded-edge-config.yaml. The 1.0.0 install.sh full dump is confirmed by `git show 0aadd61:rdf-node/install.sh` lines 47-55.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Add a migration step in install.sh (or load_config) that drops the known retired keys (`telemetry.require_ground_receipt_for_bulk`, `freshness.receipt_warn_ms`, `freshness.receipt_lost_ms`) and rewrites the file after making a backup. At minimum, validate the existing config with the new release before restarting services, abort with a clear message, and list all three keys in OPERATIONS.md.
- **Verifikasi:** Reproduced independently. I dumped the 1.0.0 defaults the way 1.0.0 install.sh does (git 0aadd61 install.sh:47-55, full save_config dump) and loaded the result with the current load_config. It fails three times in a row: telemetry.require_ground_receipt_for_bulk, then freshness.receipt_warn_ms, then freshness.receipt_lost_ms, all UNKNOWN_CONFIG_KEY (config.py:23-24). docs/OPERATIONS.md:61-63 names only the first key; the auditor's line numbers 242-244 are wrong, but the content is right. install.sh:47 keeps the existing config and install.sh:88 restarts rdf-edge, which is Type=exec, so it crash-loops into StartLimitBurst=10. `rdf-node setup` at install.sh:91 then raises in load_config and …

#### DEP-2 · HIGH · confirmed · bug
**`install.sh --ground` upgrade never restarts rdf-ground.service, so the old receiver keeps running**  
`install.sh:99`

- **Masalah:** The Ground branch only installs the unit file, runs daemon-reload and prints 'Next: sudo bash scripts/provision-ground.sh'. On an already-provisioned Ground, provision-ground.sh exits 1 at line 13 ('Provisioning already exists. Refusing...'). Nothing restarts rdf-ground.service, and OPERATIONS.md 'Upgrade' only says to run the same installer. The running Python process keeps the old modules. api.py's ROOT was resolved through the old `current` symlink, so it also keeps serving the old web assets. This release changed the Angular wire format (RDF2 chunks to JSON) and the docs say Edge and Ground must be upgraded together. After a 'successful' upgrade the old Ground still runs the old decoder, and the new code (with DEP-1's config failure) only starts at the next reboot. Both releases report `__version__ = '1.0.0'`, so capabilities.version cannot reveal the mismatch.
- **Dampak:** After an upgrade, Ground silently runs the previous protocol version. Angular frames from the upgraded Edge are rejected or misdecoded until someone reboots or restarts the service manually, which then exposes the config crash.
- **Bukti:** Code trace: install.sh:97-101 has no restart or try-restart for rdf-ground; scripts/provision-ground.sh:13 refuses to re-run; docs/OPERATIONS.md:238-248 says nothing about restarting Ground. src/rdf_node/__init__.py still has version 1.0.0 although the CHANGELOG lists breaking Unreleased changes.
- **Saran perbaikan:** In the Ground branch, validate the existing config with the new code, then `systemctl try-restart rdf-ground.service`. Change the 'Next:' hint so it is only printed when /etc/rdf-ground-mqtt/mosquitto.conf does not exist. Bump __version__ for wire-incompatible releases.
- **Verifikasi:** The Ground branch at install.sh:97-101 only installs the unit and runs daemon-reload; nothing restarts or try-restarts rdf-ground. provision-ground.sh:13 refuses to run when mosquitto.conf exists, and OPERATIONS.md 'Upgrade' only says to run the same installer. The running process keeps its imported modules. api.py:16 resolves ROOT with .resolve(), so it keeps serving web assets from the old release directory. The old ground.py (0aadd61) decodes Angular as RDF2 chunks through Assembler, so JSON frames from an upgraded Edge are rejected until a reboot or manual restart, and that restart then hits DEP-1. __version__ is still 1.0.0 in both releases. This affects every Ground upgrade and leaves …

#### DEP-3 · MEDIUM · confirmed · contract-mismatch
**Brokers provisioned by 1.0.0 keep an ACL without settings/reported and the diagnostic topics, and there is no migration path**  
`scripts/provision-ground.sh:49`

- **Masalah:** The 1.0.0 provisioner gave Node Control write access to `config/reported` (and `ground/receipt`), not `settings/reported`, `telemetry/diagnostic/doa` or `telemetry/diagnostic/angular`. It also gave Ground controller and viewer read access to `config/reported`, not `settings/reported`. The current provisioner writes the new ACL but refuses to run when /etc/rdf-ground-mqtt/mosquitto.conf exists. docs/MQTT_GROUND.md:40-45 tells existing-broker users to add only the two diagnostic write rules and never mentions `settings/reported`. On an upgraded system: (1) QoS 0 diagnostic DoA/angular publishes are silently dropped by Mosquitto, so Ground's diagnostic views stay empty. (2) A Ground 'Refresh config' makes the Edge publish `settings/reported` at QoS 1. Mosquitto 2.x answers an ACL-denied MQTT 5 publish with PUBACK 0x87, and mqtt.py treats any PUBACK reason >= 0x80 as fatal (`raise ValueError('PUBLISH_REJECTED')`). The whole Edge Control session is torn down and reconnected, and the settings report is lost. Ground's pending request then stays stuck (see GND-1). (3) If the broker also rejects the Ground's subscription to `settings/reported`, mqtt.py treats any SUBACK code >1 as SUBACK_REJECTED and loops on reconnect, so Ground would never become READY.
- **Dampak:** After upgrading an existing deployment, settings export is broken and each attempt resets the Edge Control connection. Diagnostic telemetry disappears, and Ground may become unable to connect at all.
- **Bukti:** `git diff 0aadd61 HEAD -- rdf-node/scripts/provision-ground.sh` shows the ACL change from config/reported+ground/receipt to settings/reported+diagnostic topics. provision-ground.sh:13 refuses a re-run. In src/rdf_node/mqtt.py, the PUBACK path raises PUBLISH_REJECTED for reason >= 0x80 and the SUBACK path raises SUBACK_REJECTED for codes >1. Not run against a real Mosquitto.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Ship an idempotent `provision-ground.sh --update-acl` (or have the installer rewrite /etc/rdf-ground-mqtt/acl from the current topic list and reload Mosquitto) that keeps credentials and certificates. Document the full ACL delta (settings/reported write for Node Control, read for controller/viewer, plus the diagnostic topics) in the upgrade section.
- **Verifikasi:** `git diff 0aadd61 HEAD -- scripts/provision-ground.sh` confirms the ACL delta: node-control gains settings/reported and diagnostic/doa|angular, and controller/viewer gain settings/reported in place of config/reported. The provisioner cannot be re-run (line 13). MQTT_GROUND.md:40-45 lists only the two diagnostic rules as the migration for existing brokers. The ACL-minimum section does list settings/reported, but nothing tells the operator to apply it on upgrade. mqtt.py:344-347 raises PUBLISH_REJECTED on any PUBACK reason >= 0x80, which tears down the session and clears the outbox (mqtt.py:262-266). Mosquitto 2.x returns 0x87 to v5 clients for ACL-denied QoS1 publishes, and …

#### CAP-1 · MEDIUM · confirmed · bug
**Retained `capabilities` are published only on Control connect, so a helper that comes up later leaves Ground controls disabled**  
`src/rdf_node/agent.py:356`

- **Masalah:** `capabilities` (helper_available, remote_reboot/shutdown/ppp_restart/processing/restart/config_patch) is computed from `helper_status` and published retained only when `ctrl.generation` changes. `helper_status` is refreshed only every 20 collector ticks (~5 s, agent.py:231). rdf-edge.service has no `After=`/`Wants=rdf-control-helper.service`. Both install.sh:88 and `controls approve` (cli.py:174) restart the helper and the Edge in parallel in one systemctl call. If the helper socket is not ready at the Edge's first poll and MQTT Control becomes READY within the next ~5 s, Ground receives retained capabilities with every remote_* set to false. They are never republished until the Edge's MQTT session reconnects. ground.js (mutationBlockReason/pppReadiness) disables all mutation and PPP buttons based on those flags.
- **Dampak:** Right after the documented `controls approve` step (or an upgrade or boot), the Ground UI can show 'Helper Edge belum tersedia' / 'Target ... belum disiapkan' and refuse every remote action, even though the Edge would accept them, until the Edge's MQTT connection happens to reconnect.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced with tests/broker_fixture.Broker: the Agent was started with no helper socket and the retained capabilities had helper_available/remote_reboot/remote_processing = (False, False, False). A fake helper then came up. `agent.capabilities()` returned (True, True, True, True), but the broker's retained capabilities stayed (False, False, False, False), with 1 capabilities publish in total. Script: scratchpad/audit/repro_caps.py.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Republish capabilities whenever their content changes (compare the dict without `t`, the way `state` uses last_sent_state), and refresh helper_status promptly on startup. Add `Wants=rdf-control-helper.service` and `After=rdf-control-helper.service` to rdf-edge.service.
- **Verifikasi:** I re-ran scratchpad/audit/repro_caps.py. The retained capabilities stay (False,False,False,False) while agent.capabilities() returns True for all four, and only one capabilities publish is made. In code, capabilities are offered only inside `if ctrl.generation!=self._generation` (agent.py:356-360). helper_status is refreshed every 20 ticks (agent.py:231-233). rdf-edge.service has only After=network.target. install.sh:88 and cli.py:174 restart the helper and the Edge in a single parallel systemctl call. The helper is single-threaded (UnixStreamServer, helper.py:375), but its ops use --no-block, so long blocking is unlikely. The main trigger is the startup race, and the result is stale Ground …

#### GND-1 · MEDIUM · confirmed · bug
**Ground 'Refresh config' stays blocked (SETTINGS_REQUEST_PENDING) after any lost request or response; the bundled Ground never uses operation.get**  
`src/rdf_node/ground.py:342`

- **Masalah:** `settings_request_id` is cleared only when a matching settings/reported arrives, when a non-APPLIED terminal ack/config arrives, or when the Edge sid changes. There is no timeout. config.get only needs the Ground's own broker connection to be READY, not a live Edge. If the operator presses Refresh while the Edge is offline (retained state is still present), or the command or report is lost (Edge reconnect, outbox cleared, 30 s expiry, ACL denial from DEP-3), every later Refresh is rejected. The sid only changes when the Edge process restarts, not on an MQTT reconnect. The docs say Ground re-queries lost results (MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md:98,101; PROTOCOL.md:235), but neither ground.py nor ground.js ever sends `operation.get`. Lost ACKs therefore also leave Ground journal entries at REQUESTED/ACCEPTED/VERIFYING forever.
- **Dampak:** The operator can no longer read the node's settings from Ground (or keep the safe-settings form current) until the Edge or Ground process is restarted. Stale operation stages mislead the operator about command outcomes.
- **Bukti:** Reproduced (scratchpad/audit/repro_settings_pending.py): with retained state and no Edge subscriber, the first submit returned REQUESTED. After the Edge re-published state with the same sid, the next two submits returned `REJECTED SETTINGS_REQUEST_PENDING`. grep finds no operation.get usage in web/ground.js or src/rdf_node/ground.py.
- **Saran perbaikan:** Expire the pending settings request after the command TTL plus the report expiry (about 45 s), or when Control reconnects. After a reconnect or timeout, query pending operation IDs with operation.get, or correct the docs.
- **Verifikasi:** I re-ran repro_settings_pending.py and got REQUESTED, then REJECTED SETTINGS_REQUEST_PENDING twice. settings_request_id is cleared only at ground.py:132 (sid change), :152 (matching report) and :242-243 (non-APPLIED terminal ack/config). There is no timeout. config.get skips the health check (ground.py:325), so pressing Refresh while the Edge is offline (retained state still present) is easy. A lost settings/reported after an APPLIED ack also leaves it stuck: the Edge clears its own id at offer time (agent.py:388-392), and the outbox can then be cleared or expire. The Ground journal has no expiry sweep. web/ground.js and ground.py never send operation.get, although MQTT_TOPIC_SUMMARY.md:98 …

#### UI-1 · MEDIUM · confirmed · doc-mismatch
**Ground preview never displays UNVERIFIED angular frames, so the plot is empty in the default commissioning state**  
`web/ground.js:628`

- **Masalah:** ground.js fetches only `/api/v2/angular/latest`, which returns only flags=63 LIVE frames. Frames with partial flags on telemetry/angular (the default while authority_verified/angle_verified are false) and telemetry/diagnostic/angular are stored by ground.py and exposed only at `/api/v2/angular/diagnostic/latest`, which no UI code calls. docs/MQTT_GROUND.md:123 (acceptance step 4) says 'Ground menampilkan keduanya sebagai UNVERIFIED' for both DoA and angular, but only the DoA is shown.
- **Dampak:** During commissioning (the default unverified state), an operator following the acceptance steps sees no curve on Ground and may conclude the Angular channel is broken, although the Edge is publishing 360-value frames.
- **Bukti:** Comparing API calls: grep of '/api/v2/...' in web/ground.js shows no angular/diagnostic/latest; api.py:146-148 serves it. plot(a) returns early when a is null (ground.js:556), so only the empty dial face is drawn.
- **Saran perbaikan:** When /angular/latest is null, poll /api/v2/angular/diagnostic/latest and draw it in a distinct UNVERIFIED style with its validation_reasons. Alternatively, correct MQTT_GROUND.md step 4 to point to the API.
- **Verifikasi:** ground.py:96-107 stores partial-flag telemetry/angular frames in diagnostic_angular, and angular_view (ground.py:275) shows only ALL_FLAGS frames. ground.js:628 fetches only /api/v2/angular/latest, and grep shows no call to /api/v2/angular/diagnostic/latest (api.py:146-148). The page has a DoA diagnostic panel but nothing for unverified Angular. authority_verified and angle_verified default to false (example.yaml:8-9), so in the default commissioning state the Ground plot is empty, which contradicts MQTT_GROUND.md:123 step 4 ('Ground menampilkan keduanya sebagai UNVERIFIED'). Step 6 points to the API for diagnostic Angular, so the docs only partly cover this.

Document the precedence and show 'override aktif dari panel' in doctor and on the Data page.

Once custom credentials have been saved, control_custom/bulk_custom stay true (agent.py:165-166), so the imported credentials are ignored as well. No doc explains this precedence.
